On 120 RoboLab tasks, policies fine-tuned on our learned clocks succeed more often and move with up to half the jerk compared with policies fine-tuned on the original demonstration timing and uniformly accelerated to the same speed. On a real robot, our policy stacks cups in 3.50s, 1.72$\times$ faster than the default policy's 6.02s while succeeding as often, in 29 of 30 trials. Videos and further material are available at https://mllab.snu.ac.kr/projects/learned_speed_prior.

</details>


### [248] [Agentic Hazard Mapper: Emulating Expert Map-Making with AI Agents for Risk Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32738)
*Sidi Wu,Lifeng Lin,Yizi Chen,Anna Yokokubo,Lorenz Hurni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多智能体系统 Agentic Hazard Mapper，通过模拟专业制图工作流与迭代设计权衡，自动生成适应不同数据与场景的灾害地图，用户实验显示其提升了空间理解与疏散规划表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害地图对公众风险沟通、防灾准备和疏散规划至关重要，但地方政府与社区往往缺乏制图专业知识，导致地图设计质量难以保证，因此需要自动化手段降低制图门槛。

Method: 采用多智能体系统架构，模拟专业制图工作流与迭代式设计权衡，并针对不同数据、地点和灾害情境自适应调整设计决策；先通过地图专家的形成性研究指导系统设计，随后开展用户研究和专家/实践者访谈进行评估。

Result: 生成的地图提升了参与者的空间理解与疏散规划任务表现，降低了感知工作负荷与任务完成时间，且总体优于对应的市政地图并更受偏好。

Conclusion: Agentic 制图方法能有效支持非专业主体的灾害地图生产；未来需关注人工监督、面向不同用户与沟通情境的适配，以及将智能体支持扩展到灾害地图生产与风险沟通的更广泛环节。

Abstract: Hazard maps are essential tools for public risk communication, preparedness, and evacuation planning, yet effective cartographic design is not always ensured due to limited access to cartographic expertise across local governments and communities. We present Agentic Hazard Mapper, a multi-agent system that automates hazard-map production by emulating professional cartographic workflows and iterative design trade-offs while adapting design decisions to different data, locations, and hazard contexts. We conducted a formative study with map experts to inform the system design, followed by a user study and interviews with map experts and hazard practitioners. The generated maps improved participants' spatial understanding and evacuation-planning performance, reduced perceived workload and task completion time, and were generally preferred over corresponding municipal maps. We further discuss human oversight, adaptation across users and communication contexts, and opportunities to extend agentic support across broader stages of hazard-map production and risk communication.

</details>


### [249] [ActionRAG: Evidence-Refining Graph Diffusion for Long-Term Memory in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32752)
*Navyansh Mahla,Sashank Vandrangi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ActionRAG，一种图记忆架构，将谨慎的索引与查询时的图再评估相结合，用图结构证据修正初始相似度以提升 LLM 智能体长期记忆的多跳检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体需要能随时间增长的交互历史中恢复精确且互相一致的证据，尤其当答案依赖事实间关系而非孤立语义匹配时。现有记忆流水线有两处脆弱环节：索引阶段实体重复被碎片化或不同实体被错误合并；在线检索阶段把初始相似度当作最终结果，即使局部图提供了可修正它的证据。

Method: 索引侧：协调类型标签并采用分层实体消解（tiered entity resolution）。查询侧：双通道实体与三元组检索形成初始证据集并诱导有界局部图；查询条件化的接收方归一化扩散（receiver-normalized diffusion）利用局部路径结构精炼三元组分数；辅以种子保护、枢纽惩罚通量（hub-penalized flux）与三元组实体化，保留直接证据同时恢复遗漏连接。

Result: 在 MemoryAgentBench（MAB）上评测，覆盖准确检索、冲突消解、长程理解与测试时学习。在 8 个确定性评分列上平均准确率 55.1，比最强检索式记忆基线高 5.4 分。按 MAB 查询时成本核算，4 个报告成本列上平均合成成本低于每查询 \$0.001；在消费级 Apple Silicon 上，分别测得的检索侧 p99 组件之和为 440.7 ms。

Conclusion: 将图结构信号引入查询时评分修正，并配合稳健的实体消解索引，可在保持低合成成本（<$0.001/查询）与可接受检索延迟（p99 合计 440.7 ms）的同时显著提升多跳记忆检索准确率，说明"索引谨慎 + 查询时图再评估"是长程记忆的有效范式。

Abstract: LLM agents need long-term memory that can recover precise and mutually consistent evidence from growing interaction histories, particularly when answers depend on relations among facts rather than isolated semantic matches. Current memory pipelines remain vulnerable at two stages: indexing errors can fragment repeated entities or merge distinct ones, and online retrieval often treats initial similarity scores as final even when the surrounding graph provides evidence for revising them. We introduce ActionRAG, a graph memory architecture that couples cautious indexing with query-time graph retroevaluation. Its indexing pipeline reconciles type labels and applies tiered entity resolution. Given a query, dual-channel entity and triple retrieval forms an initial evidence set and induces a bounded local graph. Query-conditioned receiver-normalized diffusion refines triple scores using local path structure. Seed protection, hub-penalized flux, and triple materialization preserve direct evidence while recovering missed connections. We evaluate ActionRAG on MemoryAgentBench (MAB), which tests accurate retrieval, conflict resolution, long-range understanding, and test-time learning. Across its eight deterministic-scoring columns, ActionRAG achieves 55.1 average accuracy, improving over the strongest retrieval-based memory baseline by 5.4 points. Under MAB's query-time cost accounting, mean synthesis cost stays below \$0.001 per query on the four reported cost columns, and the sum of separately measured retrieval-side p99 components is 440.7 ms on our consumer Apple Silicon setup.

</details>


### [250] [HarnessMM: Recursive Self-Improvement for Harness Token Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32813)
*Yuhua Jiang,Chang Xu,Feifei Gao,Li Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HarnessMM 通过在模型与工具边界处复用已确定的决策、替换重复的推理回调，结合递归自我改进（RSI）选择配置，从而在不改变 actor 权重的前提下大幅降低 agent harness 的 token 开销，同时保持或提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agent harness 在工具反馈后会反复调用推理，即使早先的调用已经确定了下一步动作，造成大量冗余 token 消耗。论文希望在不修改模型主体权重的情况下提升 harness 的 token 效率。

Method: 提出 HarnessMM：(1) RSI 递归地根据训练反馈精炼配套 harness 配置，并用验证集挑选部署配置；(2) 在模型-工具边界设置控制器，复用已解决的决策以替代后续推理回调，actor 权重固定；(3) 用可执行决策图（EDG）存储精确动作、字面参数、因果依赖与公共守卫；(4) Guarded Decision Reuse 依据观察到的 receipts 激活持久决策，Certified Callback Substitution 通过原生接口执行并记录慢回调的移除；(5) 遇到新语义决策、证据不足或选择器弃权时回退到原始 actor。该表示限定了反馈可改动的范围，并用完整任务度量质量与成本。

Result: HumanEval 上 Codex 成功率保持 90.82%，Tokens/task 从 66.72k 降至 47.02k（节省 29.54%）；DeepSeek Harness 成功率从 71.43% 提升至 85.71%，Tokens/task 从 96.28k 降至 11.62k（节省 87.93%）。MBPP 消融显示相比单次使用图成本低 26.91%，相比保留回调低 19.52%。

Conclusion: 带守卫的决策复用是降低现有 harness 中重复推理的实用方法。

Abstract: Agent harnesses repeatedly invoke reasoning after tool feedback, even when an earlier call has already fixed the next action. We introduce HarnessMM (Harness Minus Minus), a method for improving harness token efficiency through guarded decision reuse and recursive self-improvement (RSI). RSI iteratively refines companion harness configurations from training feedback, with validation selecting the configuration for deployment. A controller at the model-tool boundary reuses resolved decisions to replace later reasoning callbacks while actor weights remain fixed. Executable Decision Graphs store exact actions, literal arguments, causal dependencies, and public guards. Guarded Decision Reuse activates these persistent decisions from observed receipts; Certified Callback Substitution executes them through the native interface and records removal of the registered slow callback. New semantic decisions, unsupported evidence, and selector abstention invoke the original actor again. The representation restricts what feedback may change, while complete-task measurements establish quality and cost. On HumanEval, HarnessMM preserves Codex success at 90.82\% while reducing Tokens/task (k) from 66.72 to 47.02 (29.54\% saving). For DeepSeek Harness, success rises from 71.43\% to 85.71\% while Tokens/task (k) fall from 96.28 to 11.62 (87.93\% saving). A separate MBPP ablation measures 26.91\% lower cost than single-use graphs and 19.52\% lower cost than retained callbacks. These results establish guarded decision reuse as a practical way to reduce repeated reasoning in existing harnesses.

</details>


### [251] [Reimagining Enterprise RAG for Multi-Agent Systems: A Knowledge Layer That Organizes Before It Retrieves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32851)
*Garima Agrawal,Prasun Das,Akshay Sarvade,Priyanka L,Minisha N,Sravani Joshna,Hemath Manivannan,Lavanya Korada,Sambhrit Pattanayak,Srashti Gupta,Siddu R,Prasad Kalyanasundaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 面向企业软件开发生命周期的智能体需要系统的行为逻辑而非文档。本文用七智能体逆向工程管线处理 241 个仓库、18 个服务，产出含范围、旅程、规则与验收标准的海量工件。但回收不等于可用：跨服务的 offer、eligibility、activation 等实体同名而语义相异，单个端到端旅程涉及上千条近似规则。作者将本体置于"工件层面"，提出自描述目录 + 稀疏元数据注册表 + 基于工件的导航超图构成的知识层，先组织后检索，使查询落在受约束的工件标识上，错误服务的相似规则直接缺席而非被统计排序掩盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业需要可被智能体执行的行为逻辑，而传统利益相关者文档无法提供。逆向工程虽能大规模回收逻辑，却带来歧义问题：共享词汇下的同名实体在各服务中操作语义不同，扁平向量检索会把错误服务的近乎相同规则以高相似度返回且无错误信号。内容级本体不存在，而把每个重复实体出现都关联起来成本过高且难以跨发布周期维护。

Method: 七个智能体组成的逆向工程管线，处理 18 个服务的 241 个仓库，生成描述范围、用户旅程、规则和验收标准的大量工件。核心方法是在工件层而非内容层建立本体：每个工件保留生产者、组件、服务、领域和类型等出处信息，在阅读内容前先行消歧。知识层由三部分组成——自描述目录、稀疏元数据注册表、定义在工件（而非抽取实体）之上的导航超图。查询先解析为工件标识，再用这些标识约束向量搜索，从而在结构上排除其他服务的竞争规则。

Result: 该知识层在一周内建成，可通过约定接入新服务，服务对象包括 SDLC 智能体、开发者、质量工程师和分析师。实际应用中支撑了 100 多项生产缺陷的根因分析与修复（经开发者验证），并将 35 次发布回归失败归因到具体规则和服务。

Conclusion: 在机器生成的语料中，消歧应靠结构与出处而非统计相似度：把本体建在工件层、先组织后检索，可以在成本可控（不足一周）且可持续（按约定扩展）的前提下，让庞大的近似规则集合变得可安全检索，并直接产生缺陷定位与回归归因的工程价值。

Abstract: Enterprises are deploying agentic AI across the software development lifecycle, and they need the behavioral logic of the systems they act on, not stakeholder documentation. A seven-agent reverse-engineering pipeline recovered this logic, processing 241 repositories across 18 services into thousands of artifacts describing scope, journeys, rules and acceptance criteria. Recovery does not confer usability. Entities like offer, eligibility, and activation recur across services under a shared vocabulary, related in name but operationally distinct, so one end-to-end journey implicates over a thousand near-identical rules that must not be conflated. Flat vector retrieval fails, returning an identical rule from the wrong service at high similarity with no signal of error. Content-level ontology, the traditional remedy, did not exist, and relating every occurrence of recurring entities would have cost more than the engagement and not survived a release cycle. We place the ontology at the artifact level. In this machine-generated corpus, each artifact retains its producer, component, service, domain and type, provenance that disambiguates before content is read. We present a knowledge layer that organizes before retrieving: a self-describing catalog, a sparse metadata registry, and a navigation hypergraph defined over artifacts rather than extracted entities. Queries resolve to artifact identifiers that constrain vector search, so the competing rule from another service is absent rather than outranked, making disambiguation structural, not statistical. Built in under a week and admitting new services by convention, the layer serves SDLC agents, developers, quality engineers and analysts. It supported root-cause analysis and resolution of 100+ production defects with developer verification and attributed 35 release regression failures to specific rules and services.

</details>


### [252] [A Foundation Model for Policy In-Context Longitudinal Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32863)
*Wenxin Chen,Kyra Gan,Mingyu Lu,Zilin Jing,Fei Wang,Hoifung Poon,Tristan Naumann,Peniel Argaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PICLE-PFN 是首个支持动态治疗策略的纵向因果效应估计基础模型，用上下文演示表示策略，单次前向即可预测条件平均潜在结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向因果效应估计受时变混杂和治疗-混杂反馈回路困扰；现有方法需按数据集拟合干扰模型，且多针对预设治疗序列或预测时域，部署时计算耗时。

Method: 将目标策略表示为上下文中的历史-决策演示，提供统一接口；在纵向结构因果模型采样的合成数据上预训练，摊销跨数据集、策略与时域的因果效应估计；对新观测数据集与目标策略直接一次前向预测 CAPO，无需任务特定训练或调参。

Result: 在合成与半合成纵向基准上，相比各数据集单独训练调优的 SOTA 传统估计器，取得有竞争力的 RMSE，不确定性校准显著改善，部署运行时间仅为极小部分。

Conclusion: 基于先验数据拟合网络的基础模型可统一、高效地处理动态策略下的纵向因果效应估计，兼具精度、校准与部署效率优势。

Abstract: Longitudinal causal effect estimation is complicated by time-varying confounding and treatment-confounder feedback loops. Existing methods generally require data-specific nuisance-model fitting, and many are tailored to prespecified treatment sequences or prediction horizons, leading to time-consuming computation at deployment. We introduce *Policy In-Context Longitudinal causal Effect estimation with Prior-data Fitted Networks* (**PICLE-PFN**), the first foundation model for longitudinal causal effect estimation that supports *dynamic* treatment policies. PICLE-PFN represents target policies through in-context history-decision demonstrations, providing a unified interface for a broad class of treatment policies. Pretrained over synthetic data sampled from longitudinal structural causal models, PICLE-PFN amortizes causal effect estimation across datasets, policies, and horizons. Given a new observational dataset and target policy, it directly predicts conditional average potential outcomes (CAPOs) in one forward pass without task-specific training or hyperparameter tuning. Across synthetic and semi-synthetic longitudinal benchmarks, PICLE-PFN achieves competitive RMSE and substantially improved uncertainty calibration with a fraction of deployment runtime, compared to state-of-the-art conventional estimators trained and tuned separately for each dataset.

</details>


### [253] [From Deliberately Constructed Systemic Representations of the World of Activity to Dialogue: A Five-Level Communication Architecture for Strong Language-Based ASI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32869)
*Maxim Osovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出用于"思维与活动"中消息（话语）生成与理解的五层组织框架，论证语言模型的上下文无法呈现并验证话语形成的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的上下文与训练权重本身无法证明存在刻意建构的系统性表征，话语形成的基础（习得图式、活动世界的系统表征、被接受为真的联结）在上下文之外预先确立，因此需要一套可检验、可调整的结构来审视这些基础。

Method: 将消息形成与理解划分为五个层级：活动世界的系统表征、从其中切出的具体情境、情境的叙事描述、消息的完整意义、文本；区分"情境的图式模型"与"关于它的故事"。正向由完整意义经缩写文本由共建表征重构，反向由完整意义经扩展描述与情境图式模型回溯系统基础。理论依托Shchedrovitskiy的系统方法论、Korzybski的结构微分与Mel'chuk的意义-文本理论。

Result: 给出一种具体的系统-活动内容组织方式，可用于审视和调整消息基础，并控制其转换带来的后果；同时阐明了上下文型文本生成与系统基础重构之间的区别。

Conclusion: 语言模型不能仅凭上下文或权重奠定话语基础；未来研究指向建立在可重构系统基础上的"活动大型图模型"，以区别于从上下文直接生成文本。

Abstract: This paper proposes a five-level organization of message formation and understanding within thinking and activity in which ASI participates. Its central thesis is that a language model's context is fundamentally incapable of presenting the foundations of utterance formation for examination and verification. These foundations are established in advance by acquired schemas, systemic representations of the world of activity, and connections accepted as true within them. Neither context nor trained LLM weights, in themselves, attest to the presence of deliberately constructed systemic representations. The five levels distinguish systemic representations of the world of activity, a particular situation cut out from those representations, its narrative description, the full sense of a message, and text. A situation's schema-model is not identical to a story about it. A recipient reconstructs the full sense from an abbreviated text through jointly constructed and agreed-upon representations and joint activity. Reverse work proceeds from the full sense through an extended description and the situation's schema-model to the reconstruction of systemic foundations. The theoretical foundations draw on the systems methodology of G. P. Shchedrovitskiy, the structural differential of A. Korzybski, and the Meaning-Text theory of I. A. Mel'chuk. The contribution is a specific systemic-activity organization of content for examining and adjusting message foundations and controlling the consequences of their transformation. Further research concerns large graphical models of activity on reconstructible systemic foundations, distinct from text generation from context.

</details>


### [254] [Do "True" and "False" Add Up to One? Complement Consistency in LLM-Agent Opinion Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32874)
*Anouar Imel,Jin Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文测试LLM智能体在观点模拟中报告的概率是否具备"互补一致性"（把命题改写为其否定、把p换成1-p后，两个报告应相加为1），发现Qwen3-4B、Llama-3.2-3B、Gemma-3-4B三个小模型系统性违反该性质，且这种不一致在多轮反馈中导致互补初始种群发散，故模拟概率不宜直接等同于信念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-agent观点模拟把智能体报告的数值概率当作其"信念状态"并在网络中传播。若该数值真正表示智能体的立场，那么命题与其否定、p与1-p的成对问答应严格互补（和等于1）。论文旨在检验这一隐含前提是否成立。

Method: 在三个小开源模型（Qwen3-4B、Llama-3.2-3B、Gemma-3-4B）上做配对实验：同一命题及其补集表述分别询问。数据来源包括运行中网络模拟的2304个智能体状态、AVeriTeC的48条真实事实核查命题，以及24个只有两种结果的受控世界基准。并检验同义改写对照、重复采样、同伴规模与报告强度稳健性，以及互补初始群体多轮反馈（5轮）后的镜像偏离，另做"先排程后定向"实验作对照。

Result: Qwen配对报告平均和为1.18，真实事实核查命题上同样超过1；受控基准中三个模型全部失败，偏差是同义改写的2–10倍，且在重复采样、不同群体规模与报告强度下持续存在。互补初始的两群体在5轮后对每个模型都出现正向发散。"将答案与其配对答案的补集取平均"可按构造恢复一致性并降低两种措辞的平均Brier损失，但最后一轮仅对一个模型优于两种原始措辞中较好的那一个；先排程后定向实验因固定措辞而只出现微小稳定交互。

Conclusion: 模型报告的概率缺乏重述不变性（互补一致性），因此不能直接解读为信念；在把模拟概率当作信念使用之前，必须先单独检验改写一致性。同义改写等常规检验会低估这一问题。

Abstract: In LLM-agent opinion simulations, each agent reports the probability that a claim is true after seeing its own previous probability and the probabilities reported by its neighbors, and that number becomes its state in the next round. If the number describes the agent's position rather than the sentence it was shown, then rewriting the claim as its complement and replacing every probability p with 1 - p should return the complementary answer, so the two reports should sum to one. We test this with three small open models: Qwen3-4B, Llama-3.2-3B and Gemma-3-4B. On 2,304 agent states taken from a running network simulation, Qwen's paired reports sum to 1.18 on average, and on 48 real fact-checking claims from AVeriTeC they again exceed one. A controlled benchmark of 24 worlds, each with exactly two possible outcomes, shows the same failure in all three models. The discrepancy is two to ten times larger than the change produced by a same-meaning paraphrase, and it persists under repeated sampling and across peer group sizes and report strengths. When the reports are fed back into two populations that start from complementary states, the populations do not remain mirror images: their divergence is positive after five rounds for every model. Averaging each answer with the complement of its paired answer restores consistency by construction and lowers Brier loss averaged over the two wordings, but at the final round it beats the better of the two original wordings for only one of the three models. A schedule-by-targeting experiment on the same model gives a small, stable interaction because such sweeps keep the claim wording fixed. Consistency under rewording therefore needs its own test before simulated probabilities are read as beliefs

</details>


### [255] [Advancing SVD-based LLM Compression via Layer-Wise Error Model Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32878)
*Moritz Thoma,Maximilian Groezinger,Maximilian Forstenhäusler,Emad Aghajanzadeh,Manoj Rohit Vemparala,Christos Anagnostopoulos,Pierpaolo Mori,Nael Fasfous,Alexander Frickenstein,Daniel Mueller-Gritschneder,Ulf Schlichtmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LEMS（层级误差建模搜索）与KFAC-SVD，改进LLM低秩SVD压缩的秩分配与分解质量，在Mistral/Qwen3/Llama 3上零样本精度提升最多4.8个百分点，困惑度平均改善15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩SVD压缩LLM存在两大局限：(i) 全局秩分配中未校准的误差代理无法刻画复杂的误差传播；(ii) 基于Fisher的估计器导致严重秩坍缩，分解质量差。

Method: LEMS：引入层级误差替代指标，融合局部与全局层重要性及传播偏置，通过ILP求解实现全局秩分配。KFAC-SVD：利用逐token统计量改进分解质量，缓解Fisher-based SVD的秩亏问题。

Result: 在Mistral、Qwen3、Llama 3系列上，LEMS稳定优于现有搜索策略，零样本精度最高提升4.8个百分点，并可泛化至70B规模模型；KFAC-SVD平均困惑度改善15%。

Conclusion: 结合误差传播建模的秩分配与基于token统计的SVD分解，可有效提升LLM低秩压缩的效果，且具备良好的大模型扩展性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Low-rank SVD-based compression offers a powerful strategy to reduce the computational costs of LLMs. However, existing methods face two key limitations: (i) global rank allocation, where uncalibrated error proxies fail to capture complex error propagation, and (ii) decomposition quality, where Fisher-based estimators suffer from severe rank collapse. In this work, we address these limitations by introducing Layer-wise Error Modeling Search (LEMS) and KFAC-SVD. LEMS advances rank allocation by introducing a layer-wise error surrogate that integrates local and global layer importance alongside a propagation bias, enabling effective global rank allocation via an ILP formulation. KFAC-SVD improves decomposition quality by utilizing token-wise statistics, mitigating the rank deficiency observed in prior Fisher-based SVD approaches. Across Mistral, Qwen3, and Llama 3 model families, we show that LEMS consistently outperforms existing search strategies, delivering significant zero-shot accuracy gains of up to 4.8 p.p. that generalize to model sizes of 70B parameters, while KFAC-SVD achieves an average perplexity improvement of 15%. Project page and code: https://lems-svd.github.io

</details>


### [256] [NgramRL: Accelerating RL Post-Training for Structured Generation via Rollout-Aware N-gram Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32881)
*Nilesh Prasad Pandey,Jangseon Park,Jason Kong,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NgramRL：用近期 rollout 历史构建 per-prompt n-gram 草稿器加速 RL 训练的 rollout 生成，配合自适应 fan-out 预取器，热身缓存下每次目标模型前向提交 3.60–7.77 个 token，吞吐最高提升 1.38x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 后训练中自回归 rollout 生成占总训练时间 70% 以上，是主要成本。现有投机解码不适配：基于模型的草稿器占用稀缺 GPU 显存且随策略演化而失配；无模型方法未显式利用跨训练轮次的重复性。

Method: 观察到同一 prompt 的近期 rollout 是高质量、自更新的草稿来源。据此提出 NgramRL：从有界近期 rollout 历史为每个 prompt 构建 n-gram 草稿器，无需辅助模型、草稿器训练或常驻 GPU 状态，并由目标模型验证保证分布不变。另引入自适应 fan-out 预取器，预测可能验证结果 (j, b)，用因果预算分配器集中预取高概率结果，未覆盖结果回退顺序查找且不改变已提交 token。

Result: 在两个模型家族、三个推理与结构化生成任务上，热身缓存下每次前向提交 3.60–7.77 token，比 SuffixDecoding 多至 1.99x、比 prompt-lookup 解码多 7.0x。Spider 上，预算 16 时预取器覆盖 Qwen3-8B 96.6% 的结果，实测 rollout 吞吐较同步 NgramRL 提升 1.38x，接近 1.40x 的重叠上限。

Conclusion: 利用训练中 rollout 的自重复性可构建零额外显存代价的高效草稿器，配合 CPU 预取隐藏开销，显著加速 RL rollout，且不损害分布正确性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but its cost is dominated by autoregressive rollout generation, which can exceed 70% of training time. Existing speculative-decoding methods are poorly suited to this setting: model-based drafters consume scarce GPU memory and may become misaligned as the policy evolves, while existing model-free methods do not explicitly exploit repetition across training epochs. We observe that recent rollouts of the same prompt provide a high-quality, self-updating draft source. Based on this insight, we propose NgramRL, a per-prompt n-gram drafter constructed from bounded recent rollout history. NgramRL requires no auxiliary model, drafter training, or persistent GPU-resident drafter state, while target-model verification preserves the rollout distribution. To hide CPU cache lookup and scheduling, we further introduce an adaptive fan-out prefetcher that predicts likely verification outcomes (j, b), consisting of the accepted draft length j and target-emitted continuation token b. A causal budget allocator concentrates prefetches on high-probability outcomes, while uncovered outcomes fall back to sequential lookup without changing committed tokens. Across two model families and three reasoning and structured-generation workloads, NgramRL commits 3.60-7.77 tokens per target-model forward in the warm-cache regime, up to 1.99x more than SuffixDecoding and 7.0x more than prompt-lookup decoding. On Spider, the prefetcher covers 96.6% of Qwen3-8B outcomes at a candidate budget of 16 and improves measured rollout throughput by 1.38x over synchronous NgramRL, approaching its 1.40x overlap ceiling.

</details>


### [257] [Closing the Modality Gap via Simplex-Constrained Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32890)
*Shubham Gupta,Siva Reddy,Perouz Taslakian,Valentina Zantedeschi,Cem Subakan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将多模态对比模型的嵌入域从单位球面改为概率单纯形，用轻量 softmax 适配器与全变差相似度，把模态间质心距离缩小 97–99%，并在五个检索基准上保持或超过原有性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP、CLAP 等多模态对比模型虽学到共享表示，但嵌入仍存在顽固的"模态间隙"（modality gap）。作者希望从几何结构入手消除这种分离。

Method: 纯几何方法：把嵌入域由单位球面 S^{d-1} 换成概率单纯形 Δ^{d-1}。两者内在维度相同，但单纯形的非负、单位质量约束充当跨模态校准的结构先验，迫使两个模态落在同一坐标系中。实现上接轻量 softmax 适配器，并用全变差（Total Variation）作为相似度度量。

Result: 在五个多模态检索基准上，质心 ℓ2 间隙降低 97–99%；检索性能与无约束标准模型持平或更优，表明单纯形几何带来的是真正的语义对齐。

Conclusion: 单纯形几何可作为一种有效的结构先验，在不牺牲检索性能的前提下显著缓解模态间隙，为多模态表示对齐提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: While multimodal contrastive models like CLIP and CLAP successfully learn shared representations, their embeddings typically exhibit a persistent \emph{modality gap}. We propose addressing this separation through a purely geometric approach: changing the embedding domain from the unit sphere, $S^{d-1}$, to the probability simplex, $Δ^{d-1}$. Although both manifolds share the same intrinsic dimension, the simplex acts as a structural prior; its nonnegative, unit-mass constraints serve as a cross-modal calibration that forces both modalities to ground themselves in the same coordinate system. By attaching lightweight softmax adapters and using Total Variation similarity, we reduce the centroid $\ell_2$ gap by 97--99\% across five multimodal retrieval benchmarks. This confirms that simplicial geometry induces genuine semantic alignment, matching or exceeding the retrieval performance of standard unconstrained models.

</details>


### [258] [SemantEq: Faithful Bidirectional Equivalence of Formal Statements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32919)
*Xinrui Li,Pawan Sasanka Ammanamanchi,Siddharth Bhat,Rianna Jamdar,Yongxi Lin,Stella Biderman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SemantEq 关系及配套工具，更严格地判定形式化陈述是否忠实翻译原陈述，通过"允许步骤链"而非简单逻辑等价来区分语义等价与无关陈述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陈述自动形式化的瓶颈已从证明正确性转向陈述忠实性。直接用逻辑等价 P↔Q 检验会"证明太多"：任意两个可证陈述都能经 P↔True↔Q 通过，证明器越强误判越多。现有方法通过限制证明器并检查两个方向引用的方式不够原则化。

Method: 1) SemantEq：要求每个陈述只能通过允许的步骤链（逻辑重排、库内方程重写等）转化为另一陈述，按 Lean 校验的证明项而非策略脚本判定。2) SemantEq-Eval：无模型的搜索式验证器。3) SemantEq-ProofAudit：基于该关系推导策略，审计模型生成的证明。

Result: 在 ProofNetVerif 测试集上，SemantEq-Eval 达 100% 精确率、65.6% 召回（BEq+ 为 95.8%/61.8%）；CPU 时间增 8.9 倍最多多判 3 对。LLM 能找更多证明，但两个 prompt 下对约五分之二非等价对证出双向。ProofAudit 拒绝全部 104 个非等价对，并将 407 个等价对召回从 65.4% 提升至 81.1%。

Conclusion: 将等价证明的生成与接受分离，使更强证明器可提升召回而不改变验收语义，为陈述忠实性提供了更可靠且有原则的判定框架。

Abstract: Statement autoformalization translates mathematics from natural language into a proof assistant such as Lean 4, and models now do it at scale. As provers get stronger, the burden of correctness shifts from the proof to the statement, that is, to whether the formal statement faithfully translates its source. Given a trusted reference formalization, the natural test is logical equivalence, and it proves too much. $P \leftrightarrow Q$ holds trivially for any two statements $P$ and $Q$ that the library proves, because a prover can prove each separately and pass through $P \leftrightarrow \mathrm{True} \leftrightarrow Q$. The stronger the prover, the more unrelated pairs pass. Earlier methods restrict the prover and check that each proof names the other statement. We introduce SemantEq, a more principled relation that instead requires each statement to turn into the other only through a chain of allowed steps, such as logical rearrangement and rewriting with the library's own equations. Our programs judge a proof by the proof term Lean checks, not by its tactic script. SemantEq-Eval, a search without a model, reaches 100% precision and 65.6% recall on the ProofNetVerif test split under our adjudicated labels, against 95.8% and 61.8% for BEq+, and an 8.9-fold increase in its CPU time adds at most three pairs. A language model finds more proofs, but under two of four prompts it proves both directions of about two in five of the non-equivalent pairs. SemantEq-ProofAudit audits each model-written proof under a policy derived from the relation and accepts none of the 104 non-equivalent pairs, and it raises recall on 407 equivalent pairs from 65.4% to 81.1% under the best prompt. Separating the generation of equivalence proofs from their acceptance lets a stronger prover raise recall without changing what an acceptance means. We release code and data at: https://github.com/hlxr89/SemantEq

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [259] [Understanding Tool-Mediated Interaction Failures in Agentic GUI Software: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31698)
*Yuchen Shao,Wenjing Liu,Yuheng Huang,Mengqian Xu,Ziqun Bao,Long Yang,Lei Ma,Ting Su,Chengcheng Wan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体通过工具与实时GUI交互时出现的失败，构建了Malva缺陷库（335个真实失败，来自40个开源应用），归纳为11种缺陷模式，并提出静态契约检查器MalvaGuard。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体无法直接操作GUI，需依赖截图/UI树捕获、动作转换、变化上报等工具中介交互，这种中介可能使智能体对任务状态的视图与实时GUI产生偏差，导致失败。

Method: 收集并确认40个开源应用中的335个真实失败构建Malva缺陷库；将失败归类为源于状态表示、状态转换和同步问题的11种缺陷模式；据此实现部署前静态契约检查器MalvaGuard。

Result: 缺陷普遍存在（90%应用含不止一种缺陷类型），且多为静默失败：86.6%的失败表现为结果不正确，其中119例在无误停信号情况下产生错误行为。MalvaGuard在额外20个应用语料上达到57.3%精确率和76.8%召回率。

Conclusion: GUI中介交互的缺陷系统性且常静默，可通过缺陷分类体系与部署前静态检查在实践中加以识别和缓解。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly complete tasks through live graphical user interfaces (GUIs). Because the model cannot directly operate the GUI, these systems often rely on tools to capture screenshots or UI trees, translate model outputs into GUI actions, and report whether the interface changed. We study failures where this tool-mediated interaction makes the agent's view of the task diverge from the live GUI. We construct Malva, a defect library with 335 confirmed real-world failures from 40 open-source applications. These failures fall into 11 defect patterns rooted in state representation, state transition, and synchronization problems. They are widespread: 90% of the studied applications contain more than one defect type. They are also often silent: incorrectness appears in 86.6% of failures, including 119 cases that produce wrong behavior without a fail-stop signal. We operationalize this taxonomy with MalvaGuard, a pre-deployment static contract checker that reaches 57.3% precision and 76.8% recall on an additional 20-application corpus.

</details>


### [260] [Before the Ticket: Grounding HPC User Support in Documentation and Resolved Tickets with an Agentic LLM Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31703)
*Elham J. Barezi,Primus C Kabuo,Niveditha Nerella*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对HPC帮助台重复工单问题，构建了一个基于MCP调用两个工具（文档RAG与Llama-3-8B QLoRA微调模型）的本地化助手，但端到端合并并未超越单工具，其中微调工具效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPC帮助台收到的多数问题其实已在用户指南或历史工单中有答案，但指南难导航、工单历史用户无法检索，导致用户仍重复提交新工单。同时工单数据敏感，不能离开机构，需要有本地部署的解决途径。

Method: 构建带编排器的助手，通过Model Context Protocol调用两个工具：对实时文档的RAG，以及在从历史工单挖掘的11,244条查询-解决方案对上用QLoRA微调Llama-3-8B，并合并两者答案；所有模型均在本地或校内托管LLM服务上运行。用Purdue RCAC的400张留出工单（与训练集不重叠、无近似重复）评估，由两个未参与生成答案的模型家族作评委。

Result: 把解决方案改写为自助式指导再微调效果最好，与RCAC官方解决方案的一致性BERTScore F1从0.06提升到0.27，平均判为最正确。端到端系统未超越其工具，但两工具互补：逐题选更优答案可超过任一工具（判分3.41 vs 3.20，为oracle上界），而合并逻辑在78%答案中跟随文档工具。改为返回单一工具答案可恢复微调工具质量（3.19 vs 2.66），但学习选择器和LLM验证器都不及单用微调工具。

Conclusion: 经改写为自助指导的微调优于基线和文档RAG；两工具具互补性但对答案的合并/选择仍是未解难题，当前简单的合并反而削弱系统质量，未来应关注答案选择与验证的可改进空间。

Abstract: Most questions that reach an HPC help desk have already been answered in the center's user guides or in a ticket someone else filed last semester; users still file new tickets because the guides are hard to navigate and the ticket history is not theirs to search. We present an assistant that answers from both sources: an orchestrator calls two tools over the Model Context Protocol, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over the live documentation and a Llama-3-8B model fine-tuned with QLoRA on 11,244 query-resolution pairs mined from historical tickets, and merges their answers. Every model runs on-premises or on the university's hosted LLM service because ticket data may not leave the institution. On 400 held-out tickets from Purdue's Rosen Center for Advanced Computing, disjoint from training and free of near-duplicates, and evaluated by two judges from model families that generated none of the answers, fine-tuning on resolutions rewritten into self-service guidance is the clear winner in agreement with RCAC's resolutions (BERTScore F1 improves from 0.06 to 0.27 over the base model) and is judged most correct on average. The end-to-end system does not improve on its tools, although they are complementary: choosing the better answer per question would exceed either tool (judged correctness 3.41 versus 3.20, an oracle bound), while the merge follows the documentation tool in 78% of answers. Returning one tool's answer instead recovers the fine-tuned tool's quality (3.19 versus 2.66), but neither a learned selector nor an LLM verifier beats that tool alone.

</details>


### [261] [RubricCraft: Where Solutions Diverge, Criteria Emerge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31707)
*Hosein Hasanbeig,Priyanshu Gupta,Ashish Tiwari,Vu Le,Sumit Gulwani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RubricCraft is a method that constructs textual rubrics (evaluation criteria) for open-ended agent tasks by comparing multiple candidate solutions. On SpreadsheetBench v2, its rubrics reject fewer wrong outputs (29.5% vs. 42.6%) than prompt-only rubrics while accepting all checker-valid outputs and ranking correctness better.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Evaluating language agents on open-ended artifact-producing tasks is hard: model-based graders are inconsistent and exhaustive programmatic checks are impractical. Textual rubrics make criteria explicit, but writing them requires domain expertise, and rubrics generated from task descriptions alone miss requirements and failure modes that only become visible in attempted solutions.

Method: RubricCraft treats rubric construction as generating natural-language specifications guided by candidate solutions. Given a task description and independently sampled solver trajectories, it compares how each attempt satisfies or violates task requirements, and uses these differences to construct rubric criteria. It then applies the criteria to collected solutions and revises checks that overlook errors or penalize valid alternatives, yielding rubrics that capture partial correctness without needing any single solution as a complete reference.

Result: On SpreadsheetBench v2, versus a prompt-only rubric written and applied by the same model, RubricCraft rubrics pass fewer wrong outputs (29.5% vs. 42.6%), fail none of the outputs the benchmark checker accepts, and rank outputs by correctness more often while separating models equally often. Repair using a single attempt leaves grading unchanged, but repair driven by attempts from three model families acts on different tasks, and an evidence audit judges most repairs as improvements, many on tasks whose reference solution is itself wrong.

Conclusion: Solution-guided, multi-attempt-diverse rubric construction yields more reliable and discriminative grading of open-ended agent artifacts than description-only prompt-based rubrics, and also surfaces errors even in benchmark reference solutions.

Abstract: Evaluating language agents on open-ended, artifact-producing tasks is difficult: model-based graders can be inconsistent, while exhaustive programmatic checks are often impractical. Textual rubrics make evaluation criteria explicit, but writing them requires domain expertise. Rubrics generated from task descriptions alone can miss requirements and failure modes that become apparent in attempted solutions. We introduce RubricCraft, which treats rubric construction as the generation of natural-language specifications guided by candidate solutions. Given a task description and independently sampled solver trajectories, RubricCraft compares how each attempt satisfies or violates the task requirements and uses these differences to construct rubric criteria. It applies the criteria to the collected solutions and revises checks that overlook errors or penalize valid alternatives. The resulting rubrics capture partial correctness without requiring any single solution to serve as a complete reference. On SpreadsheetBench v2, against a prompt-only rubric written and applied by the same model, RubricCraft rubrics pass fewer wrong outputs (29.5% against 42.6%), fail none of the outputs the benchmark's own checker accepts, and rank outputs by correctness more often, while separating models as often. Repair on a single attempt leaves grading unchanged, but repair driven by attempts from three model families acts on different tasks, and an evidence audit judges most of those repairs improvements, many on tasks whose reference solution is itself wrong.

</details>


### [262] [Mining the Design Space of Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31709)
*Xavier Cadet,Junyan Cheng,Peter Chin,Kyle Richardson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出两阶段流程，从工作流代码与执行轨迹中挖掘可复现的LLM多智能体系统设计原则，并构建了包含28,568个可执行工作流的语料库来统计验证这些原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM多智能体系统（MAS）设计依赖试错和“民间经验”：人工研究只测试少数通信结构或拓扑，自动化优化器在选出任务特定工作流后丢弃搜索轨迹。作者希望把这些被丢弃的轨迹变成可统计检验的设计原则来源。

Method: 两阶段流水线：(1) 复用AFlow的MCTS优化器，在6个可组合算子与26个确定性验证的Enigmata任务上搜索，保留所有中间轮次，构建28,568个可执行工作流语料；(2) 沙箱化AutoResearch循环，在L0惩罚的验证目标下挖掘编排代码与提示模板中的特征，并在留出搜索上检验候选原则（共300次特征挖掘试验）。

Result: 发现三个层面的稳健效应：以独立求解器+一次性聚合开头（Star-first）的工作流表现更差（Δ=-0.045）；持久共享文档（SharedMemory）优于辩论（Δ=+0.135）；提示具体性与基于占位符的上下文路由带来的增益可与通信结构选择相当（Δ=+0.118）。

Conclusion: 该流程与结果证明，可把原本废弃的搜索轨迹转化为发现并验证多智能体工作流设计原则的统计试验平台。

Abstract: Designing large language model (LLM) multi-agent systems (MAS) still relies on trial and error and informal "folk wisdom." Manual studies test only a few communication structures or topologies (e.g., sequential, parallel, shared-memory, or debate), while automated optimizers discard search trajectories after selecting a task-specific workflow. We present a two-phase pipeline for mining reproducible design principles from workflow code and execution traces. We repurpose AFlow's MCTS optimizer across six composable operators and 26 deterministically verified Enigmata tasks, retaining all intermediate rounds to build a corpus of 28,568 executable workflows. A sandboxed AutoResearch loop then mines orchestration code and prompt templates under an $L_0$-penalized validation objective and tests candidate principles on held-out searches. Across 300 feature-mining trials, we identify effects that persist on held-out searches across three levels of workflow design: workflows beginning with independent solvers and one-shot aggregation (Star-first) perform worse ($Δ= -0.045$); persistent shared documents (SharedMemory) outperform debate ($Δ= +0.135$); and prompt specificity and placeholder-based context routing yield gains comparable to communication-structure selection ($Δ= +0.118$). More broadly, our pipeline and results show the feasibility of turning discarded search trajectories into a statistical testbed for discovering and validating multi-agent workflow design principles.

</details>


### [263] [Compact-Bench: Measuring What Context Compaction Costs Long-Horizon Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31710)
*Ruopei Sun,Jianfeng Cai,Kangwen Zhao,Wengang Zhou,Houqiang Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Compact-Bench 通过“完整历史 vs 仅摘要”配对续写实验，隔离压缩本身对长任务续写的影响：14 个摘要模型造成 5-64% 的修复失败损伤，LLM 摘要仅保留 ≤16% 的反复出现细节（机械抽取为 79.1%），说明信息保留不等于任务可续写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期编码智能体需靠上下文压缩突破窗口限制，但压缩可能抹掉完成任务所需的状态。已有评测只看效率、信息留存或端到端成功率，无法单独识别“某次历史替换”的因果影响。

Method: 提出 Compact-Bench：从同一仓库状态出发，配对比较“完整历史续写”与“仅摘要续写”；评测反复出现的细节与多次连续生产压缩。构建 35 个 SWE-bench 修复任务、146 个智能体会话、106 条生产压缩链，覆盖 14 个摘要骨干与 5 种提示。

Result: 在 22 个完整历史成功案例中，不同骨干损伤 5-64%：Claude Opus 4.8 保留最多，DeepSeek-Flash 损伤最大。以 Kimi-K3 作摘要器时，Kimi Code 提示保留最多续写（19/22），Claude Code 保留最多反复细节（15.0%）。LLM 摘要反复细节保留 ≤16.0%，机械抽取 79.1%，生产摘要首个边界为 15.7%。失败具系统性：某修复被 13/14 骨干损伤，另一修复被全部 5 种提示损伤。

Conclusion: 可靠的上下文压缩须保留“完成任务所需的进度”，仅保留相关信息不足以保证续写成功；需按进度可续性而非信息量来设计压缩。

Abstract: Context compaction lets long-horizon coding agents continue beyond their context windows, but can erase the state needed to finish a task. Prior evaluations measure efficiency, retained information, or end-to-end success, leaving the effect of a specific history replacement difficult to isolate. Compact-Bench pairs full-history and summary-only continuations from a shared repository state, and evaluates recurring details and successive production compactions. It covers 35 SWE-bench repairs, 146 agentic sessions, and 106 production chains. Across 14 summarizer backbones, stable damage ranges from 5-64% of 22 full-history successes: Claude Opus 4.8 preserves the most repairs, while DeepSeek-Flash damages the most. With Kimi-K3 as summarizer, the Kimi Code prompt preserves the most task continuations (19/22), whereas Claude Code retains the most recurring details (15.0%). LLM summaries retain at most 16.0% of recurring details, compared with 79.1% for mechanical extraction; production summaries retain 15.7% at the first boundary. Failures recur across methods: one repair is damaged by 13/14 backbones, and another by all 5 prompts. These results show that reliable compaction requires preserving the progress needed to complete a task, and that retaining relevant information alone does not ensure successful continuation. Code is available at https://github.com/cai-jianfeng/compact-bench and data is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/caijanfeng/compact-bench.

</details>


### [264] [MBABench-2.0: Evaluating AI Agents on Constructing Financial Spreadsheets Beyond Simple Verifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31711)
*Julian Poeltl,Thomson Yen,Patrick Shea,Shubham Anil Varma,Dhruv Rastogi,Zihao Chen,Mengshi Zhang,Hongseok Namkoong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 MBABench-2.0，含 101 个从零构建财务电子表格的任务，并用 129 条、12 类标准评价质量；前沿智能体最高通过率仅 16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子表格基准多为"填空式"任务，仅在已完成的表格上做小幅编辑，无法反映真实工作流中最核心、最耗时的部分：建模决策、格式设计与工作表布局。

Method: 构建 101 个原创的从零构建表格任务（最难任务估计需 3 年以上经验建模师 33 小时）；因可读性、可维护性、可审计性难以用单元测试或最终答案验证，设计覆盖 12 个类别共 129 条评价准则；对 8 个模型、4 种 harness 类型的 20 余个智能体进行评测。

Result: 前沿智能体很少能同时做到准确并满足质量准则，表现最好的智能体通过率仅 16%。

Conclusion: 当前智能体在从零构建电子表格方面存在显著能力差距，该基准为该方向提供了更贴近真实工作流的评测手段。

Abstract: Spreadsheets are the lingua franca of modern finance, driving valuation, forecast, audit, and many other core workflows of the industry. As LLM agents advance to automate knowledge work, spreadsheet construction is a natural and pertinent testbed. However, most existing spreadsheet benchmarks evaluate agents on fill-in-the-blank style tasks, where an agent performs minor edits in otherwise complete spreadsheets. This protocol fails to capture the most central and time-consuming part of the spreadsheet workflow: the modeling decisions, format design, and sheet layout regular knowledge workers need to toil through to build a workbook from scratch. We introduce MBABench-2.0, a benchmark of 101 original spreadsheet construction tasks, the hardest of which is estimated to take 33 hours for a modeler with 3+ years of experience. Unlike coding or math, where a unit test or a final answer can verify an agent's output, the quality of a financial spreadsheet that concerns readability, maintainability, or auditability, is not so simply verifiable. To holistically evaluate spreadsheet construction, we introduce 129 criteria spanning 12 categories that operationalize quality beyond the accuracy of the spreadsheet. Evaluating over 20 agents across 8 models and 4 harness types, we find that frontier agents rarely construct spreadsheets that are both accurate and meet our quality criteria: the top agent achieves a pass rate of only 16% on the benchmark. The benchmark demonstrates a substantial gap in spreadsheet building capability of current agents.

</details>


### [265] [Reuse, Rewrite, or Reject: Evidence-Constrained Trace Reuse for Repeated Mobile GUI Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31712)
*Bingyu Bai,Cheng Zhang,Erhu Feng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出图谱-文本模板实现移动GUI智能体的执行轨迹选择性复用，大幅降低LLM推理开销并加速执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动GUI智能体常重复执行相似任务，复用历史执行可减少冗余LLM推理与执行开销，但用户指令和环境变化可能使先前动作失效。

Method: 系统将变化归纳为六类代表性类别，发现变化未必使整个流程失效，单个转移在校验后可复用。提出图谱-文本模板：图捕捉UI状态与动作转移，文本编码任务意图、流程结构与带类型槽位；并用"记录-模板-回放"框架基于当前指令与实时UI状态选择性复用、调整或丢弃单个转移。

Result: 在AndroidWorld五个基础智能体上平均减少约80%的LLM推理token，多数智能体实现至少1.5倍加速；在Android与HarmonyOS真机评测中动作复用率达70.7-79.8%，多数配置下保持2倍以上加速。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度选择性复用执行转移，可在保证任务适应性的同时显著降低LLM推理成本并提升GUI智能体执行效率，且跨平台有效。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents frequently perform repetitive tasks with similar interaction procedures. Reusing previous executions can reduce redundant LLM inference and execution overhead, but variations in user instructions and execution environments may invalidate previously successful actions. We systematically characterize these variations into six representative categories and observe that such changes do not necessarily invalidate entire procedures: individual transitions can remain reusable when validated against the current task context and interface state. Based on this insight, we propose graph-text templates for selective execution-trace reuse. The graph captures UI states and action transitions, while the text encodes task intent, procedural structure, and typed slots for variable content. A record-template-replay framework constructs templates from verified executions and leverages the current instruction and live UI state to reuse, adapt, or discard individual transitions. Given an execution trace, our framework reduces LLM inference token consumption by approximately 80% on average across five base agents on AndroidWorld, with most agents achieving at least 1.5-fold execution speedup over LLM-only execution. In real-device evaluations on Android and HarmonyOS, our framework achieves action reuse ratios of 70.7-79.8% while maintaining over 2-fold execution speedup in most evaluated configurations.

</details>


### [266] [What Open-Source Projects Tell Their Coding Agents: A Census of AGENTS.md and CLAUDE.md](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31715)
*Pawel Zarzycki*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对13种语言的热门GitHub仓库进行普查，将根目录AGENTS.md和CLAUDE.md视为指令集，用模型辅助标注+人工复核分析编码智能体的指令文件实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体可通过仓库中的指令文件被引导，但这一新兴实践缺乏系统性实证了解。

Method: 普查13种语言的热门活跃GitHub仓库，以AGENTS.md与CLAUDE.md合并为单一指令集；采用模型辅助标注加人工复核，并做预设检验与探索性分析（流行度、活跃度、项目类型关联）。

Result: 指令文件出现在部分仓库及最受星仓库中；两者并存时CLAUDE.md常指向AGENTS.md；内容为项目知识与代码/构建/工作流规则的实用混合；大量指令不给理由；AI署名态度分歧（提交信息多禁止、PR多要求披露）；约1/3指令集有生成器痕迹，4/5创建后被编辑；流行度与PR及AI贡献规则相关；LLM/智能体项目在采用者中过代表；模型间一致性远高于与人工标注的一致性。

Conclusion: 指令文件实践普遍且多样，自动化标注需谨慎；作者公开标注、分类法与文件版本指针。

Abstract: Coding agents can be guided with instruction files in a repository. We examine this emerging practice through a census of popular, active GitHub repositories in thirteen languages, treating each repository's root AGENTS.md and CLAUDE.md as one instruction set. Agent instructions appear in of repositories and of the most-starred; where both files exist, CLAUDE.md usually points to AGENTS.md. We analyse statements using model-assisted annotation and human review. The annotations reveal a practical mix of project knowledge and rules about code, building and workflow. Collaboration rules name concrete requirements, from test plans to commit formats, yet of directives give no reason. Projects disagree on AI attribution: commit-message rules more often prohibit it than require it, while pull-request rules more often require disclosure. A third of instruction sets have a generator-associated signature, a tool-inserted block, shared text or an AI-marked creation commit; four in five are edited after creation. Prespecified tests associate repository popularity with rules on pull requests and AI contributions. Exploratory analyses link activity to process-oriented content and show that LLM and agent projects are over-represented among adopters. The models agree much more closely with each other than with the human reviewer's topic assignments, a caution for automated annotation. We release the annotations, taxonomies and pointers to the analysed file versions.

</details>


### [267] [SWE-Clar: Can Coding Agents Clarify Incomplete Client Requirements and Deliver Greenfield Software?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31854)
*Jialei Li,Dingxian Wang,Xiaoyan Zhao,Yimeng Bai,Xuanyi Zhu,Yang Zhang,Chao Yin,Wentao Shi,Ganesh Prabhu,Karthik Gomadam Rajagopal,Brett Levert,Darvin Yi,Andrew Rabinovich,Zhao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 SWE-Clar，一个基于 Upwork 真实高价值委托的基准，评测代码代理的“需求澄清”能力与后续代码实现，强调先澄清后实现、并用显式测试检验需求理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由职业绿地开发中，代理需要把不完整客户需求转化为可用软件；客户难持续反馈，且拿到答复不等于正确理解复杂需求，因此需专门评测需求澄清能力。

Method: 基于 100 个 Upwork 真实项目（总支付≥4000 美元）构建任务，每个任务先进行多轮 agent-客户代理交互；随后独立运行“代码实现＋验收测试”和“澄清评估”两项，二者共享澄清上下文但隔离，防止评估问题泄露；澄清评估用 512 个问题及参考答案，覆盖显式与隐含需求。

Result: 九个前沿模型中表现最好的 GPT-6 Astra 实现得分最高，但任务通过率仅 67%，体现实软件开发挑战性。

Conclusion: 独立软件交付对当前模型仍困难；SWE-Clar 提供真实场景下澄清与实现分离的评测框架，数据与代码已公开。

Abstract: Greenfield software development in freelance settings requires coding agents to turn incomplete client requests into working software. This makes requirement clarification essential, yet clients may be unable to provide continuous feedback over long development cycles, and receiving their answers does not guarantee correct understanding of complex requirements. These challenges motivate two benchmark design choices: clarification precedes code implementation, and its effectiveness is assessed through explicit tests of requirement understanding. Guided by these choices, we introduce SWE-Clar, a benchmark grounded in real, high-value client commissions to evaluate requirement clarification and code implementation in this setting. It comprises 100 tasks based on real software development jobs on Upwork, each with at least US\$4,000 in total payments. Each task begins with multi-round interaction between a coding agent and a client agent, followed by separate runs for code implementation with acceptance testing and clarification assessment. For the latter, we construct 512 questions paired with reference answers from real client-freelancer interactions, covering both explicitly stated requirements and those inferred from the conversational context. Both runs begin from the same clarification context but remain isolated, preventing assessment questions from guiding code implementation. Experiments with nine frontier models show that even GPT-6 Astra, which has the highest observed implementation score, achieves a task pass rate of only 67%, underscoring the challenge of independent software delivery in this setting. The dataset and evaluation code are available at https://github.com/upwork/SWE-Clar.

</details>


### [268] [Knowing When to Stop: The Evidence-Action Gap in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31895)
*Tianyu Chen,Xinran Zhao,Vijay Viswanathan,Shoubin Yu,Mingyuan Zhou,Tongshuang Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 将"环境不可完成"作为可测量现象，提出ImpasseBench（在可修复任务中注入12类阻塞），以"证据充分步"为锚点评估智能体的过度坚持/求助行为，并测试升级工具与外部监控两种干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体被训练为持续尝试完成任务，即使环境已使其不可能完成。通常仅需几步就能获得"只有外部帮助才能恢复"的证据，但智能体很少据此行动：过度坚持、尝试不当绕行、并宣称记录不支持的"成功"——作者称之为"证据—行动鸿沟"（evidence-action gap）。

Method: 提出ImpasseBench：在原本可解的修复任务中注入12种真实环境阻塞，并在每条轨迹中标注"证据已足够"的具体步数。以该步为基准对动作打分，从而区分合理努力、过度坚持与过早交接。在8个前沿模型、19,371条轨迹上评估；并测试两种干预：升级（escalation）工具，以及面向忽略该工具模型的external monitor。

Result: 几乎所有需要外部帮助的运行都收集到了充分证据，但仍有三分之一耗尽预算；检测到的所有不安全请求均发生在该证据步之后。升级工具将预算耗尽率从34.0%降至6.7%；外部监控使环境恢复后的任务成功率近乎三倍。但触发的升级仍非有效交接：工单能可靠指出阻塞点，却常遗漏智能体自身的进度与验证状态。

Conclusion: "知道何时停止"需要显式支持：既要在让智能体交接的工具层面提供支持，也要在它留下的报告中结构化呈现进度与验证状态。仅靠智能体的自发判断不足以弥合证据—行动鸿沟。

Abstract: Coding agents are trained to complete tasks, and they keep trying even when the environment makes completion impossible. The evidence that only outside help can restore progress usually appears within a few steps, yet agents rarely act on it: they over-persist, attempt undesired workarounds, and claim success the record does not support -- a phenomenon we call evidence-action gap. To measure it, we introduce ImpasseBench, which injects twelve realistic environment blockers into otherwise solvable repair tasks and marks, in each trajectory, the step at which the collected evidence becomes sufficient. Scoring actions relative to that step separates appropriate effort from over-persistence and premature handoff. Across eight frontier models and 19,371 trajectories, we find that nearly every run that needs outside help collects sufficient evidence, yet a third still exhaust their budget, and every unsafe request we detect comes after that point. Fortunately, an escalation tool, or an external monitor for models that ignore the tool, already guards against much of this behavior: the tool cuts budget exhaustion from 34.0% to 6.7%, and the monitor nearly triples task success after environment restoration. Even then, a fired escalation is not yet a useful handoff: tickets reliably name the blocker but often omit the agent's own progress and verification status. Taken together, we argue that knowing when to stop therefore needs explicit support, both in the tool that lets an agent hand off and in the report it leaves behind.

</details>


### [269] [Are LLM-Generated Formal Specifications Faithful to Their Requirements? A Study on Bimodal Formal Specification Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32053)
*Wenhan Wang,Shiming Chen,Zeyu Sun,Shenghua Feng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文对"双模态"场景下的LLM形式化规约生成进行实证研究：同时给出自然语言需求与正确性未知的代码实现，构建1403个C程序基准并用Frama-C/ACSL验证，发现现有方法在代码错误时易生成弱规约而误通过验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM规约生成工作普遍假设输入程序是正确的，这与形式化验证的真正目的（判断正确性未知的程序是否满足需求）相悖，因此需要研究在代码正确性未知时的规约生成表现。

Method: 构建包含1,403个C程序的基准，在双模态设定（自然语言需求+代码实现）下用LLM生成ACSL规约，并用Frama-C进行验证；同时与先进的提示方法以及非形式化的HoarePrompt方法进行对比分析。

Result: 主要发现：1）当代码正确时，额外提供自然语言需求并不能提升生成规约的通过率；2）当代码错误时，LLM可能生成弱规约或被实现误导的规约从而错误通过验证，且高级提示方法无法完全缓解；3）相比非形式化的HoarePrompt，LLM生成的形式化规约在预测需求-代码一致性上效果更差。

Conclusion: 这是首次将LLM形式化规约生成用于"按需求验证程序正确性"的探索，指出"忠实于需求的规约生成"是未来LLM形式化规约研究的新方向。

Abstract: Formal specification generation is a critical step towards formal verification and correctness assurance of software. Recently, large language models have been widely adopted in automatic generation of formal specifications. However, existing LLM-based specification generation approaches often assume that the input program is correct, which deviates from the real purpose of formal verification---to figure out whether the program meets its requirements when its correctness is unknown. Therefore, we conduct an empirical study of LLM-based specification generation under the bimodal setting: given both the natural language requirement and its corresponding code implementation, whose correctness is unknown. We construct a benchmark of 1,403 C programs and evaluate ACSL specification generation with Frama-C verification. Our study reaches several interesting findings: first, when the code implementation is correct, providing the natural language requirement does not further improve the pass rate of generated specifications. Second, when the code implementation is incorrect, LLM-based specification generation may erroneously pass verification by producing weak or implementation-misled specifications, and this cannot be fully alleviated by advanced prompting approaches. Compared with the non-formal HoarePrompt approach, LLM-generated formal specifications are less effective in predicting requirement-code consistency. Our study takes a first look at applying LLM-based formal specification generation to verifying program correctness against requirements, and points to requirement-faithful specification generation as a new direction for future LLM-based formal specification research.

</details>


### [270] [CrowdStrike Channel File 291: An Engineering Analysis of a Global Endpoint Outage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32078)
*Luiz Parente*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对2024年7月19日CrowdStrike更新导致约850万台Windows设备崩溃事件的系统性复盘：根源为检测模板声明21个输入而传感器代码仅提供20个，触发内核模式内容解释器中未受检查的数组访问；文章识别出四类保障缺口并提出分层控制建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该事件影响规模巨大（约850万设备），但公开讨论多集中于单一"代码bug"，缺乏对接口验证、测试选择、部署策略与恢复依赖之间交互作用的整体分析。作者希望区分确证的调查结论与工程推断，并从生命周期保障角度解释为何特权软件所消费的配置需要与代码同等的保障。

Method: 基于多源材料的案例重建与工程分析：厂商技术报告、微软崩溃分析与恢复指导、国会记录、运营影响报告。方法上对文献发现与工程推断做明确区分，并围绕接口验证、测试选择、部署政策、恢复依赖性四条线索进行交互分析。

Result: 识别出四项具有实质后果的保障缺口：(1) 验证所依据的定义与实际消费者不匹配；(2) 测试恰好避开了缺失的那个输入；(3) 分发环节缺少以健康状态为门控的渐进发布；(4) 恢复路径依赖网络连通性，而部分崩溃主机无法维持连接。作者强调发布回退、端点恢复与服务恢复必须分别评估。

Conclusion: 建议的控制措施分为三类——缺陷预防、暴露遏制、服务恢复——并配套明确验证准则、发布节奏约束与运营权衡说明。核心结论：特权软件所消费的配置需具备与代码相当的生命周期保障；同时须将版本回退、端点修复与服务恢复作为相互独立的目标分别设计。

Abstract: On July 19, 2024, a CrowdStrike Falcon content update caused Windows systems to crash, affecting about 8.5 million devices according to a Microsoft estimate. The immediate defect was an inconsistency between a detection template that declared 21 inputs and sensor code that supplied 20. A new matching criterion exposed an unchecked array access in the kernel-mode content interpreter. This article reconstructs the incident from vendor technical reports, Microsoft's crash analysis and recovery guidance, congressional records, and operational impact reports. It distinguishes documented findings from engineering inferences and examines how interface validation, test selection, deployment policy, and recovery dependencies interacted. Four assurance gaps were consequential: validation against a definition the consumer did not meet, tests that avoided the missing input, distribution without health-gated promotion, and recovery paths requiring connectivity that some crashing hosts could not maintain. Recommended controls separate defect prevention, exposure containment, and service restoration, with verification criteria, release-cadence constraints, and operational tradeoffs. The case shows why configuration consumed by privileged software requires lifecycle assurance comparable to code, and why release reversion, endpoint recovery, and service restoration must be assessed separately.

</details>


### [271] [Beyond Correctness: How Novice Software Engineering Students Exercise Oversight of Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32118)
*Ali Shakiba,Thomas Chaffey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过145名软件工程一年级学生的962条AI交互记录与172次干预，分析学生在AI辅助工程中识别何种输出特征、为何及如何干预。发现74.4%的干预涉及"适切性"问题而非明确错误（16.9%），学生多采取直接修改输出（51.0%）或优化提示词（28.2%），且感知到轻微缺陷时评估信心更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI使软件工程学生的工作从"产出制品"转向"评估与监督AI生成结果"，但学界对学生在此类干预中识别哪些输出特征、如何在真实工程情境中行使监督知之甚少。

Method: 收集145名一年级软件工程学生通过结构化AI日志记录的962条学生-AI交互轮次（含172次学生声明的干预），采用多维度编码框架，刻画所识别输出特征、干预原因、采取的行动及行动方式。

Result: 74.4%的干预涉及"适切性问题"，远高于正确性问题的16.9%，表明监督更多关乎合理输出是否适合工程任务而非显式错误。可分类干预中，学生最常直接修改输出（51.0%），其次为优化提示词（28.2%），二者多与工程适切性相关。个体内分析显示，感知含轻微缺陷的输出伴随更低的评估信心。

Conclusion: 新手AI监督超越了错误检测，涵盖对"适用性"的判断以及对AI辅助工程工作的主动干预两方面。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) is shifting software engineering students' work from producing artefacts towards evaluating and supervising AI-generated outputs. However, little is known about what characteristics students identify in outputs when reporting interventions, or how they exercise oversight during authentic software engineering work. We analysed 962 student-AI interaction turns documented by 145 first-year software engineering students through structured AI Journals, including 172 student-declared interventions. Using a multi-dimensional coding framework, we characterised the output characteristics identified in these reported interventions, students' reasons for intervening, the actions they took, and how those actions were enacted. Fitness problems were identified in 74.4% of interventions, compared with 16.9% involving correctness problems, indicating that oversight more often concerned whether plausible AI outputs were suitable for the engineering task than whether they contained explicit errors. Among classifiable interventions, students most frequently modified generated outputs (51.0%) or refined their prompts (28.2%), with both response types commonly associated with engineering fit. Within-student analysis further showed that outputs perceived as containing minor flaws were associated with lower evaluation confidence than outputs perceived as perfect. Taken together, these findings characterise novice AI oversight as a process that extends beyond error detection to encompass both judgement of fitness for purpose and active intervention in AI-assisted engineering work.

</details>


### [272] [Pairing Matters: Performance, Failures, and Efficiency in Lightweight Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32131)
*Lu Zhao,Christopher M. Poskitt,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对6个开源agent harness与3个轻量级编码模型的全部18种组合在100个SWE-bench Verified任务上进行受控全因子评测（共1800次运行），发现harness-model配对极大影响解题率（同一模型跨harness最多相差44个百分点）、失败模式与token效率（每解决任务token消耗可相差20倍以上），据此提出配对选择与harness工程实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件团队自托管编码agent需将模型与agent harness配对部署，但现有榜单只报告固定的harness-model配置，缺乏跨配对的受控比较，无法指导实际选型。

Method: 对6个开源harness×3个轻量级编码模型的全因子组合，在100个SWE-bench Verified任务上于统一serving、计量与评分条件下运行1800次；并对1100次失败运行应用基于证据的失败分类法进行分析。

Result: 同一模型跨harness解题率最多差44个百分点且harness排名随模型变化；不同配对的失败模式差异显著，某harness下的主导失败模式在另一harness下未必主导；即便同为模型驱动的循环式harness，每解决任务计费token相差超过20倍。

Conclusion: harness-model选择是轻量级编码agent部署的核心决策，单一harness或模型上的强表现不能外推到其他组合；本文给出配对选择、token效率评估与轨迹驱动harness工程的实践指导，并公开全部实验记录与分析产物。

Abstract: Self-hosting coding agents is often necessary for software-engineering teams that must keep proprietary code and development traces within controlled infrastructure. Lightweight, open-weight models make such deployment more feasible by reducing hardware requirements, while strong results on repository-level benchmarks demonstrate their potential for demanding coding tasks. Deploying these models as coding agents requires pairing them with an agent harness, making their joint performance a key deployment consideration. Yet existing leaderboards primarily report fixed harness-model configurations rather than controlled comparisons across alternative pairings. To address this gap, we investigate harness-model interactions through a controlled full-factorial evaluation of six open-source harnesses and three lightweight coding models across 100 SWE-bench Verified tasks, producing 1,800 graded runs under common serving, metering, and grading conditions. For the same model, resolution rates differ by up to 44 percentage points across harnesses, while harness rankings change across models. We apply an evidence-based failure taxonomy to 1,100 unsuccessful runs and find substantial differences in failure patterns across harness-model pairings. A model's dominant failure modes under one harness need not dominate under another. Efficiency is similarly dependent on the pairing: even among model-directed loop harnesses, charged tokens per resolved task vary by more than 20-fold. These findings make harness-model selection a central deployment decision for lightweight coding agents. Strong performance with one harness or model does not guarantee similar results with another. We translate these findings into practitioner guidance for pairing selection, token-efficiency assessment, and trajectory-informed harness engineering, and release the complete experimental records and analysis artifacts.

</details>


### [273] [Large-Scale Study of GitHub Copilot Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32313)
*Izah Sohail,Yerdanat Kurmantayev,May Mahmoud,Sarah Nadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究真实环境下7,581个Copilot生成的PR中的人机协作轨迹，发现58%无人工干预，干预通常短暂，且主要工作发生在初次轨迹中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体研究多基于SWE-bench类基准，假设智能体在单轨迹中自主运行，这与开发者实际与智能体交互式协作的真实场景不符，存在研究空白。

Method: 收集7,581个由Copilot编写且会话数据公开的PR，分析其人类在环（human-in-the-loop）交互轨迹；统计干预比例、Human-AI循环次数、合并与未合并PR的迭代差异，并对代表性PR做定性分析。

Result: 58%的Copilot PR无人工干预；干预时协作较短（中位数2次Human-AI循环）；合并的PR初次轨迹迭代更少；首次干预后轨迹显著减少20.8次迭代、8.4分钟，而后续干预迭代下降很少、时长无显著变化；开发者还通过反馈委托工作或自行添加提交等多种方式协作，但有时需多次甚至跨PR版本重复引导Copilot。

Conclusion: 真实人机协作中大部分工作在初始轨迹完成，开发者干预多为小而聚焦的请求；研究揭示了单轨迹自主假设的局限，为更贴近现实的编码智能体评估与设计提供依据。

Abstract: AI coding agents are increasingly integrated into software development workflows. However, most coding agent studies rely on benchmarks similar to SWE-bench, which assume autonomous agents operating in a single trajectory. This assumption does not mirror real-world usage, where developers interactively collaborate with agents. We address this gap by studying human-in-the-loop agent trajectories in the wild, focusing on 7,581 Copilot-authored PRs with publicly accessible session data. We find that 58% of Copilot PRs have no human intervention, and that when developers do intervene, collaboration is brief (median of 2 Human-AI loops). Merged PRs also tend to have fewer initial trajectory iterations than unmerged ones. We also find that the trajectory following the first human intervention exhibits a significant drop of 20.8 iterations and 8.4 min in duration. However, trajectories following later interventions show minimal iteration drops and no significant duration change, signifying that most of the work happens in the initial trajectory. Our qualitative analysis of illustrative PRs explains this pattern: many developer interventions are small, focused requests that Copilot completes in shorter later trajectories. We also illustrate that developers collaborate with Copilot in multiple ways, including delegated work through feedback as well as adding their own commits. However, we find that developers may need to guide Copilot through multiple (sometimes repeated) interventions in the PR, or even across multiple editions of the PR.

</details>


### [274] [From Faulty Claims to Incorrect Patches: An Empirical Study of Task-Grounded Reasoning in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32338)
*You Wang,Jinjun Huang,Zhonghao Jiang,Jialun Cao,Jiachi Chen,Zhongxin Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次实证研究编码agent链式思维中的"任务接地推理"(task-grounded reasoning)：从4个开放权重推理模型在SWE-bench Pro上的400条轨迹中抽取可核查的原子断言并验证其对错，发现此类推理仅占CoT token的18.5%却每条轨迹产出191个断言，至少9.6%为错误；2/3的错误补丁可追溯到错误断言，修正单个断言能使89.0%的用例修复关联缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对编码agent失败的轨迹分析多停留在与任务无关的行为模式（如重复动作），但agent可能流程合理却基于对代码、需求或候选修复的错误理解而失败。推理模型的CoT中恰恰暴露了这类可核查的任务相关断言，然而此前不清楚CoT中有多少可核查断言、多少是错的、错误断言是否真的导致错误补丁。

Method: 在SWE-bench Pro上分析4个开放权重推理模型的400条轨迹。用LLM从CoT中抽取原子断言，由LLM agent采集证据对断言逐一判定对错，再人工将错误补丁的缺陷回溯到具体错误断言，并用"断言纠正后的重放"验证每条因果链。

Result: 任务接地推理仅占CoT token的18.5%，但每条轨迹平均含191个原子断言，其中至少9.6%有误；错误断言出现在96.7%的已解决轨迹中，故其存在本身是弱失败信号；但188个错误补丁中2/3可追溯至错误断言；纠正单个断言后有89.0%的用例修复了关联缺陷，纠正全部此类断言可解决11.7%的失败任务。

Conclusion: CoT中携带可用于诊断和修复agent失败的任务特定信号，但要利用它们必须精准定位少数真正导致补丁缺陷的错误断言，而非简单以错误断言数量作为失败判据。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based coding agents increasingly resolve real-world software engineering tasks, yet still fail on many of them. Prior trajectory studies characterize these failures through task-agnostic patterns of agent behavior, such as repeated actions. However, an agent may follow a sound workflow yet act on a faulty understanding of the existing code, the task requirements, or a candidate fix. Reasoning models, which power most state-of-the-art coding agents, reveal this understanding in their native chain-of-thought (CoT), whose claims about the task can be checked against the facts. Yet it remains unclear how many such checkable claims the CoT contains, how many are faulty, and whether faulty claims actually cause incorrect patches. We present the first empirical study of such claims, which we term task-grounded reasoning, analyzing 400 trajectories of four open-weight reasoning models on SWE-bench Pro. We extract atomic claims from the CoT with an LLM, assess them against evidence gathered by an LLM agent, and manually trace the defects of incorrect patches back to faulty claims, confirming each link with a claim-corrected replay. Task-grounded reasoning covers only 18.5% of the CoT tokens, yet yields 191 atomic claims per trajectory, at least 9.6% of which are faulty. Faulty claims also occur in 96.7% of the resolved trajectories, so their mere presence is a weak signal of failure. Yet two thirds of the 188 incorrect patches trace back to faulty claims. Correcting such a claim makes the agent fix the linked defect in 89.0% of the cases, and correcting all such claims resolves 11.7% of the failed tasks. Our findings show that the CoT carries task-specific signals for diagnosing and repairing agent failures, but exploiting them requires pinpointing the few faulty claims that actually cause patch defects.

</details>


### [275] [DeepAVR: Deep Analysis via Multi-Agent Collaboration for Automated Vulnerability Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32345)
*Woorim Han,Hyunjun Kim,Brian Junekyu Lee,Kyeongmin Kim,Younghan Lee,Hyungon Moon,Yunheung Paek*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DeepAVR：多智能体端到端漏洞自动修复框架，通过将中间修复结果转为结构化诊断反馈、以补丁条件重定位挖掘隐藏根因，在VulnLoc上修复率达92.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM漏洞修复方法假设完美故障定位或直接从sanitizer日志端到端修复，但当根因在物理上远离崩溃点或被逻辑掩盖时，初始报告信息不足，导致修复失败。

Method: 提出多智能体框架DeepAVR，将修复分解为故障定位、补丁生成、编译验证、安全验证、功能验证与补丁评审等专用角色，并在角色间路由反馈以迭代优化定位与补丁假设；核心机制包括补丁条件重定位（用中间补丁的执行结果作为诊断信号发现隐藏根因）和根因导向的补丁评审阶段（识别症状级修复与不完整修复）。

Result: 在真实C/C++漏洞修复数据集VulnLoc上显著优于现有方法，为92.3%的漏洞生成正确补丁；在SAN2VULN上展现强泛化能力，可有效修复训练中未见过的漏洞。

Conclusion: 将中间修复产物转化为诊断反馈、迭代协同定位与补丁生成，是应对隐藏根因漏洞的有效范式，且具备跨数据集泛化性。

Abstract: Automated Vulnerability Repair (AVR) has emerged as a critical defense against software security flaws, aiming to triage and fix bugs autonomously. Early LLM-based approaches focused on patch generation under the assumption of perfect fault localization, while recent end-to-end methods attempt to repair vulnerabilities directly from sanitizer logs. However, these methods often fail when the root cause is deeply hidden--either physically distant from the crash site or logically masked by interdependent errors--making the initial report insufficient. We propose DeepAVR, a multi-agent framework for end-to-end AVR that performs deep analysis by transforming intermediate repair outcomes into structured diagnostic feedback. DeepAVR decomposes repair into specialized roles--Fault Localization, Patch Generation, Compile Validation, Security Validation, Functionality Validation, and Patch Review--and routes feedback across these roles to iteratively refine both localization and patch hypotheses. In particular, DeepAVR introduces patch-conditioned re-localization, which treats execution results from intermediate patches as diagnostic signals to uncover hidden root causes, and a root-cause-oriented patch review stage that detects symptom-level fixes and incomplete repairs beyond execution-based validation. Evaluation on the real-world C/C++ vulnerability repair dataset VulnLoc shows that DeepAVR significantly outperforms prior methods, generating correct patches for 92.3% of vulnerabilities. Furthermore, results on SAN2VULN demonstrate strong generalization, with DeepAVR effectively repairing vulnerabilities unseen during training.

</details>


### [276] [Complexity concentration as a god-class and god-method detector: a pre-registered test of a scale-free outlier rule against tool definitions and human labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32466)
*Daniel Sagenschneider*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文预注册检验一个无需语料拟合常数的“上帝单元”判定规则：以修改z分数≥3.5判定稳健离群复杂度；在445个多语言仓库与492个MLCQ Java仓库上对PMD、MLCQ评审及两种定义进行一半调参、一半读取的评估，结论为不确定；该规则对PMD上帝类的排序AUC达0.916/0.908。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有god-class/god-method检测器依赖在语料上拟合的原始尺度阈值，人群变化即需重新拟合；而LLM生成代码的复杂度分布可能不同，需要无需重拟合的检测器，首要问题是能否对齐人类代码的尺度。

Method: 规则为：若某单元复杂度相对其所在代码库是稳健离群（修改z分数≥3.5）则为god单元，无原始尺度常数。在445个仓库（10种语言、171个Java）及492个MLCQ Java仓库上，与PMD GodClass、MLCQ评审者及另外两种定义比较；信号与截止值在一半仓库上选定、在另一半上读取；报告per-repository AUC、non-inferiority边际、recovery与跨人群迁移测试。

Result: 结论在两个人群上均不确定，但原因不同：主运行中默认规则在恢复率上不劣于同人群调参的截止值（边际内），但多标记约两倍非god类；MLCQ上标记量相近，但合并恢复率未达边际。以认知复杂度per-file最大值排序PMD god class的per-repository AUC为0.916和0.908。合并数据下原始值排序优于per-repository分数，跨人群携带截止值几乎无损。额外标记多为小类（过半含达到PMD方法级阈值的单个方法），漏检为复杂度分散于多方法的大类；两名源码读者对额外标记性质意见不一致。

Conclusion: 集中度规则能定位复杂度所在之处，但“该处是否为god单元”属于耦合问题；作者以一项已注册的后续研究接手该问题，并明确指出当前结论对人类与LLM代码的尺度匹配仍不确定。

Abstract: God-class and god-method detectors mark concentrated complexity with a raw-scale threshold, a constant fitted on a corpus and re-fitted when the population moves. This paper pre-registers a test of a rule with no raw-scale constant: a unit is a god unit when its complexity is a robust outlier for its own codebase, a modified z-score of 3.5 or more. On 445 repositories in ten languages, 171 of them Java, and the 492 Java repositories of MLCQ, the rule is scored against PMD's GodClass, MLCQ's reviewers and two further definitions, with signal and cutoff chosen on half the repositories and read on the rest. LLM-written code may gather its complexity differently, the case for a detector that needs no re-fitting; the first question is whether one can match the scale of existing human-written code. The verdict is inconclusive on both populations, by different arms. The rule ranks PMD's god classes from the per-file maximum of cognitive complexity at per-repository AUCs of 0.916 and 0.908. On the main run it is, at its default, non-inferior within the margin to a cutoff tuned on the same population on recovery, and flags about twice as many non-god classes; on MLCQ it flags about as many and falls short of the margin on pooled recovery. Pooled, the raw value ranks god classes better than the per-repository score, and a cutoff carried between the populations lost almost nothing: both are human-written code on one scale, so the test is of matching that scale, not of surviving a shift. The rule's extra flags are small classes, just over half holding a method at PMD's own method-level threshold; its misses are large classes whose complexity is spread over many methods. Two readers of the source did not agree on what the extra flags are. Concentration finds where the complexity is; whether that place is a god unit is a question about coupling, which a registered follow-on study takes up.

</details>


### [277] [OPTARI: An open-source software framework for clinical translation of optoacoustic imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32468)
*Moritz Schillinger,Maximilian Bader,Adrian Buehler,Felix Wachter,Ferdinand Knieling,Vasilis Ntziachristos,Katharina Breininger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OPTARI 是一个开源图形化软件，基于 napari 与 PATATO，面向临床光声-超声（OPUS）研究，提供标注、重建、生色团解混、深度学习分割及时/频分析，并以独立可执行程序发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光声与超声联合成像应用前景广，但缺乏非编程用户可用的分析软件：现有工具要么是基于脚本的工程框架，要么是厂商专有软件，限制了临床研究的开展。

Method: 在 napari 图像查看器与 PATATO 光声数据处理工具包之上构建图形界面应用；支持厂商原生格式与开放 HDF5、符合 IPASC 标准；集成 ROI 标注、重建、生色团解混、深度学习组织分割、时域与谱域分析模块；以独立可执行文件分发。

Result: 功能组合在对比工具中无出其右；自动组织分割在肌肉组织上与专家标注结果相当；批处理使单次扫描分析时间由数分钟降至不足 1 秒；已在在研临床 OPUS 研究中实际使用。

Conclusion: OPTARI 在工程框架与医学研究之间架起桥梁，为临床光声成像研究提供可复现、标准化的开源分析基础。

Abstract: Combined optoacoustic (OptA) and ultrasound imaging is a promising modality across a wide range of pathologies. Its use in clinical research, however, is limited by a lack of analysis software accessible to users without programming expertise. Existing tools are either script-based engineering frameworks or proprietary vendor applications. We present OPTARI, an open-source graphical software application to facilitate large-scale clinical optoacoustic-ultrasound (OPUS) studies. OPTARI builds on the napari image viewer and the PATATO toolkit for OptA data processing. It supports native vendor and open HDF5 data formats, aligns with the IPASC standard, and is distributed as a standalone executable. Features include region-of-interest annotation, reconstruction, chromophore unmixing, deep-learning-based tissue segmentation, and temporal and spectral analysis modules. OPTARI offers a combination of features for clinical study analysis that none of the compared tools provide. The automated tissue segmentation produced results comparable to expert labeling for muscle tissue, and batch processing reduced analysis time from minutes to under one second per scan. The tool is used in ongoing clinical OPUS studies. OPTARI connects engineering frameworks with medical research and provides an open-source basis for reproducible and standardized analysis in clinical OptA imaging studies.

</details>


### [278] [WaitLens: Temporal VLM-Based Adaptive Waiting for Industrial GUI Testing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32482)
*Shika Ikeura,Xuan Wang,Ziyi Kuang,Jianping Xu,Chao Kang,Yongxiang Hu,Xiaoyu Wang,Juxing Yuan,Yangfan Zhou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 WaitLens，通过微调轻量级 VLM 在带等待结束时间标注的 GUI 加载轨迹上，预测自适应等待时机，大幅减少 GUI 测试中的冗余等待。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI 测试代理中固定等待占交互间隔时间 42.3%，且 62.9% 的等待发生在界面已加载完成后；已有自适应等待方案依赖特定应用数据或平台信息，难以跨工业环境泛化。

Method: 在带等待结束时间标注的 GUI 加载轨迹上微调轻量级视觉语言模型（VLM），并引入时间重采样构建多样化训练样本，使模型从时序观测中学习不同的加载行为，判断是否继续等待。

Result: 等待决策准确率达 86.08%（基线 VLM 仅 54.13%）；基于美团工业测试流水线录制数据，相较 20 秒固定等待，界面就绪后的平均额外等待减少 94.98%。

Conclusion: 用轻量 VLM 学习 GUI 加载时序可实现平台无关的自适应等待，显著提升 GUI 测试代理的工业部署效率。

Abstract: Improving the efficiency of GUI testing agents is important for industrial deployment. Beyond LLM inference, fixed waiting between consecutive interactions introduces substantial overhead. Our industrial study reveals that fixed waiting accounts for 42.3% of the time within the sampled interaction intervals, comparable to the 54.8% spent on model calls. Moreover, 62.9% of the waiting time is spent after the GUI has already finished loading. Although prior studies have proposed adaptive waiting strategies, their reliance on app-specific data or platform-specific information limits their applicability across diverse industrial environments. To this end, we propose WaitLens, which fine-tunes a lightweight VLM on GUI loading trajectories annotated with waiting end times. We introduce temporal resampling to construct diverse training examples, enabling the model to learn varying GUI loading behaviors from temporal observations. Experiments show that WaitLens achieves 86.08% accuracy in deciding whether to keep waiting, compared with 54.13% for the backbone VLM. Our evaluation using recordings from Meituan's industrial testing pipeline shows that WaitLens reduces mean extra waiting after the interface is ready by 94.98% compared with a 20-second fixed wait.

</details>


### [279] [Beyond Coverage and Mutation: A Blind-Spot Analysis of LLM-Generated Unit Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32548)
*Xiaoyu Shi,Wenqiang Luo,Boyang Yang,Haoye Tian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出义务驱动的测试评估框架BlindSpotEval(含边界值充分性、控制流充分性、断言强度三指标)与ObligBench基准,揭示LLM生成测试存在系统性的"快乐路径偏差"盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单元测试评估依赖分支覆盖率和变异分数等聚合指标,只能量化测试套件对程序行为的覆盖程度,却无法识别其系统性地遗漏了哪些"行为义务"(behavioral obligations)。

Method: 提出与Arrange-Act-Assert模式对齐的BlindSpotEval框架,包含Boundary-Value Adequacy、Control-Flow Adequacy、Oracle Strength三项指标及执行可靠性评估;并构建ObligBench——按复杂度分层、含源端义务标注的Java基准(895个复杂函数+700个低复杂度函数);在4种提示策略下评估4个LLM生成的14,320个测试。

Result: 错误断言占测试失败的15.6%,说明覆盖率高不代表断言正确;可执行测试中null、负值、空参数的覆盖率仅34.8%、23.8%、38.8%(布尔参数平均覆盖>93%);catch块与default分支的控制流充分性得分仅0.100和0.312;断言强度整体偏高但不均衡,对对象返回值属性和异常的断言较弱;这些盲点在低复杂度函数中同样存在。

Conclusion: LLM生成测试与人工测试一样存在快乐路径偏差,且该偏差不能仅用代码复杂度解释;BlindSpotEval能提供构件级的充分性画像,识别欠测的义务类型,指导超越聚合分数的针对性改进。

Abstract: Existing evaluation protocols for unit tests rely on aggregate metrics such as branch coverage and mutation score. These metrics quantify the extent to which a test suite exercises program behavior, but do not identify the behavioral obligations it systematically leaves unchecked. We propose BlindSpotEval, an obligation-based evaluation framework aligned with the Arrange-Act-Assert pattern. The framework comprises three metrics, Boundary-Value Adequacy, Control-Flow Adequacy, and Oracle Strength, complemented by an assessment of execution reliability. We also introduce ObligBench, a complexity-stratified Java benchmark comprising 895 complex and 700 low-complexity functions with source-side obligation annotations. Evaluating 14,320 tests from four LLMs under four prompting strategies reveals systematic blind spots. Incorrect assertions account for 15.6% of test failures, illustrating that coverage alone does not establish oracle correctness. Among executable tests, coverage of null, negative, and empty arguments reaches only 34.8%, 23.8%, and 38.8%, compared with average coverage above 93% for booleans. Catch blocks and default groups have Control-Flow Adequacy scores of only 0.100 and 0.312.
