Across both dimensions, LLM-generated tests exhibit the happy-path bias observed in human-written tests, concentrating on normal inputs and expected flows while leaving unusual values and exceptional paths unchecked. Oracle strength is high on average but uneven, with weaker assertions for object return property values and exceptions. These blind spots also occur in low-complexity functions, suggesting that code complexity alone does not explain them. BlindSpotEval provides construct-level adequacy profiles that identify under-tested obligation types and can guide targeted improvements beyond those supported by aggregate scores alone.

</details>


### [280] [Tool-Calling LLM Agents Under Realistic MCP Faults: Scale Does Not Purchase Fault Tolerance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32555)
*Antonio Ken Iannillo,Joshua Segun Owotogbe,Roberto Natella,Francesco Avallone,Kristina Kudryavtseva,Indika Kumara*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 MCP-Injector：协议感知的 MCP 工具故障注入中间件，基于真实生态故障分布注入永久/瞬态/间歇故障；实验显示故障使任务完成分从 8.08 降至 3.23（降 60%），模型规模（20B→120B）并不提升容错性，而 agent 框架的错误处理差异更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MCP 基准假设工具总是正确、及时响应，缺乏对真实工具故障下 agent 韧性的测试，因而掩盖了工具调用 agent 的运行脆弱性。

Method: MCP-Injector 为即插即用、协议感知的故障注入中间件，拦截 MCP 工具调用路径，支持永久/瞬态/间歇三档持久性及可设种子的调度；故障模型由 1,774 个开源 MCP 仓库的 57,240 条 issue/PR 线程挖掘的（故障模式×持久性）分布校准。实验覆盖 1,350 个单元：2 种骨干规模（20B、120B）× 3 种 agent 栈（自研多轮 plan-and-execute、OpenAI Agents SDK、LangChain）× 3 种注入条件。

Result: 永久故障偏重的压力剖面使任务完成分下降 60%（8.08→3.23/10）；120B 相比 20B 的韧性比无显著优势（p=0.96），故障下完成率反而略低（91.1% vs 95.6%，差 4.4 点，95% CI [-0.2, 9.3]）；不同 agent 栈吸收故障能力存在差异。

Conclusion: 规模不能换来容错，框架级错误处理比模型规模更关键；仅凭能力基准会掩盖运行脆弱性，协议感知故障注入应作为工具调用 agent 的补充评测维度。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on external tools exposed via the Model Context Protocol (MCP), yet current benchmarks assume tools respond correctly and on time, leaving agent resilience to realistic tool failures untested. We introduce MCP-Injector, a drop-in, protocol-aware fault-injection middleware that interposes on the MCP tool-call path with controlled persistence (permanent, transient, intermittent) and seedable schedules. Its fault model is calibrated to the observed incident distribution (failure mode x persistence) of the public MCP ecosystem, mined from 57,240 issue and pull-request threads across 1,774 open-source MCP repositories. In a 1,350-cell experiment crossing two backbone sizes (20B, 120B), three agent stacks (custom multi-round plan-and-execute, OpenAI Agents SDK, LangChain), and three fault injection conditions, we find that faults drawn from this permanent-heavy stress profile cause severe quality degradation (task-completion score falls by 60%, from 8.08 to 3.23 out of 10), and that scale does not purchase fault tolerance for the two gpt-oss backbones we test: the 120B retains no larger share of its fault-free score under faults (resilience-ratio model, $p = 0.96$), and its completion rate under faults is, if anything, lower (91.1% vs. 95.6%; difference 4.4 points, 95% CI $[-0.2, 9.3]$). Agent stacks differ in how well they absorb faults, which suggests that framework-level error handling matters more than model scale. These results demonstrate that capability benchmarks alone can mask operational brittleness, motivating protocol-aware fault injection as a complementary evaluation dimension for tool-calling agents.

</details>


### [281] [Lessons Outlive Their Failures: A Case Study of Rules for AI Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32578)
*Hassan Hassanzadeh Aliabadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 作者对个人纯 Markdown 知识库进行了六周案例研究：三个 AI 代理工具在同一套规则（AGENTS.md、技能、钩子、自动检查）下每日编辑。建立 118 条代理失败及其修复/是否复发的台账，测试月度文档审计对 47 条过时语句与 20 处植入错误的检出，并在隔离 git 克隆中重放 5 次历史失败。结论偏探索性：文档漂移是最大失败类别，机械检查有效，但许多针对旧失败的规则已失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 harness（规则文件、按需技能、运行时钩子、自动检查）研究多为单时点快照或短基准测试，缺少对规则长期演化与实际有效性的纵向观察，因此作者想弄清：代理失败如何发生、修复是否持久、文档是否会漂移，以及哪些规则该保留、哪些该淘汰。

Method: 六周纵向个案研究，单一仓库、三工具共用一套规则；构建 118 条代理失败台账（含修复与复发情况）；用月度 LLM 文档审计对 47 条已知过时陈述与 20 处植入错误进行测试；在隔离 git 克隆中对 5 个历史失败做有/无修复的对照重放；每个条件 3 次运行，结果按探索性呈现。主要模型包括 Claude Sonnet 5.5、Sonnet 4.5 等。

Result: 文档漂移是最大失败类别（118 条中 48 条，但其中 35 条来自同一次审计日）。单次审计约检出已知漂移的一半（Sonnet 5.5 均值 50%），其发现至少 93% 正确。Sonnet 5.5 在重放任务中完成 4/5；其中 3 个失败在有/无修复时都未复现。仅“过时计数”失败在去掉检查后重现，而检查在每次运行中都拦截成功。旧模型上，em-dash lint 检查成功拦截了代理起草的 em-dash。关于运行时估算的书面规则未能改变 Sonnet 4.5 的高估行为。

Conclusion: Harness 中的经验教训可能比其针对的失败存活更久：部分修复已成冗余，而文档漂移持续存在、仍需机械检查兜底。持续演化 harness 的工具会“增加规则快于淘汰规则”，因此应定期重测规则并及时退役过时条目。

Abstract: AI coding agents increasingly work inside a harness: rule files such as AGENTS.md, on-demand skills, runtime hooks and automated checks. Most studies of harnesses look at a single point in time or at short benchmark runs. I report a six-week case study of my personal system of record, a plain-markdown repository that three AI agent tools edited daily under one shared rule set. I built a ledger of 118 agent failures, their fixes and whether they came back. I also tested the system's monthly LLM documentation audit against 47 known stale statements and 20 planted errors. Finally, I replayed five past failures in isolated git clones, with and without their fixes. All results come from one repository with three runs per condition, so I report them as exploratory. Stale documentation (drift) was the largest failure class (48 of 118), although 35 of those were found on one audit day. A single audit run found about half of the known drift (Claude Sonnet 5.5 mean: 50%), and at least 93% of its findings were correct. On Sonnet 5.5, the agent finished four of the five replayed tasks. In three of those four, the failure did not happen, with or without its fix. Only the stale-count failure came back without its check, and the check stopped it in every run. On older models, an agent drafted an em-dash once while the em-dash lint check was active, and the check rejected it. A written rule on run-time estimates did not change Sonnet 4.5's overestimates. These results suggest that harness lessons can outlive the failures they were written to fix, while drift persists and still needs mechanical checks. Tools that evolve a harness add lessons faster than they retire them, so they should re-test their rules from time to time and retire the obsolete ones.

</details>


### [282] [Can AI Agents Help Robotic Engineers? Lessons from Two Focus Days in Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32636)
*Salim Fares,Steffen Herbold*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在一家编程工业机器人的中小企业开展案例研究，发现AI智能体整体未提升工程师生产力（仅高度重复性代码生成任务例外），但效果随工程师学习与智能体领域适配而改善；AI使用经验比机器人领域经验更能决定成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者已广泛使用能自主执行多步编程任务的AI智能体，但这一做法能否迁移到机器人工程尚不清楚——该领域工程师使用小众、厂商专属语言编程工业机器人，并依赖仿真工具的视觉反馈。

Method: 对一家编程工业机器人的中小企业进行案例研究，观察工程师使用AI智能体完成实际任务的表现。

Result: 智能体未提升工程师生产力，仅在对生成高度重复代码有需求的任务上有效；随时间推移效果变好，原因是工程师学会了使用方法且智能体被适配到该领域；AI经验对使用成功的影响大于机器人工程经验。

Conclusion: AI智能体并非即插即用方案：企业需培训工程师使用智能体、为学习曲线预留时间，并确保智能体配置匹配领域与开发者需求；显著的学习曲线表明，关于AI生产力收益的研究应采用纵向设计，否则会遗漏工程师在实践中的采纳过程。

Abstract: Software developers increasingly use AI agents that execute multi-step programming tasks on their own. However, it is unclear if and how this transfers to robotic engineering, where engineers program industrial robots in niche, vendor-specific languages and rely on visual feedback from simulation tools. Within this paper, we report a case study on the use of AI agents by an SME that programs industrial robots. Our results show that the agent did not make the engineers more productive, with the exception of a task that required the generation of highly repetitive code. However, the results with the agent improved over time, as the engineers learned how to use it and the agent was adapted to the domain. Moreover, the experience with AI mattered more for the success with the agent than the experience with robotic engineering. This shows that AI agents are not a plug-and-play solution. Instead, companies must train their engineers on the usage of agents, plan time for a learning curve, and ensure that agent configurations match the domain and developer requirements. The strong learning curve we observed further implies that studies on productivity gains with AI should be longitudinal, as they otherwise miss how engineers adopt agents in practice.

</details>


### [283] [StateMirror: Pre-Execution Detection of Hallucinated Tool Calls in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32648)
*Shiwen Shan,Yintong Huo,Zhiying Wu,Yuxin Su*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出并定义"幻觉工具使用"(hallucinated tool use)问题，并设计轻量级执行前检测器 StateMirror，在 harness 边界拦截动作、提取状态谓词并对真实仓库确定性校验，以低延迟、零 token 开销提前阻止无效动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 编码智能体常提出在真实执行环境中无效的动作（如访问不存在路径、缺失依赖包），初步统计 4,398 条轨迹显示此类幻觉普遍且代价高（恢复中位数需 3 步交互与 22.4K token）。现有框架依赖执行后的被动反馈，浪费运行时资源；而执行环境由具体代码库与 harness 组件构成，许多动作前提可在执行前抽取并验证。

Method: StateMirror 为轻量级执行前检测器：在 harness 边界拦截智能体动作，通过 AST 与命令分析提取状态谓词，并对实时仓库做确定性校验，精确定位环境不匹配之处，从而在执行前阻止无效动作及其后续恢复循环，并提供执行前反馈供智能体纠正。

Result: 在 2,508 条动作上评估：检测精度 0.940、F1 0.817，F1 比最强 LLM 判官高 35.3%；中位检测延迟由 17.73 s 降至 1.50 ms，且零 token 消耗。执行前反馈使智能体纠正超过 95% 的幻觉工具调用；在 79 个任务的在线缓解中，执行时间减少 15.8%、token 消耗减少 11.8%，同时保持任务解决率。

Conclusion: 通过对动作前提进行确定性、执行前校验，StateMirror 能高效拦截幻觉工具使用，替代昂贵的被动事后反馈，在不损失任务成功率的前提下显著降低智能体执行时间与 token 成本，为编码智能体的可靠执行提供实用方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based coding agents may propose invalid actions that are not applicable in the execution environment, such as accessing non-existent paths or missing packages. We term this mismatch hallucinated tool use. A preliminary study of 4,398 agent trajectories reveals that these hallucinations are pervasive and costly, requiring a median of 3 additional interaction steps and 22.4K tokens to recover. Existing frameworks rely on reactive, post-execution feedback, wasting runtime resources. Nevertheless, since an agent's execution environment is grounded in concrete codebase and harness components, many action prerequisites can be extracted and validated prior to execution. To this end, we introduce StateMirror, a lightweight pre-execution detector. StateMirror intercepts actions at the harness boundary, extracts state predicates via AST and command analysis, and deterministically validates them against the live repository. By pinpointing exact environment mismatches before execution, StateMirror preempts invalid actions and subsequent recovery cycles. Evaluation on 2,508 actions shows StateMirror achieves 0.940 precision and 0.817 F1 in detecting such hallucinations, surpassing the strongest LLM judge by 35.3% in F1 while reducing median detection latency from 17.73 s to 1.50 ms with zero token consumption. For practical mitigation, StateMirror's pre-execution feedback enables agents to rectify over 95% of hallucinated tool calls. During live mitigation on 79 tasks, StateMirror reduces agent execution time by 15.8% and token consumption by 11.8% while maintaining task resolution.

</details>


### [284] [CC4Q Workbench: From Quantum Repositories to Analyzable Code Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32651)
*Zenghui Zhou,Yuechen Li,Zheng Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出开源工具 CC4Q Workbench，将量子SDK代码注释的提取、量子相关分类与人工验证整合为可复用工作流，并发布六个SDK共39,593条句级代码注释单元数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子SDK的高层API需要理解数学原理、电路语义与硬件特性，代码注释能补充API调用中未显式表达的解释。但在新SDK中研究注释需协调注释抽取、量子专用分类、人工验证等多个独立步骤，导致重复的集成工作。

Method: 给定指定revision的Python仓库，自动抽取代码-注释对，将注释切分为句级单元，并用两个已训练分类器识别量子相关内容并分配量子知识类别；提供Web界面支持数据集浏览、代码片段查看、新仓库语料构建、模型预测隐藏的二分类人工标注及用导出标注微调分类器。

Result: 发布六个SDK数据集共39,593条SCCU的汇总统计，其中17,517条可在Web界面浏览；在原始数据集之外的三层Qiskit应用仓库上，流程无需改代码即可运行，产出11,517条SCCU；对379条SCCU的人工核验显示人工标注与二分类器预测的一致率为85.0%。

Conclusion: CC4Q Workbench 通过工具化整合降低量子软件注释研究的重复工程成本，并展示了跨仓库的可用性与可接受的人机一致性，支持后续语料构建与分类器迭代。

Abstract: Quantum software development kits (SDKs) expose high-level APIs whose use requires an understanding of underlying mathematical principles, circuit semantics, and hardware properties. Code comments can provide natural-language or structural explanations that are not explicit in API calls. However, studying comments in a new quantum SDK still requires researchers to coordinate separate steps for comment extraction, quantum-specific classification, and manual verification, resulting in repeated integration effort. To address this problem, we present CC4Q Workbench, an open-source workbench that integrates these steps into a reusable workflow. Given a Python repository at a specified revision, CC4Q Workbench automatically extracts code-comment pairs, segments comments into sentence-level units, and applies two trained classifiers to identify quantum-specific content and assign quantum-knowledge categories. Its web interface supports exploration of bundled datasets, inspection of code snippets, corpus construction from new repositories, manual annotation with model predictions hidden during binary labeling, and classifier fine-tuning using exported annotations. The release provides aggregate statistics for six SDK datasets comprising 39,593 sentence-level code comment units (SCCUs), of which 17,517 are browsable in the web interface. Applied to three additional application-layer Qiskit repositories outside the original CC4Q dataset, the pipeline ran without code changes and produced 11,517 SCCUs. Manual verification of 379 SCCUs showed 85.0% agreement between human annotations and the binary classifier's predictions.

</details>


### [285] [OpenDraco: A Platform for Engineering and Experimenting with Configurable Multi-Agent Architectures for Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32686)
*Xiang Feng Ye Pan,Vincenzo De Martino,Matias Martinez*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OpenDraco 是一个开源平台，将 LLM 多智能体系统的建模、执行、评估与重构整合到统一工作流中，并用自动化程序修复（APR）任务验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对 LLM 多智能体系统的迭代式实验生命周期（建模配置、执行评估、结果比较、系统重构）缺乏端到端支持，研究者难以在统一环境中比较不同配置的效果与代价。

Method: 平台将智能体、提示词、工具、模型、参数与通信拓扑表示为可导出、带版本的 JSON 规范，并转换为可执行的 LangGraph 工作流以支持不同交互结构；用户可用本地或云端 LLM 修改并重新评估配置。作者将其实例化于 APR 任务，在合成数据与真实 SWE-bench Lite 问题上评估一个串行基线与七种重构配置。

Result: 修复结果相近的配置之间成本差异显著；基于开源 LLM 的基线在 77 个 SWE-bench 问题中解决 12 个；额外的概念验证任务显示其可扩展到 APR 之外。

Conclusion: OpenDraco 支持异构多智能体配置的迭代实验，使研究者能在共同环境下考察结果与成本。

Abstract: Experimenting with LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) involves an iterative cycle of modeling configurations, executing and evaluating them, comparing results, and refining system designs. Existing approaches provide limited end-to-end support for this experimentation lifecycle. We present OpenDraco, an open-source platform that integrates these activities into a single workflow. OpenDraco represents agents, prompts, tools, models, parameters, and communication topology in exportable, versioned JSON specifications, translated into executable LangGraph workflows supporting different interaction structures. Users can modify and re-evaluate configurations using local or cloud-based LLMs. We instantiate OpenDraco for automated program repair (APR) and evaluate a sequential baseline and seven reconstructed configurations on synthetic and real-world SWE-bench Lite issues. The results reveal substantial cost differences among configurations with similar repair outcomes, while the baseline resolves 12 of 77 SWE-bench issues using open-source LLMs. Additional proof-of-concept tasks illustrate applicability beyond APR. OpenDraco supports iterative experimentation with heterogeneous MAS configurations, enabling researchers to examine their outcomes and costs within a common environment. Demonstration video: https://youtu.be/278sOR4lBEk.

</details>


### [286] [Trusting the Tool: Asymmetric Deference of LLM Vulnerability Auditors to Static-Analysis Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32876)
*ZhiHong Liu,Shuyao Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLM+静态分析流水线中，模型对证据真伪的辨别能力及“顺从”方向性此前未被测量。作者构建C/C++过程内分析器生成证明义务，在6种仅注释不同的证据条件下审计PrimeVul的916对漏洞/修复函数，共16,572次审计、11个模型配置。结果：真证据仅提升0.02；假证据呈不对称——假“保证”使漏报最多降19点，假“警报”无显著影响且更常被质疑；提示指令效果不稳定；逐条验证并展示被驳斥声明在假保证下提升12点。建议流水线先验证工具保证，研究应采用方向感知的顺从度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将LLM与静态分析结合的流水线让模型把分析器输出当作证据，但缺乏测量：模型能否区分真实/虚假证据，其审慎程度是否取决于证据的具体主张。需要方向感知地量化模型对工具的“顺从（deference）”。

Method: 构建C/C++过程内分析器生成证明义务；对PrimeVul中漏洞与已修复函数构造916对、4个不相交集合；设计6种仅注释不同的证据条件（含伪造“缺失检查已存在”的保证与伪造“新增检查缺失”的警报）；测试4家厂商11个模型配置、16,572次审计，无训练/微调；并做内部注册复现（7个新模型）及预先设定的逐条验证干预实验。

Result: 真证据几乎不提升准确率（+0.02）；假证据不对称：相对同操作的真注释，假保证使真实漏洞检出率最多下降19点，假警报则无显著影响且更常被质疑。复现确认假保证推动判决的力度大于假警报（均值不对称+0.16，95% CI +0.10~+0.24）。审计提示中的指令不能可靠降低顺从，最佳指令未能复现。逐条独立验证并展示被驳斥声明，在假保证下准确率+12点（p=0.002，预先设定检验）且真证据下无损失；第二模型增益较小且不显著。

Conclusion: 流水线应在模型依赖工具保证之前先验证其真实性；相关研究应采用方向感知的方式测量模型对工具证据的顺从程度；单纯的提示指令不足以抑制盲目信任。

Abstract: Pipelines that combine large language models (LLMs) with static analysis let the model consume the analyser's output as evidence, but whether the model distinguishes true from false evidence, and whether its scrutiny depends on what the evidence claims, has not been measured. We built an intraprocedural analyser for C/C++ that produces proof obligations and audited vulnerable and patched functions from PrimeVul under six evidence conditions that differ only in the notes, including fabricated assurances that a missing check exists and fabricated alarms that an added check is missing. The study covers 916 pairs in four disjoint sets, eleven model configurations from four vendors and 16,572 audits; no model is trained or fine-tuned. True evidence barely improved accuracy (+0.02). False evidence acted asymmetrically: relative to true notes about the same operations, false assurances lowered the detection of real vulnerabilities by up to 19 points, whereas false alarms never significantly lowered accuracy and were disputed far more often. An internally registered replication on seven new models confirmed that false assurances move verdicts towards their claim more than false alarms do (mean asymmetry +0.16, 95% CI +0.10 to +0.24). Instructions in the audit prompt did not reliably reduce deference; the best one failed to replicate. Verifying each claim in a separate call and showing the refuted claims raised accuracy under false assurances by 12 points in a pre-specified test (p=0.002) without loss under true evidence; for a second model the gain was smaller and not significant. Pipelines should therefore verify a tool's guarantees before the model relies on them, and studies should measure deference in a direction-aware way.

</details>


### [287] [Green Code or Costly Code? Evaluating the Energy Costs of Iterative LLM-Based Code Optimizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32891)
*Sadra Emami,Benjamin Weigell,Mohammad Reza Mousavi,Gunel Jahangirova,Hugo Araujo,Bernhard Bauer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文实证检验了"LLM性能优化必然降低能耗"这一假设，发现LLM自我改进流程生成的"更快"代码相比人类参考解能耗更高（+4%至+886%），且推理能耗巨大，性能与能效目标并不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界普遍假设LLM优化代码带来的执行加速会自然转化为更低的总体能耗，但这一假设缺乏实证检验。作者希望验证两方面：优化后的代码相对人类参考解是否真正节能，以及优化过程本身的能耗成本能否被后续执行节省所抵消。

Method: 在EffiBench全部1000道题上评估11个开源模型、4种解码配置；全程采集进程级硬件能耗测量；比较LLM生成代码与人类规范参考解的执行能耗，并计算优化能耗的盈亏平衡点。

Result: 推理能耗达0.79–8.68 MJ（约相当于250–2800次GPT-4.1查询）；限制最大输出token可最多降低69%能耗；44个配置中有42个生成的代码能耗高于人类参考解，开销为+4%至+886%，两个例外仅有边际增益，盈亏平衡需10^6–10^7次执行；MoE架构推理能耗显著低于同规模稠密模型且不损失正确性。

Conclusion: 性能优化与能效并非总是一致目标：LLM自我改进倾向于产出"追求更快但更耗能"的代码。架构选择（如MoE）比解码参数是更具影响力的可持续性杠杆，碳排放/能耗评估应纳入LLM代码生成流程。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to optimize code for performance. This practice often assumes that improvements in execution speed naturally translate into overall lower energy consumption. We challenge this assumption empirically by measuring whether performance-optimized code produced by LLM self-refinement pipelines is also energy-efficient relative to canonical human reference solutions, and whether the energy cost of the optimization process can be offset by subsequent execution-time savings. We evaluate 11 open-source models across four decoding configurations on all 1,000 problems of EffiBench, a state-of-the-art performance benchmark, while collecting per-process, hardware-level energy measurements throughout. Inference energy spans 0.79 to 8.68 MJ across model-variant pairs, which is roughly equivalent to the energy of 250 to 2800 GPT-4.1 queries. Limiting the maximum number of output tokens has the largest impact, reducing energy use by up to 69% compared to the default configuration. On the execution side, 42 of 44 configurations produce code that consumes more energy per problem than the canonical human reference, with overheads ranging from +4% to +886%. The two exceptions achieve only marginal gains, with break-even points on the order of 10^6 to 10^7 executions. Mixture-of-Experts architectures consume substantially less inference energy than equivalently-sized dense models without sacrificing correctness, making architectural choice a more consequential sustainability lever than decoding parameters. Performance optimization and energy efficiency are not always aligned goals: LLM self-refinement consistently produces faster-seeking but energy-heavier code relative to what a human engineer would write. All artifacts are available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23598170.

</details>


### [288] [Runtime BiDi Structural Verification Catches Right-to-Left Layout Defects that Accessibility, Actionability, and Logical-Property Linting Miss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32934)
*Karan Yadav*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出运行时结构不变式（zero-golden 计算样式/几何模式 + 双向保守差分模式），在无头浏览器中检测 RTL 布局镜像缺陷，基准召回 13/14 且零误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用物理方向性 CSS（margin-left、border-left、text-align:left）编写的界面在 dir="rtl" 下渲染时未被镜像，会产生 RTL 布局缺陷；axe-core、Playwright 与 Stylelint 现有逻辑属性规则均无法定位此类问题。

Method: 两种互补的运行时结构不变式：1) zero-golden 模式，仅基于计算样式与几何的结构不变量，无需基准；2) 双向（bidi）保守差分模式，比较 LTR 与 RTL 渲染差异，对双向文本保持保守。均在无头浏览器中执行。

Result: 在 14 个从 Bootstrap 与 Material Components Web 修复提交重建的真实 RTL 缺陷基准上召回 13/14（2 个由 zero-golden、11 个由差分模式），已修补版本无误报；真实 bootstrap.rtl.css 上 2 中检出 1；16 个负例 LTR 孤岛与 43 个未修改真实组件（四个设计系统、三种 RTL 架构）上误报为 0（95% 上界 ≤7%）；并在 Bulma 1.0.4 中发现真实缺陷。对照：Stylelint 核心规则在 Bootstrap LTR 与正确 RTL 构建上产生相同的 1744 条警告，伪本地化检测不到任何镜像缺陷，朴素像素镜像差分仅 F1 0.87 且需在测试集上调阈值。

Conclusion: 两种模式互补：zero-golden 是装饰/样式不对称类缺陷的唯一检测器（5/6），但会误报正确的逻辑 border-inline-end；差分模式对该类无效，却从不误报且在块级 inset/margin 类上经验上精确。本文仅限检测，修复为未来工作。

Abstract: Bidirectional (right-to-left, RTL) layout defects arise when interfaces authored with physical directional CSS (margin-left, border-left, text-align:left) are rendered under dir="rtl" without being mirrored. Accessibility engines (axe-core), actionability engines (Playwright), and even Stylelint's current core logical-property rule fail to localise these defects. We present runtime structural invariants -- a zero-golden mode over computed style and geometry, and a bidi-conservative differential mode -- that catch them in a headless browser. On a held-out benchmark of 14 real RTL bugs reconstructed from dated fix commits in Bootstrap and Material Components Web, the detector recovers 13/14 (2 via zero-golden, 11 via the differential mode), with no false alarm on any patched version; on the actual committed historical bootstrap.rtl.css it catches 1 of 2 attempted. It reports 0 false positives on 16 negative LTR-island cases and, in both modes, on 43 unmodified real components across four design systems and three RTL architectures (rule-of-three 95% upper bound <= 7%), and it surfaced a real directional defect in the current Bulma 1.0.4. By contrast, Stylelint's core rule emits an identical 1744 warnings on Bootstrap's LTR and correct-RTL builds, pseudo-localisation detects none of the mirroring bugs, and a naive pixel mirror-and-diff reaches only F1 0.87 with a test-set-tuned threshold. The two modes are complementary: zero-golden is the sole detector of the decoration/styling-asymmetry class (5/6) but false-flags the correct logical border-inline-end, while the differential detects none of that class yet never false-alarms and is empirically exact on the block-level inset/margin class. We scope this paper to detection; repair is future work.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [289] [Randomization to Balance Performance under Nominal and Compromised Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31957)
*Colton Hill,Brandon Collins,Philip N. Brown*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体系统在正常通信与通信受损两种情形下能否用统一的随机化程度兼顾性能，证明存在一个随机化水平使正常效率接近最优、受损效率严格为正，但该保证随可选资源数量增加而减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 博弈论方法在通信正常时可提供系统级性能保证，但依赖通信的方法在通信被破坏时不鲁棒；随机决策虽能在通信受损时避免极差结果，却在通信恢复后无法带来性能提升。因此尚不清楚是否存在单一方法能同时兼顾两种通信情形。

Method: 作者设想由系统操作者告知智能体应如何随机化其动作选择，以在多智能体资源分配等场景中平衡正常通信与通信受损下的性能，并对随机化水平与效率之间的关系进行理论分析。

Result: 对任意场景（如某多智能体资源分配实例），均存在一个随机化水平使得正常通信下的效率接近最优，同时通信受损下的效率严格为正；但随智能体需在越来越多的资源间做选择，该保证会减弱。

Conclusion: 研究为将随机化作为跨通信情形实现鲁棒性能的工具提供了初步基础。

Abstract: It is well-known that under nominal communication, where agents are able to share information with one another, game-theoretic methods provide system-level performance guarantees in multiagent cyber-physical human systems such as distributed resource allocation. However, approaches that depend on communication are frequently not robust when communication is compromised. While randomized decision-making can prevent arbitrarily poor outcomes under compromised communication, these performance guarantees often do not improve when communication abilities are restored. Thus, it remains unclear whether a single approach can perform well under nominal communication and remain robust under compromised communication. In this paper, we study whether a system operator can inform agents about how much they should randomize their choice when selecting actions in order to balance performance across both settings. We show that for any scenario (e.g., a multiagent resource allocation instance), there exists a level of randomization where nominal efficiency is near-optimal while compromised efficiency is strictly positive. We then show that this guarantee weakens as agents must choose between increasingly many resources. These results provide an initial step toward understanding randomization as a tool for robust performance across communication settings.

</details>


### [290] [Differentiable Combinatorial Auction Design: Optimizing Revenue-Welfare-Fairness Frontiers With Graph Attention Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31977)
*Mai Pham,Vikrant Vaze,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 提出可微分、约束感知的组合拍卖设计框架，通过构造式组合约束保证分数可行性，可端到端学习近似策略防欺诈机制；在合成基准与机场时刻剥离案例中，收入较VCG提升73.5%和180%，并给出收入-福利-公平Pareto前沿作为监管决策支持工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共基础设施（机场时刻、能源资源）分配存在相互依赖资源与复杂侧约束，VCG虽保证最大福利，但无法最大化收入，也难以处理多目标权衡，因此需要可学习、可权衡的组合拍卖机制设计方法。

Method: 提出可微分、约束感知的组合拍卖设计框架，实现分数可行且近似策略防欺诈机制的端到端学习；核心是构造式组合约束执行技术，避免惩罚项近似；提出图结构CAGraph捕捉物品与投标人间依赖，并设CANet、CAFormer基线；另用匹配追踪式分解将分数分配映射为可行离散分配，并评估离散化差距。

Result: 在合成基准和机场时刻剥离案例中，所学机制期望收入分别比VCG最高提升73.5%与180%，经验遗憾较小；并可视化收入、福利、公平的Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: 该框架为公共基础设施拍卖提供可端到端学习、构造式可行的组合拍卖设计方法，兼顾效率、收益与公平，并可辅助监管者在盈利性、效率与公平间做决策权衡。

Abstract: Designing optimal auctions for allocating public infrastructure-such as airport slots and energy resources-is challenging due to interdependent resources and complex side-constraints. While the Vickrey-Clarke-Groves (VCG) auction guarantees maximum welfare, it cannot maximize revenue or accommodate the multi-objective trade-offs inherent in public infrastructure systems. We propose a differentiable, constraint-aware framework for combinatorial auction design that enables end-to-end learning of fractionally feasible, approximately strategy-proof mechanisms. Our primary contribution is a combinatorial constraint enforcement technique that ensures fractional feasibility by construction, bypassing the need for penalty-based approximations. We introduce CAGraph, a novel graph-based architecture designed to capture dependencies between items and bidders, alongside CANet and CAFormer as deep learning baselines, for combinatorial settings. We also provide a Matching Pursuit-inspired decomposition that maps fractional allocations to feasible discrete allocations and empirically evaluates the resulting discretization gap. In synthetic benchmarks and an airport slot-divestiture case study, respectively, the learned mechanisms achieve up to 73.5% and 180% higher expected revenue than VCG, under small empirical regret. Finally, we visualize the Pareto frontiers for revenue, welfare, and fairness, providing regulators with a decision-support tool to design infrastructure auctions that balance profitability, efficiency and equity.

</details>


### [291] [Capacity Modification for Matching with Couples: An Integer Programming and Computational Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32793)
*Zexiao Wu,Genjiu Xu,Péter Biró*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 该论文研究带夫妻的医院/住院医师问题中的容量修改，联合决定医院容量与稳定匹配，提出Exact与Cutoff两种整数规划建模，并从理论上证明小幅度容量调整即可恢复稳定匹配的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带夫妻的医院/住院医师问题中稳定匹配可能不存在，实践中需通过调整医院容量来恢复稳定性，但容量与匹配需共同决定，缺乏系统的建模与理论刻画。

Method: 构建Exact与Cutoff两种整数规划模型；假设每对夫妻要么两人均匹配要么均不匹配；分析任意严格偏好下每院容量至多改2、总容量至多增1的界；在次响应(sub-responsive)与次完备(sub-complete)夫妻偏好下分析每院至多加1的界；开展大规模计算实验。

Result: 任意严格偏好下，容量每院改动至多2、总增至多1即可保证稳定匹配存在且界紧；次响应/次完备偏好下每院至多加1、总增加量不超过夫妻数且界紧；实验中Exact在固定容量下略快，Cutoff在最小修复实验中显著更优，3万医生规模下完成率更高；96,000个市场中，小容量改动总能恢复存在性。

Conclusion: 小幅容量修改足以保证带夫妻医院/住院医师问题稳定匹配的存在性，且所提整数规划模型在大规模市场中高效可行，为实际匹配机制设计提供理论与计算支持。

Abstract: We study capacity modification in the Hospitals/Residents problem with Couples, in which hospital capacities and a stable matching are jointly determined. Exact and Cutoff integer programming formulations are developed for the resulting problem. We assume that, for each couple, either both members are matched or neither is. For arbitrary strict preferences, every instance admits a stable matching after changing each hospital capacity by at most two. Total capacity increases by at most one, and this bound is tight. Under sub-responsive and sub-complete couple preferences, a stable matching exists after adding at most one position to each hospital. The total number of additions is at most the number of couples, and this bound is also tight. Computational experiments show that Exact formulations are modestly faster at fixed capacities, whereas Cutoff formulations perform substantially better in the minimum repair experiments and attain higher completion rates in markets with up to 30,000 doctors. Across 96,000 synthetic and reconstructed markets, small capacity changes restore existence whenever no stable matching exists at the original capacities.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [292] [ConsMSA: Semantic Distribution Consistency Learning for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31731)
*Pan Wang,Lipeng Ke,Huajun Ying,Pritish Mohapatra,Rohan Sarkar,Suresh Lakhani,Sankar Venkataraman,Jingtong Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConsMSA提出跨模态与模态内语义分布一致性评分(I²CS)，将其用于正则化、token重加权与冗余剪枝，实现鲁棒且高效的多模态情感分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始多模态序列存在模态内与跨模态的语义冗余和冲突，影响鲁棒理解并增加计算开销。

Method: 将多模态token特征投影到共享情感空间计算I²CS，结合预测相关性得到一致性感知重要性信号，用于：(i)训练时对齐潜分布的正则项；(ii)导出语义感知权重自适应重加权token；(iii)作为剪枝冗余/冲突token的准则。

Result: 在CMU-MOSI、CMU-MOSEI、CH-SIMS上达到SOTA；激进token压缩下仍鲁棒，仅保留10% token即可获得可比精度。

Conclusion: 语义分布一致性可作为兼顾预测鲁棒性与计算效率的有效基础。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis (MSA) aims to predict human sentiments by integrating signals from different modalities such as text, video, and audio. However, raw multimodal sequences often suffer from semantic inconsistency--exhibiting redundancy or conflicts within and across modalities--which hinders robust understanding and increases computational cost. To this end, we introduce ConsMSA, which explicitly formalizes semantic distribution consistency across both $\textit{intra}$- and $\textit{inter}$-modality, providing a consistency-aware mechanism for robust and efficient multimodal sentiment prediction. Specifically, ConsMSA projects multimodal token features into a shared sentiment space to compute an Intra- and Inter-modality Consistency Score ($I^2CS$). By coupling this score with predictive relevance, we formulate consistency-aware importance signals that are utilized: (i) as a consistency regularizer to align latent distributions during training, (ii) to derive semantic-aware weights for adaptive multimodal token reweighting, and (iii) as a practical criterion to prune redundant or conflicting tokens. Extensive experiments on CMU-MOSI, CMU-MOSEI, and CH-SIMS demonstrate that ConsMSA achieves state-of-the-art performance while remaining robust under aggressive token compression--retaining only 10% of tokens yields comparable accuracy. These results establish semantic distribution consistency as a promising foundation for synergizing predictive robustness with computational efficiency.

</details>


### [293] [Collaborative In-Context Learning for Indoor Radio Map Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31702)
*Yuanzhe Peng,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文首次将室内无线电地图估计(RME)建模为协作式上下文学习(ICL)问题，提出Poly-Ripple框架：多个客户端用大规模自回归视觉模型在无需参数更新下进行ICL，通过涟漪序自回归建模捕捉传播因果结构，并用重要性感知聚合利用跨客户端的异构上下文知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内RME方法依赖有监督学习，需要昂贵的标注数据和针对特定任务的模型训练；同时室内环境中建筑布局等隐私敏感信息难以集中化处理。

Method: 将室内RME形式化为协作ICL问题；提出Poly-Ripple，各客户端基于私有示范提示在LAVM上做无更新的ICL并预测同一场景查询；采用涟漪序(自发射机向外)自回归建模以契合无线传播因果结构；引入重要性感知聚合以在上下文长度约束下融合各客户端的异构上下文知识；并给出理论分析。

Result: 在公开室内RME基准上的大量实验表明，Poly-Ripple持续优于代表性ICL基线，且结果与理论分析一致。

Conclusion: Poly-Ripple为隐私约束下的室内RME提供了免训练、协作式的新范式，验证了涟漪序自回归建模与重要性感知聚合的有效性。

Abstract: Indoor radio map estimation (RME) is essential for wireless applications such as coverage planning, localization, and network deployment. However, most existing methods follow a supervised learning paradigm that requires costly labeled data and task-specific model training. Moreover, indoor environments involve privacy-sensitive information, such as building layouts and structures, that often cannot be centralized. To address these challenges, we make the first attempt to formulate indoor RME as a collaborative in-context learning (ICL) problem. We propose Poly-Ripple, a framework in which multiple clients perform ICL using large autoregressive vision models (LAVMs) without model updates. Each client is prompted by its private demonstrations and predicts the radio map for the same scene query. To capture the causal structure of wireless propagation outward from the transmitter (Tx), we adopt ripple-ordered autoregressive modeling. We further introduce importance-aware aggregation to exploit heterogeneous contextual knowledge distributed across clients under the context length constraint. We provide theoretical analysis and conduct extensive experiments on a public indoor RME benchmark. The experimental results show that Poly-Ripple consistently outperforms representative ICL baselines, while aligning with our theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [294] [The Path to NDE 4.0: A Review of Algorithms, Techniques, and Strategies for Autonomous Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31704)
*Ashiqur Rahmana,Venkata Devesh Reddy Seethi,Zachary Kral,Austin Yunker,Rajkumar Kettimuthu,Hamed Alhoori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述无损检测(NDI)领域现状，从计算算法、物理技术、工业应用、研究目标四个视角梳理，指出该领域正从人工模拟判读转向以数据为中心的NDE 4.0范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NDI是现代质量保证的基石，对保障基础设施与制件安全可靠至关重要。需要系统梳理其快速演进的技术格局，识别阻碍广泛落地的瓶颈与研究缺口，为后续研究提供战略指引。

Method: 文献综述方法：综合广泛学术文献，通过四个维度(计算算法、物理技术、工业应用、研究目标)对NDI现状进行归纳分析。

Result: 超声与视觉检测主导物理技术；缺陷自动识别正显著转向深度学习与生成模型。同时存在两大关键瓶颈：标准化开放数据集稀缺，以及AI模型的"黑箱"特性阻碍推广。

Conclusion: 未来NDI研究应聚焦可解释AI(XAI)、标准化数字孪生协议，并推动智能自主的检测数据与先进传感技术融合，融入资产生命周期管理，推进NDE 4.0落地。

Abstract: Non-Destructive Inspection (NDI) is a cornerstone of modern quality assurance, critical for ensuring the safety and reliability of infrastructure and manufactured components without causing damage. This paper presents a comprehensive review of the current NDI landscape, analyzing the field through four distinct lenses, namely, computational algorithms, physical techniques, industrial applications, and research objectives. We synthesize a broad range of academic literature to illustrate the rapid evolution from manual, analog interpretation to a digitized, data-centric paradigm known as NDE 4.0. Our analysis highlights the dominance of ultrasound and visual inspection as primary physical techniques, while revealing a significant shift toward deep learning and generative models for automated defect recognition. Despite these technological strides, the industry faces critical bottlenecks, notably the scarcity of standardized open-access datasets and the "black box" nature of AI models, which hinders widespread adoption. By charting these developments and identifying key research gaps, such as the need for explainable AI (XAI) and standardized digital twin protocols, this review offers a strategic roadmap for future research. It concludes that the future of NDI research should focus on the successful integration of intelligent, autonomous fusion of inspection data with advanced sensor technologies into asset lifecycle management.

</details>


### [295] [Does LeWorldModel Preserve Local Action Geometry?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31716)
*Vasil Vasilev,Siddharth Nayak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文检验预训练 LeWorldModel (LeWM) 能否重现小动作变化的效果，用 Gram 矩阵在三种扰动尺度上测量动作敏感性，发现最敏感动作方向与模拟器存在约 36°/42° 偏差，且敏感性高度依赖尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型常被用于控制与规划，但其对细小动作变化的响应是否与真实环境一致尚不明确，需要量化评估其动作敏感性几何。

Method: 在 Reacher 与 OGBench Cube 上采集近千个状态，用匹配的模拟器与模型响应构造 Gram 矩阵，在三种扰动尺度（最小、10 倍等）下比较动作敏感性；并对比像素/嵌入几何、物理状态几何，以及在留出方向上与同迹各向同性基准的嵌入响应误差。

Result: 最小尺度下最敏感方向中位夹角约 36°(Reacher) 与 42°(Cube)；扰动放大 10 倍后归一化 Gram 差异中位下降约 70% 与 47%；像素与嵌入几何跨尺度变化大于物理状态几何；留出方向上环境 Gram 的嵌入响应误差中位为 17%/28%，优于各向同性基准的 26%/38%；LeWM 在该尺度误差更高。

Conclusion: 结果支持对动作敏感性进行"尺度特定"的评估；动作敏感性对控制性能的影响仍是开放问题。

Abstract: We examine how closely pretrained LeWorldModel (LeWM) reproduces the effects of small action changes. Using matched simulator and model responses, we measure action sensitivity with Gram matrices at three perturbation scales. Across nearly a thousand states from Reacher and OGBench Cube, the most sensitive action directions differ by median angles of about $36^\circ$ and $42^\circ$, respectively, at the smallest scale. Increasing the perturbation tenfold reduces median normalized Gram discrepancy by roughly 70\% on Reacher and 47\% on Cube. Geometry measured in pixels and embeddings varies more across scales than the chosen physical-state geometry. On held-out directions, environment Grams achieve median embedding response errors of 17\% and 28\%, compared with 26\% and 38\% for an isotropic reference with the same trace. LeWM's errors are higher at this scale. These findings support scale-specific evaluation of action sensitivity; its effect on control remains an open question.

</details>


### [296] [Towards Adversarially Robust Multigraph Neural Networks for Financial Crime Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31718)
*Dennis Heijmans,H. Çağrı Bilgi,Rui Wang,Kubilay Atasu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对多重图神经网络（MultiGNN）的可扩展结构攻击与对抗训练框架，基于节点-边关联矩阵优化结构扰动，并引入基于环的正则化约束攻击合理性。实验表明学习型攻击显著降低AML检测性能，而对抗训练能有效提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融交易网络天然表现为多重图（同一账户对之间的多笔交易为不同边），现有MultiGNN在反洗钱任务上表现优异，但其对篡改交易结构以规避检测的对抗攻击的鲁棒性尚未被研究。

Method: 构建可扩展的结构攻击框架，将投影梯度下降（PGD）与投影随机块坐标下降（PRBCD）推广至节点-边关联矩阵上的结构扰动优化；并引入基于环的正则项，惩罚全局有向2-环和3-环计数变化，作为攻击合理性的结构度量；同时开展对抗训练。

Result: 在合成模式检测和真实AML数据集上，学习型攻击在降低检测性能（PR-AUC）方面显著优于不可学习的扰动基线；对抗训练在这些攻击下持续提升模型鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MultiGNN对学习型结构攻击具有脆弱性，对抗训练是一种有效的防御手段，为多重图神经网络的鲁棒反洗钱检测提供了框架与代码支持。

Abstract: Financial transaction networks are naturally represented as multigraphs, where repeated transactions between the same pair of accounts are modeled as distinct edges. Recently proposed multigraph neural network architectures leverage this structure explicitly to achieve a strong performance on anti-money laundering (AML) tasks. However, their robustness to adversaries that manipulate transaction structures to evade detection remains largely unexplored. We present the first scalable framework for structural attacks and adversarial training of multigraph neural networks using node-edge incidence matrices, adapting projected gradient descent and projected randomized block coordinate descent methods to optimize structural perturbations over such matrices. We further introduce a cycle-based regularizer that penalizes changes in the global counts of directed 2- and 3-cycles, providing a structural measure of attack plausibility. Experiments on synthetic pattern-detection and realistic AML datasets show that our learned attacks substantially outperform non-learnable perturbation baselines in reducing detection performance (PR-AUC), while adversarial training consistently improves robustness against these attacks. These results demonstrate that multigraph neural networks are vulnerable to learned structural attacks and that adversarial training provides an effective defense. Our code is available at https://github.com/denheijmans/robust_multignn.

</details>


### [297] [Searching with Sparse Histories: Cross-Task Architecture Search from Incomplete Search Logs and Zero-Cost Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32179)
*Yuefeng Xu,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种风险鲁棒策略，通过冠军初始化与联合成对排序器预训练，将稀疏源日志与零成本代理结合，用于跨任务NAS迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAS代价高昂，跨任务迁移可复用历史知识降低成本，但现有方法面临搜索历史稀疏与任务特异迁移失效的问题。

Method: 结合稀疏源日志与零成本代理，采用基于冠军（champion）的初始化与联合成对排序器（pairwise-ranker）预训练的风险鲁棒策略。

Result: 在TransNAS-Bench-101上显著缓解最坏情况失败：Micro autoencoding的最差10%排名由290降至43，Macro room layout由465降至45；在13个cell-space目标中，10个目标上均值与最坏情况均优于纯代理排序器，同时保持有竞争力的平均性能。

Conclusion: 该风险鲁棒迁移策略有效应对稀疏历史与迁移失败，在多数目标上兼顾平均与最坏情况表现，为低成本NAS迁移提供可行方案。

Abstract: Neural architecture search (NAS) is expensive, but cross-task transfer can reduce costs by reusing knowledge from previous tasks. However, existing methods struggle with sparse search histories and task-specific transfer failures. We propose a method, a risk-robust strategy that combines sparse source logs with zero-cost proxies through champion-based initialization and joint pairwise-ranker pretraining. On TransNAS-Bench-101, the proposal mitigates severe worst-case failures, reducing worst-10% ranks from 290 to 43 for Micro autoencoding and from 465 to 45 for Macro room layout. Across 13 cell-space targets, it outperforms a proxy-only ranker on 10 targets in both mean and worst-case performance, while maintaining competitive average performance.

</details>


### [298] [End-to-End Differentiable Diffusion Conditioning for Physics-Informed Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31721)
*Ricardo Luna Gutierrez,Vineet Gundecha,Rahman Ejaz,Varchas Gopalaswamy,Riccardo Betti,Sahand Ghorbanpour,Aarne Lees,Soumyendu Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可微扩散条件化(D2C)，利用确定性采样使逆去噪链对条件信号可微，将冻结的条件扩散模型变为端到端梯度优化器，用于离线MBO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维物理系统优化面临昂贵评估与复杂约束；现有离线MBO方法存在保守性、无约束代理模型利用过度、或仅是静态推理采样器，无法针对精确物理目标细化候选解。

Method: 基于确定性采样使逆扩散链对条件可微的特性，D2C在测试时通过冻结代理目标和物理约束惩罚的梯度（经逆链反传）直接优化条件变量，并在proposer-evaluator组合框架中实现。

Result: 在惯性约束聚变激光脉冲优化、可持续数据中心负载调度两个真实任务上取得最强结果，并在四个Design-Bench任务上取得最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: 将冻结的条件扩散模型转化为可微的端到端优化器，可有效克服离线MBO的保守性与静态采样局限，在真实物理与调度任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Optimizing high-dimensional physical systems under expensive evaluations and complex constraints is a central challenge in science and engineering. In offline model-based optimization (MBO), methods must improve designs using only a fixed dataset and no test-time access to the true objective. However, existing methods typically suffer from conservatism, unconstrained surrogate exploitation, or function as static inference-time samplers that cannot refine candidates against precise physical objectives. In this paper, we propose Differentiable Diffusion Conditioning (D2C) for offline MBO, which leverages the fact that deterministic sampling makes the reverse denoising chain differentiable with respect to the conditioning signal. D2C turns a frozen conditional diffusion model into an end-to-end gradient-based optimizer by directly optimizing conditioning variables at test time via gradients from frozen surrogate objectives and physics-informed penalties backpropagated through the reverse chain. We implement this idea in a composed proposer-evaluator framework and evaluate it on laser pulse optimization for inertial confinement fusion, sustainable data-center workload scheduling, and four Design-Bench tasks. Our method achieves the strongest results on the two real-world tasks and the best average rank across four Design-Bench tasks.

</details>


### [299] [Autoregressive Multimodal Anomaly Detection with Large Language Models for Microservice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31722)
*Yixiang Tang,Fagui Liu,Guoxiang Zhong,Xi Yao,C. L. Philip Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLMMAD——基于大语言模型的自回归多模态异常检测框架，通过统一序列建模跨模态关系并保留模态特异性、用预测驱动目标消除身份捷径，在三个真实数据集上F1分别提升3.1%、1.4%、0.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统可靠性依赖有效异常检测，但现有方法有两局限：(i) 偏重跨模态融合而忽视模态特有特征；(ii) 特征重建中的“身份捷径”问题（模型直接复制输入）。

Method: 将多模态数据嵌入并重组为统一序列交由LLM处理以建模模态间关系，同时用模态专属重建头保留模态特有信息；采用预测驱动的自回归目标，让模型预测未来状态而非复制当前输入，从而消除身份捷径。

Result: 在三个真实数据集上实验，F1分数分别提升3.1%、1.4%、0.9%，持续优于SOTA方法。

Conclusion: LLMMAD兼顾跨模态融合与模态特异性，并通过预测式目标解决身份捷径，为微服务监控提供更可靠的异常检测方案。

Abstract: Microservice systems have become a dominant architecture for large-scale cloud applications, enabling rapid deployment and elastic scaling. However, ensuring the reliability and availability of microservice systems critically relies on effective anomaly detection, as failures and performance anomalies can quickly propagate across services and severely impact end-to-end system behavior. Existing multimodal anomaly detection approaches for microservice systems often struggle with two fundamental limitations: (i) the neglect of modality-specific characteristics in favor of cross-modality fusion, and (ii) the "identity-shortcut" problem that affects the feature reconstruction process. To address these issues, we propose LLMMAD, an autoregressive multimodal anomaly detection framework for microservice systems based on Large Language Models (LLMs). Initially, to reconcile cross-modality fusion with modality-specific preservation, LLMMAD embeds and recombines multimodal data into a unified sequence for LLM processing to model inter-modality relationships, while employing modality-wise reconstruction heads to retain modality-specific information. Subsequently, to eliminate the identity-shortcut, we implement a prediction-driven autoregressive objective, under which the model predicts future states rather than copies current inputs. Finally, we conduct extensive experiments on three real-world datasets. The results demonstrate that LLMMAD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving F1-score improvements of 3.1%, 1.4%, and 0.9%, respectively, thereby providing a more reliable solution for microservice monitoring.

</details>


### [300] [Cognitively Constrained Partners for Zero-Shot Coordination in Real-Time Partitioned Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31726)
*Ash Simmons,George De Ath,Richard M. Everson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"认知受限伙伴"训练框架:用基于人类生理文献的反应延迟与感知限制等程序化约束生成多样化模拟伙伴,使智能体在与不可预测人类协作时表现更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协作需要能配合不可预测人类的智能体,但真实人类反馈强化学习与大规模行为数据成本高昂,纯模拟训练又易过拟合人工约定,难以实现零样本协作。

Method: 伙伴多样性来自基于人类生理学文献的程序化约束(反应延迟、感知局限),而非奖励塑形或历史训练检查点;在改造版 Lunar Lander 环境中训练,并设计人类被试研究以便排序结果与 cross-play 直接对比。

Result: 初步证据显示此类伙伴比无约束智能体更像真人;对其训练的智能体达到最强 cross-play 基线水平,且是唯一性能不依赖训练伙伴生成方式的方案。

Conclusion: 程序化认知约束是构建类人伙伴的低成本可行途径;cross-play 作为评估指标对真实人机协作的预测能力仍需人类实验验证。

Abstract: Effective human-machine teaming requires agents that coordinate with unpredictable human partners. Reinforcement learning from live human feedback or large behavioural datasets is expensive, while purely simulated training overfits to artificial conventions and fails at Zero-Shot Coordination with real people. We propose a framework for training teaming agents against Cognitively Constrained Partners: simulated partners whose diversity emerges from procedural constraints grounded in human physiological literature, specifically reaction delays and perceptual limitations, rather than from reward shaping or historical training checkpoints. In a modified Lunar Lander environment, we report preliminary evidence that these partners play more like real people than unconstrained agents do, and that agents trained against them match the strongest cross-play baselines while being the only ones whose performance does not depend on how their training partners were generated. We also describe a human-participant study designed so that its rankings can be compared directly against cross-play, testing how well that standard evaluation predicts real human-agent teaming.

</details>


### [301] [When and How Does Muon Precondition? The Role of Structured Gradient Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31728)
*Xiaoran Chen,Binghui Li,Liwei Wang,Pinyan Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从梯度噪声几何角度解释 Muon 优化器的加速来源：在经典各向同性噪声下 Muon 仅是学习率重标定，而在异质任务频率的结构化噪声下，Muon 可把任务频率依赖从线性降为平方根，平衡常见与稀有任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 在大语言模型训练中被广泛使用，但其理论基础薄弱，尤其不清楚其预条件（preconditioning）优势究竟来自何种梯度噪声结构。

Method: 先理论+实证分析各向同性噪声下的 Muon；再构建联想记忆中小批量采样导致的结构化噪声模型（含异质任务频率），推导两任务精确平衡阈值并推广到多任务；分析 SignSGD 的旋转诱导负迁移；结合受控数值实验和初步 LLM 预训练验证。

Result: 各向同性噪声下 Muon 无加速、只等价于学习率缩放；结构化噪声下得到两任务精确平衡阈值及多任务扩展，显示任务覆盖率与梯度可靠性共同影响学习；Muon 将频率依赖由线性降为平方根；识别出 SignSGD 的旋转负迁移而 Muon 可避免；动量可降低采样噪声并跟踪变化梯度。

Conclusion: Muon 的优势依赖于梯度噪声的各向异性结构；其本质作用是软化任务频率依赖、改善稀有任务学习，并规避 SignSGD 的负迁移；快速-慢速动量在 LLM 预训练中具有实践潜力。

Abstract: Muon has become a popular optimizer for large language model training, yet its theoretical foundations remain poorly understood. In this work, we study how gradient noise geometry shapes Muon's preconditioning benefits. We first show, theoretically and empirically, that under the classical isotropic noise assumption, Muon offers no acceleration beyond a rescaling of the learning rate. This motivates us to investigate structured noise arising from mini-batch sampling in associative memory with heterogeneous task frequencies. We establish an exact balancing threshold for two tasks and extend the analysis to multiple tasks, revealing how task coverage and gradient reliability jointly shape learning. Our analysis shows that Muon can soften the dependence on task frequency from linear to square-root, helping balance the learning of frequent and rare tasks. We further identify rotation-induced negative transfer in SignSGD that Muon avoids, and explain how momentum reduces sampling noise while tracking changing gradients. Controlled numerical experiments validate these mechanisms, and a preliminary LLM pretraining study illustrates the practical promise of fast-slow momentum.

</details>


### [302] [Learning to Be Monitored: Causal Bounds and Intervention-Aware Post-Training Recipes for Chain-of-Thought Monitorability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31733)
*Qiang Fu,Xinyue Li,Andre Wibisono,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出常用的CoT监控指标TPR_mc会高估监控器对"由干预真正导致的目标行为"的检测率，将其刻画为因果检测率的上界并给出互补下界，形成更紧的评估框架；同时构建控制-干预配对数据集并设计三种训练方法提升CoT可监控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于干预的CoT监控评估（TPR_mc）只考察监控器能否从CoT中识别干预的作用，但干预下观测到的目标行为未必由干预引起，因此该指标可能高估监控器检测"真正由干预导致的目标行为"的频率，需要更严谨的因果评估，并探索模型能否被训练得更易监控。

Method: 1) 理论分析：证明TPR_mc是因果检测率的上界，推导互补下界，构建更紧的评估框架；2) 构建跨多种干预类型的控制-干预配对训练数据集；3) 基于off-policy自蒸馏、强化学习、on-policy自蒸馏设计三种干预特定训练方法，训练各基座模型的变体。

Result: 在多种模型-监控器配置下，三种训练方法在所提出的扩展评估框架下普遍优于对应基座模型，提升了CoT可监控性。

Conclusion: TPR_mc需用因果上/下界加以校正，本文框架提供了更紧的CoT可监控性评估；且LLM可通过针对性训练（自蒸馏与RL）变得更容易被监控。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring aims to detect potentially harmful behaviors in large language models (LLMs) from their CoTs. Intervention-based evaluations measure this property by asking whether a monitor can detect the role of an intervention from the CoT when the targeted behavior occurs, typically using minimal-criterion sensitivity ($\mathsf{TPR_{mc}}$) proposed by~\citep{guan2025monitoring}. However, a target behavior observed under intervention is not necessarily caused by it, and we show that this metric can overestimate how often a monitor detects target behaviors actually caused by the intervention. Specifically, we establish $\mathsf{TPR_{mc}}$ as an upper bound on this causal detection rate and derive a complementary lower bound, which provides a tighter evaluation framework for CoT monitorability. With this framework, we study whether LLMs can be trained to make their CoTs easier to monitor. We first construct a training dataset of paired control--intervention tasks spanning multiple intervention types. Using the dataset, we design and apply three intervention-specific training methods based on off-policy self-distillation, reinforcement learning, and on-policy self-distillation to train separate variants of each base model. Across various model-monitor configurations, all three methods generally improve CoT monitorability over the corresponding base models under our extended evaluation framework. Our code is available at https://github.com/xinyue-lxy/learning-to-be-monitored.

</details>


### [303] [Efficient Omni-modal Large Language Model Inference via Per-Query Modality Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31768)
*Shuqi Liu,Yuzhou Cao,Luke Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出按查询（query-wise）自适应的模态选择方法，在固定Omni-LLM的前提下决定每个查询提供哪些模态，以精度-成本优化为目标，用成本敏感学习实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Omni-LLM联合处理文本、音频、视频等多模态带来强理解与推理能力，但推理成本高；作者观察到不同查询从额外模态中获益程度不同，固定模态配置效率低下。

Method: 将模态选择形式化为精度-成本优化问题，并开发成本敏感学习方法来学习查询级模态选择策略，底层Omni-LLM保持不变。

Result: 在WorldSense上以Qwen2.5-Omni和Qwen3-Omni评估，采用输入token、延迟、金钱成本三种成本度量；查询级模态选择持续改善精度-成本权衡，甚至可超过全模态标准设置。Qwen2.5-Omni下仅约41%输入token、39%延迟、61%金钱成本即达到或超过全模态精度。

Conclusion: 查询级模态选择是提升Omni-LLM推理效率的有效且模型无关的途径，可显著降低token、延迟与费用而保持精度。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models (Omni-LLMs) can jointly process a broad range of modalities, such as text, audio, and video, enabling stronger multimodal understanding and reasoning capabilities, but often at substantial inference cost. We observe that the benefit of additional modalities varies across queries, indicating that a fixed modality configuration can be inefficient. Motivated by this, we study query-wise modality selection, which keeps the underlying Omni-LLM fixed while adaptively selecting which modalities to provide for each query. We formulate the problem as an accuracy-cost optimization problem and develop a cost-sensitive learning approach for modality selection. We evaluate our approach on WorldSense using Qwen2.5-Omni and Qwen3-Omni under three inference-cost measures: input tokens, latency, and monetary cost. Across both models, query-wise modality selection consistently improves the accuracy-cost trade-off and can even outperform the standard setting in which all available modalities are provided to the Omni-LLM. For Qwen2.5-Omni, our cost-sensitive approach matches or exceeds this full-modality accuracy while requiring only approximately 41% of the input tokens, 39% of the latency, and 61% of the monetary cost.

</details>


### [304] [Benchmarks Are Surprisingly Compaction-Friendly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31778)
*Jeanne Salle,Hardik Bhatnagar,Matthias Bethge,Sahar Abdelnabi,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了长程智能体任务中的上下文压缩（compaction）问题：固定OpenCode脚手架、实现5种压缩策略，在3个长程基准和3个前沿模型上评估，发现压缩阈值对1M上下文模型无影响，无方法优于默认自摘要，且压缩会静默丢失任务约束（反作弊规则约1/3）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体任务超出LLM上下文窗口，需通过压缩释放空间，但压缩本质有损，可能静默丢失任务约束、指令或早期推理依据。此前工作仅在玩具/合成轨迹上评估小模型，缺乏在前沿模型和真实长程任务上的系统研究。

Method: 固定OpenCode脚手架，在其中实现5种压缩策略；用3个前沿模型在3个长程基准（PaperBench、ProgramBench、PostTrainBench）上评估；分析压缩阈值影响、各方法对比、反作弊规则保留率随压缩次数变化，以及SWE-chat真实用户会话中的指令丢失情况。

Result: 1M上下文模型的任务表现不随压缩阈值明显变化；上下文最小的GPT-5.3-Codex在低阈值下PaperBench表现更差，PostTrainBench上较弱。无压缩方法优于默认自摘要；丢弃内容更多的方法对某些模型最多损失20分，对另一些模型无影响。ProgramBench上首次压缩即平均丢失约1/3反作弊规则，且随压缩次数单调上升；SWE-chat中关于"如何工作/不应做什么"的指令被丢失的频率约为"构建什么"规格的6倍。

Conclusion: 现有基准对压缩"友好"：目标固定且进度持久化在磁盘上，多数被丢弃内容可恢复，唯独那些不存在于其他地方的约束无法恢复。因此压缩的真正风险在于独特约束的静默丢失，需针对性设计保留机制和更严格的评估基准。

Abstract: Long horizon agentic tasks are now pushing LLM agents past their context window. To continue the task, the agent scaffold has to free up the context window, a process known as context compaction. Compaction is generally lossy by design: agents can silently lose task constraints, instructions, or their reasoning behind earlier decisions. Although compaction is a standard component of coding agent scaffolds, it has not been studied systematically with frontier models on realistic, long horizon agentic tasks. Previous work evaluate smaller open weight models on toy or synthetic trajectories. We fix the agent scaffold (OpenCode), implement five compaction strategies inside it, and evaluate three frontier models on three long horizon benchmarks (PaperBench, ProgramBench, PostTrainBench). For the two 1M context models, the compaction threshold does not measurably affect the task performance, whereas GPT-5.3-Codex, the model with the smallest context window, performs worse at lower thresholds on PaperBench and, less clearly, on PostTrainBench. No compaction method outperforms default self-summarization, and methods that discard more content cost some models up to 20 points while leaving other models the same. Compaction can, however, silently remove task constraints: on ProgramBench, the first compaction summary already drops about one third of the task's anticheating rules on average, and this rises monotonically with the number of compactions. On real user-agent sessions from SWE-chat, instructions about how the agent should work and what it should not do are dropped around 6 times more than specifications of what to build. We argue that current benchmarks are compaction-friendly: i.e., the objective is fixed and the agent's progress stays on disk, so most of what compaction discards can be recovered, except constraints that don't exist anywhere else.

</details>


### [305] [Not All Retained Clients Are Equal: Client Selection for Federated Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31786)
*Thanh Linh Nguyen,Quoc-Viet Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PDF：基于路径依赖的联邦遗忘客户端选择框架，利用训练中CLP加权历史挑选与目标弱耦合的保留客户端进行少量轮次重训练，实现高效遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦遗忘(FU)方法把遗忘当作事后的无状态操作，忽略了客户端影响的路径依赖性——训练期间（尤其关键学习期CLP）的贡献可能产生不成比例的持久影响。同时知识重叠与客户端间歇可用性使依赖全部保留客户端既低效又不可行。

Method: 提出PDF框架，基于"保留客户端对遗忘价值不等"的洞见：训练中维护轻量、目标无关、CLP加权的历史，记录各客户端累积更新方向与全局模型对齐度；遗忘请求到来时据此识别并排序"全局有益但与原目标弱耦合"的保留客户端，仅用top-k客户端做少量轮次的重训练式遗忘。

Result: 在FMNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100的IID与非IID设置下，PDF以显著更少保留客户端实现有效遗忘与良好保留效用：非IID CIFAR-10上遗忘准确率0%且MIA AUC接近重训练；CIFAR-100保留精度较最强FU基线最高提升6.91个百分点；相比Retrain通信减少约69%、计算减少57–61%。

Conclusion: 将客户端影响视为路径依赖并据此做客户端选择，可高效实现重训练级联邦遗忘，兼顾遗忘效果、保留效用与通信/计算效率。

Abstract: Federated unlearning (FU) aims to remove a designated target client's influence from a trained model without the prohibitive cost of retraining from scratch. Existing methods largely treat unlearning as a post-hoc, stateless operation, overlooking that client influence is path-dependent: contributions made during training, particularly during critical learning periods (CLPs), can have disproportionate and persistent effects. Moreover, knowledge overlap and intermittent client availability make relying on all retained clients inefficient and impractical. We introduce PDF, a path-dependent client-selection framework based on the insight that retained clients are not equally valuable for unlearning. During training, PDF maintains lightweight, target-agnostic, CLP-weighted histories summarizing each client's accumulated update direction and global-model alignment. Upon an unlearning request, these histories identify and rank retained clients that are globally informative yet weakly coupled to the target, enabling retraining-based unlearning with only the top-$k$ clients over a small number of rounds. Experiments on FMNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100 under IID and non-IID settings show that PDF achieves effective forgetting and strong retained utility with substantially fewer retained clients. In particular, PDF achieves $0\%$ forgetting accuracy with retraining-like MIA AUC on non-IID CIFAR-10, improves retained accuracy by up to $6.91$ percentage points over the strongest competing FU baseline on CIFAR-100, and reduces communication by approximately $69\%$ and computation by $57$--$61\%$ relative to Retrain.

</details>


### [306] [PaperScorer: How Predictable Is Paper Acceptance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31791)
*Sachin Konan,Jonathan Liu,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PaperScorer 系列模型仅凭论文文本或页面截图即可预测同行评审的接收/拒绝决定，视觉模型表现最佳（ICLR 68%、arXiv ML 77%），且置信度校准良好，但在中等评分论文上预测困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化研究多以基准分数评估，但研究价值难以归结为单一分数；人类通过同行评审判断整体贡献，但因噪声大、因素多而难以建模。作者想探究：仅给论文本身，能否直接预测其评审决定。

Method: 构建 PaperScorer 模型族，从论文提取文本或页面截图预测接收/拒绝；训练时去除作者身份与排版捷径，仅用最终决定作标签；对比视觉模型、纯文本模型与前沿推理模型。

Result: 视觉模型最具预测力（ICLR 68%、arXiv ML 77%），优于文本模型与前沿推理模型；页面图像保留图表、版式与呈现信息，信号稳健（小模型亦可、甚至压缩为单张图仍有效）；在最低与最高评分论文上最准，中等评分论文难以预测；置信度校准，最高置信预测也最准确。

Conclusion: PaperScorer 表明论文本身携带相当部分的决策信息，是迈向 AI 驱动研究评估的第一步。

Abstract: Automated research is largely evaluated by benchmark performance, but the value of research rarely reduces to a single score. Humans judge a work's holistic contribution through peer review, which is hard to model because it is noisy and depends on many factors. However, what if, given only the paper, we could predict its peer-review decision directly? We introduce PaperScorer, a family of models that predict a paper's accept or reject decision from its extracted text or page screenshots, training only on the decision after removing author identity and formatting shortcuts. Vision models are the most predictive (68% accuracy on ICLR papers and 77% on ML papers from arXiv), beating text-only and frontier reasoning models. Unlike plain text, page images retain figures, layout, and presentation. This signal is robust, surviving smaller models and even compressing the whole paper into a single image. But accurate predictions are not evenly spread. PaperScorer is strongest on the lowest and highest rated papers, while papers rated in the middle stay unpredictable for our models and frontier systems alike. Additionally, its confidence is calibrated, so its most confident predictions are also its most accurate. Overall, PaperScorer shows how much of the decision a paper carries on its own, marking a first step toward AI-driven research evaluation.

</details>


### [307] [CuraLoc: Repository-Level Fault Localization via Planned Code Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31809)
*Sangeeta Warrier,Anush Sankaran,Ryen White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CuraLoc将"检索后再规划"改为"先规划再做确定性检索"：用紧凑的符号描述让模型一次性选择起点符号、关系和编辑假设，再由确定性流程抓取源码做最终排序，在SWE-Bench上以远少得多的token实现更强的函数级定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bug报告只说"哪里出了问题"，很少指出"代码该改在哪里"。现有仓库级定位方法让语言模型迭代决定下一步检查哪些代码，导致反复调用模型、token开销巨大。作者想验证这些"检查决策"是否可以在取源码之前一次性规划好。

Method: CuraLoc分三步：(1) 先筛选候选文件，并用紧凑的行为与结构描述表示其中符号；(2) 模型做单次规划决策——同时选择起始符号、选择从每个符号出发要检查的代码关系、形成可能的编辑假设；(3) 确定性检索按请求收集源码，并结合这些假设做最终排序。这样把模型推理集中在"选择与比较代码"上，而非逐步指挥每一次检索。

Result: SWE-Bench Verified（GPT-5.2）：Func@1从LocAgent的53.8%提升到59.3%，记录token减少90.3%，并超过RepoSearcher（56.2%）和Agentless（39.6%）。SWE-Bench Lite：Func@5达83.8%，相对Agentless提升超15%。方法也支持不同骨干模型（GPT-5.2与GPT-6 Astra）均表现良好。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仓库级代码检查可以有效地被"预先规划"，不依赖模型迭代控制的导航，从而在精度与效率上同时获益。

Abstract: A bug report describes what went wrong, but rarely where the code should change. Existing repository-level localization methods often let language models iteratively decide what code to inspect next, requiring repeated model calls and substantial token usage. We investigate whether these inspection decisions can instead be planned before source retrieval. In this research, we introduce CuraLoc, which first selects candidate files and represents their symbols using compact behavioral and structural descriptions. In a single planning decision, the model jointly selects starting symbols, chooses code relationships to inspect from each, and forms hypotheses about likely edits. Deterministic retrieval collects the requested source for a final ranking informed by those hypotheses, concentrating model reasoning on choosing and comparing code rather than directing each retrieval step. We demonstrate the efficacy of our proposed approach on the SWE-Bench Lite and Verified datasets. On SWE Bench Verified with GPT-5.2, CuraLoc raises Func@1 from LocAgent's 53.8% to 59.3% while using 90.3% fewer recorded tokens, and also outperforms RepoSearcher (56.2%) and Agentless (39.6%) on Func@1. On SWE-bench Lite, CuraLoc achieves a high 83.8% Func@5 accuracy improving on Agentless by more than 15% in relative accuracy. Additionally, CuraLoc supports different model backbones, with strong localization accuracy demonstrated using both GPT-5.2 and GPT-6 Astra. These results provide evidence that repository-level code inspection can be planned effectively without iterative model-controlled navigation.

</details>


### [308] [Nested Sparsity for Scalable Pretraining and Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31818)
*Tianzhe Chu,Druv Pai,Yi Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Nestor，将条件计算机制从MoE算子推广到注意力算子，形成的嵌套稀疏与MoE稀疏天然组合；在等算力等稀疏度下预训练优于标准MoE Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE Transformer的条件计算仅在MoE算子（MLP替代）处产生稀疏；作者希望探索一种类似推广注意力算子的替代条件计算机制，并让其稀疏性与MoE稀疏性层次化嵌套。

Method: 设计一种作用于注意力算子的条件计算机制，使诱导的稀疏与MoE诱导的稀疏可嵌套（层次化组合），构建Nestor Transformer；在语言模型预训练中与等算力、等稀疏度的传统MoE Transformer对比，并测试持续预训练（长上下文适配、新领域适配）及规模扩展。

Result: 等算力等稀疏度下Nestor语言模型预训练优于传统MoE；持续预训练中更有效适配更长上下文，适配新领域更高效且对旧领域遗忘更少；这些优势随模型规模、数据规模和上下文长度增长而保持。

Conclusion: 替代性条件计算机制（注意力算子上的稀疏）对深层网络架构具有潜力，嵌套稀疏为MoE之外的可行方向；代码与检查点已开源。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) transformers, the standard backbone for modern large-scale deep learning, employ conditional computation: only a sparse subset of model weights is used in each forward pass. In MoE transformers, this sparsity arises in the MoE operator, which generalizes the multi-layer perceptron in the classical transformer. In this work, we explore an alternative mechanism for conditional computation that analogously generalizes the attention operator. The sparsity induced by this mechanism naturally nests (i.e., hierarchically composes) with the sparsity induced by the MoE operator. We show that a transformer employing this nested sparsity mechanism, which we call Nestor, performs better than a conventional MoE transformer at equal compute and equal sparsity in language model pretraining. We also show that Nestor is well-suited for continual pretraining: it can be adapted to longer contexts more effectively than conventional MoEs, as well as adapt more efficiently to new domains while forgetting less on old domains. Furthermore, we demonstrate that these benefits persist as model size, dataset size, and context length grow. Our results demonstrate the potential of alternative conditional computation mechanisms for deep neural network architectures. Code and checkpoints are available on GitHub at https://github.com/Ma-Lab-Berkeley/Nestor .

</details>


### [309] [Speculative Diffusion is a Tale of Two Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31819)
*Aaron Zorel,Guy Van den Broeck,Aditya Grover,Daniel Israel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究投机解码（speculative decoding）与扩散语言模型（dLLM）结合时的速度-质量权衡，指出投机解码可推广为"有损"形式（放宽接受准则），并联合调节扩散努力程度与接受宽松度两个参数，探索完整的帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统投机解码相对验证器分布是无损的，而扩散语言模型可通过降低扩散努力换取速度但牺牲质量。两者各自都能在速度-质量间取舍，但此前未系统研究同时调节这两个维度能否获得更优的权衡。

Method: 将扩散模型作为草稿模型进行投机扩散解码，把扩散努力程度（diffusion effort）和投机解码接受宽松度（acceptance leniency，相对验证器分布的宽松接受准则）作为两个可调参数，联合变化二者，绘制并分析完整的可达帕累托前沿。

Result: 实验表明，联合调节扩散努力与接受宽松度，相比单步扩散草稿（single step diffusion drafting）能够进一步扩展速度-质量帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 扩散努力与投机宽松度这两个"质量-速度"旋钮可联合优化，为扩散草稿投机解码提供更优的速度-质量权衡，拓展了可实现的帕累托前沿。

Abstract: Speculative decoding is a technique that accelerates the inference speed of autoregressive large language models (LLMs) by sampling from a faster, lower quality draft model. Traditionally, a fundamental characteristic of speculative decoding is that it is lossless with respect to the verifier distribution, guaranteeing speed without sacrificing quality. This differs from recently developed diffusion LLMs (dLLMs), which are notably able to achieve substantial speedups by degrading the quality of their outputs with less diffusion effort. Because diffusion models are able to sample faster than autoregressive models by generating tokens in parallel, they are considered strong candidates for draft models in speculative diffusion. While it is widely understood that diffusion models can sacrifice quality for speed, speculative decoding itself can also be generalized to generate lossy samples faster using more lenient acceptance criteria with respect to the verifier distribution. Presented with the two parameters of diffusion effort and speculative decoding leniency, this paper explores the full achievable Pareto frontier via joint variations of these parameters. Our experiments show that jointly varying diffusion effort and acceptance leniency can extend the speed-quality Pareto frontier over single step diffusion drafting.

</details>


### [310] [Rethinking Multi-View Fusion for Urban Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31850)
*Sean Bin Yang,Zongyi Xu,Jilin Hu,Ying Sun,Kristian Torp,Bin Yang,Torben Bach Pedersen,Christian S. Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRISM 是一个自适应多视图学习框架，用稀疏 MoE 路由学习区域专属的视图分配，并用 PID 式调节机制缓解多目标优化中的梯度冲突，在城市区域表示学习中兼顾效果与训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市区域表示方法采用全局共享融合与联合优化，隐含假设各模态重要性一致、多目标训练天然均衡；但实证发现两个结构性问题：(i) 模态效用随区域显著变化，静态融合会过度强调某些模态信号；(ii) 异质目标之间存在持续的梯度不平衡，导致表示偏差与训练不稳定。

Method: 提出 PRISM 自调整框架：采用稀疏 MoE 路由学习区域特定的视图分配（region-specific view allocation），实现自适应多视图融合；同时引入受 PID（比例-积分-微分）启发的调节机制，在优化过程中缓解梯度冲突。

Result: 在三个大规模城市数据集上，PRISM 与强多视图基线相比取得有竞争力的性能，同时表现出更稳定的优化行为和更均衡的区域表示。

Conclusion: 针对城市数据异质性，区域自适应融合与梯度冲突调节是提升多视图区域表示质量与训练稳定性的有效途径。

Abstract: Urban region representation learning encodes heterogeneous urban signals, such as mobility flows, points of interest, and land-use patterns, into unified embeddings for downstream tasks, including check-in prediction, crime forecasting, and service demand estimation. Existing approaches rely on globally shared fusion and joint optimization, implicitly assuming uniform modality importance and naturally balanced multi-objective training. However, urban data is inherently heterogeneous. Our empirical analysis reveals two structural issues: (i) modality utility varies markedly across regions, causing static fusion to overemphasize signals from certain modalities; and (ii) heterogeneous objectives exhibit persistent gradient imbalance, causing representational bias and unstable training. We present PRISM, a self-adjusting framework for adaptive multi-view learning. PRISM adapts sparse MoE routing to learn region-specific view allocations, while a PID (Proportional Integral Derivative)-inspired regulation mechanism mitigates gradient conflicts during optimization. Experiments on three large-scale urban datasets show that PRISM achieves competitive performance compared with strong multi-view baselines, while providing more stable optimization behavior and more balanced region representations.

</details>


### [311] [Cautious Conflict-Free Inverse Gradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31855)
*Damian Hattler,Qiang Liu,Nils Thuerey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出ConFIG方法与Adam等自适应优化器结合时，冲突消解方向会被优化器进一步处理而失去无冲突保证，提出仅三行代码的Cautious ConFIG扩展来恢复该保证，在三个PINN基准上取得稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN训练需多目标优化，边界、初始与残差损失间常出现梯度冲突。ConFIG能缓解冲突，但与Adam等自适应优化器结合后，优化器对无冲突方向再做处理，导致实际权重更新不再无冲突，破坏了原有保证。

Method: 提出Cautious ConFIG：在ConFIG基础上加入谨慎掩码（cautious masking）作为三行代码的简洁扩展，在权重更新前过滤与任务梯度不一致的更新分量，从而恢复更新前的无冲突性。在1D粘性Burgers方程、1D非线性Schrödinger方程和2D Kovasznay流三个PINN基准上评估，并用简易玩具系统分析掩码的稳定作用及其对冲突严重度、任务数和优化器滞后的响应。

Result: 三个PINN基准实验均显示相对标准ConFIG基线的一致性性能提升；玩具系统实验表明谨慎掩码具有稳定效果，且其作用随冲突严重度、任务数量与优化器滞后而变化。

Conclusion: Cautious ConFIG以极小改动解决了ConFIG与自适应优化器结合时无冲突保证失效的问题，是一种简洁有效的改进，并揭示了谨慎掩码在何种条件下最能发挥作用。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) are indispensable tools for modeling complex phenomena in physics and engineering. Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) have emerged as a powerful alternative to traditional numerical solvers by directly incorporating physical constraints into neural network loss functions. Despite their advantages, PINN training requires multi-objective optimization, which often leads to significant gradient conflicts among boundary, initial, and residual loss terms. The Conflict-Free Inverse Gradient (ConFIG) method addresses these conflicts; however, integrating ConFIG with adaptive optimizers such as Adam introduces a critical vulnerability: the conflict-free direction generated by ConFIG is further processed by the optimizer, and the resulting weight update may no longer be conflict-free with respect to the task gradients. To address this issue, Cautious ConFIG is proposed as a concise three-line extension to the ConFIG algorithm that empirically restores the conflict-freeness guarantee prior to the weight update. The method is evaluated on three PINN benchmarks: the 1D Viscous Burgers' equation, the 1D Nonlinear Schrödinger equation, and the 2D Kovasznay flow. Experimental results demonstrate consistent performance improvements over the standard ConFIG baseline. Additionally, the stabilizing effect of cautious masking and its reaction to conflict severity, task number, and optimizer lag are investigated using a simple toy system.

</details>


### [312] [Aligning Large Language Models with Human Feedback: Mathematical Foundations and Algorithm Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31862)
*Siliang Zeng,Luca Viano,Chenliang Li,Jiaxiang Li,Volkan Cevher,Markus Wulfmeier,Stefano Ermon,Alfredo Garcia,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇教程性综述，系统介绍将大语言模型与人类意图、偏好和价值观对齐的数学基础与算法框架，涵盖SFT、RLHF、DPO等主流方法，并从逆强化学习与离散选择模型视角解读人类偏好学习理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型对齐问题涉及复杂的数学与算法基础，研究者与从业者缺乏统一、系统的资源来理解主流对齐技术背后的理论根源与实现要点，因此需要一篇兼顾基础理解与方法开发框架的综合性教程。

Method: 以教程风格组织内容：介绍SFT、RLHF、DPO等标准对齐技术，并建立人类偏好学习与逆强化学习（IRL）、离散选择模型之间的理论联系；对最先进算法逐一讲解，分析其优势与局限，并给出实际实现建议。

Result: 呈现了对齐方法论的完整梳理，包括标准技术的形式化介绍、偏好学习的理论解释、算法对比分析以及面向实践的实现洞见。

Conclusion: 本文为研究者和从业者提供了一份综合性的对齐方法参考资源，既建立了对齐方法的基础理解，也为开发更鲁棒、更具可扩展性的对齐技术提供了理论框架。

Abstract: This article provides an introduction to the mathematical foundations and algorithmic frameworks used to align Large Language Models (LLMs) with human intentions, preferences, and values. We discuss standard alignment techniques, such as fine-tuning (SFT), reinforcement learning with human feedback (RLHF), and direct preference optimization (DPO). We also explore the theoretical underpinnings of learning from human preferences, drawing connections to inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) and discrete choice models. We present state-of-the-art algorithms in a tutorial style, discuss their advantages and limitations, and offer insights into practical implementation. Our exposition is intended to serve as a comprehensive resource for researchers and practitioners, providing both a foundational understanding of alignment methodologies and a framework for developing more robust and scalable alignment techniques.

</details>


### [313] [Rewriting Scaling Laws for Synthetic Data with Compute and Data Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31867)
*Nicholas Roberts,Ahmed Ahmed,Suhas Kotha,Percy Liang,Yu Wang,Alex Renda,Nitya Mani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"改写缩放律"，用模型规模、训练token数、改写token占比及自然数据遍历轮数来预测损失，并计入生成改写所花的算力。结果表明最优改写占比随算力增大而上升，且训练在改写数据上的模型可超越改写器本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成改写（用语言模型把自然文档改写成目标风格等）已成为预训练语料的常规成分，但其使用中的关键问题仍不清楚：应在改写训练数据上投入多少算力？用改写数据训练的模型能否胜过改写器本身？

Method: 提出改写缩放律，以模型规模、训练token数、改写token占比、自然数据epoch数为输入预测损失，并将生成改写的算力计入总预算。在两种设定下拟合：(1) 算力受限设定——自然数据充足，改写器为第三方、训练算力高于最大实验；(2) 数据受限设定——仅有固定种子自然数据，改写器与目标模型同规模且在该种子集上预训练。

Result: 算力受限设定中，最优改写占比随算力从3e18 FLOPs的不足10%升至1e22 FLOPs的32%；外推33倍FLOPs训练22B模型，最终损失与预测相差在1.1%以内，且在多个网页领域以改写器十分之一的训练算力取得更优BPB。数据受限设定中，最优改写占比同样随算力预算上升；用同规模改写器改写自身预训练数据训练出的模型，在各规模直至3B时均优于仅用自然数据的最佳运行。

Conclusion: 合成改写可通过缩放律视角加以研究：最优方法选择是可预测的，且缩放并不会因超出改写器能力而失效。

Abstract: Synthetic rewrites, where a language model rewrites a natural document e.g. into a target style, are now a standard ingredient of pretraining corpora. However, critical aspects of their use remain poorly understood, such as how much compute should be spent rewriting training data and whether a model trained on rewrites can outperform the rewriter. We study these two questions by proposing a rewriting scaling law that predicts loss from model size, training tokens, the fraction of training tokens that are rewrites, and the number of epochs over the natural data, charging the compute spent generating rewrites. We fit this scaling law in two settings. In the compute-limited setting, natural data is plentiful and rewrites come from a third-party rewriter trained with more compute than our largest run. We find that the estimated optimal rewrite fraction increases with compute, from under $10\%$ at $3\times10^{18}$ FLOPs to $32\%$ at $10^{22}$ FLOPs. Our scaling forecast extrapolates $33\times$ in FLOPs to train a 22B-parameter model, whose final loss is within $1.1\%$ of our prediction. This model achieves a better BPB than its rewriter on several web domains at one-tenth the rewriter's training compute. In the data-limited setting, we assume we only have a fixed seed set of natural data and that our rewriter is a model of the same size as the target, pretrained on that seed set. Again, we find the optimal rewrite fraction increases with the compute budget. We also demonstrate that a model trained on rewrites of its own pretraining data, generated by a rewriter of the same size, beats the best run on natural data alone at every model size up to 3B. Our results show that synthetic rewriting can be studied through the scaling law lens: that optimal methodological choices are predictable and scaling does not necessarily break down beyond the rewriter's capabilities.

</details>


### [314] [Forecasting Associations, Not Interventions: A Preregistered Causal Audit of Covariate-Aware Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31869)
*Zong-Han Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 若把协变量感知时间序列基础模型（TSFM）当作可做"what-if"问询的因果预言机，它们给出的其实是观测性关联斜率而非干预效应，且比回归本身更"混淆"；但数分钟干预式后训练即可显著缩小乃至修复这一差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协变量感知TSFM接受控制变量的未来路径并返回预测，用户自然会把这种问询理解为干预式(interventional)查询；然而这些模型只在观测数据上训练。论文要判定：它们回答的究竟是条件斜率∂E[Y|X]，还是因果效应∂E[Y|do(X)]——即模型是否在做因果推断，还是在做关联外推。

Method: 从三个层面展开。理论：证明贝叶斯最优的观测预测器在缺少并正确使用调整集时会以关联方式回答干预查询；对in-context最小二乘推导出"混淆差距"（观测上下文与随机化上下文下的效应估计之差）的闭式解，作为无需拟合参数的参照线。行为：在预注册网格上用精确反事实真值，测试Chronos-2、TiRex-2、TabPFN-TS、TimesFM-2.5，覆盖线性与非线性结构模型，并用观测律相同的模型对来区分"识别性限制"与"模型能力限制"。表征：用线性探针从嵌入中解码数据生成机制与真实效应。系统验证：在两个带随机化执行器的物理系统上测试预训练模型，随后用3,000条合成序列做数分钟干预式后训练（含replay与低秩适配）。

Result: 四个模型均紧密贴合理论混淆线（R² 0.90–1.00），混淆程度甚至超过回归本身（斜率1.3–1.8）。同一观测律的模型对表明这不是识别性上限：效应可识别时，最小二乘恢复100%真实效应差，Chronos-2为75%，TiRex-2仅34%。线性探针以94–96%解码生成机制、以R² 0.7–0.9解码真实效应，而预测本身并未利用这些信息。物理系统上预训练模型响应不足2–5倍，并忽略窗口内混淆。干预式后训练使差距降低最多83%并泛化到训练先验之外；其预测代价源于replay分布效应，replay预训练先验可将MASE增幅从+47%降至+10%，结合低秩适配后以+1–3%成本实现52–61%的差距缩减，并改善DoTime基准。

Conclusion: 协变量感知TSFM的"what-if"回答是关联性的，混淆程度可被理论闭式刻画，且并非数据识别性的必然结果，而是模型未利用可用的干预信息——其嵌入中实际编码了正确效应却被预测忽略。这一缺陷可通过少量合成数据的干预式后训练以很低的价格被大幅修正，为因果可信的TSFM提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Covariate-aware time series foundation models (TSFMs) accept a future path for a control variable and return a forecast, inviting what-if queries. The query is interventional; the models are trained on observational data.
