In a hierarchical multi-index model, we establish a feature-recovery separation: backward coupling recovers lower-layer directions invisible to the original forward label-weighted statistic. The linear specialization recovers residual-dependent singular-mode dynamics, while paired modes provide a dynamical mechanism for linked low-rank covariance components consistent with the rainbow-network picture. Experiments with fully connected and convolutional networks on binary CIFAR-10 show improved accuracy over forward Neural LoFi at matched widths.

</details>


### [387] [Near-Optimal List Replicability from Littlestone Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32853)
*Ari Blondal,Pooya Hatami,Chavdar Lalov,Sivan Tretiak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在可实现PAC学习框架下研究list replicability number，证明对Littlestone维数为d、对偶VC维为v*的概念类H，LR(H) ≤ 2^{O(d·min{log d, v*})}，指数级改进了此前上界2^{2^{O(d)}}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前最好的上界为Bun、Livni、Moran的2^{2^{O(d)}}，与最坏情况下的下界2^{Ω(d)}在指数上差距巨大（双重指数 vs 单指数）；尽管对固定精度ε已有2^{O_ε(d^2)}的界，但它们依赖ε。作者希望得到一个与ε无关、且指数接近最优的list replicability上界。

Method: 论文给出两条技术路线：(1) 基于差分隐私学习中近期提出的"不可约性"(irreducibility)概念，构建新的归纳框架来界定list replicability，导出2^{O(d log d)}的界；(2) 通过一个简单的全局稳定学习器——对多个经验风险最小化器（ERM）取多数投票——并借助稳定正则性(stable regularity)工具证明其稳定性，导出2^{O(d v*)}的界。二者取min得到最终结果。

Result: 证明了LR(H) ≤ 2^{O(d·min{log d, v*})}，在指数上仅比最坏情况下界2^{Ω(d)}多一个对数因子，并相对Bun-Livni-Moran的2^{2^{O(d)}}实现了指数级改进；同时该界不依赖于精度ε，优于Ghazi-Golowich-Kumar-Manurangsi和Lyu给出的固定ε下的2^{O_ε(d^2)}结果。

Conclusion: list replicability number可达到几乎匹配最坏情况下界的单指数量级，且与ε无关；结果连接了差分隐私中的不可约性思想与稳定正则性工具，为理解可实现PAC学习中可复现性的本质极限提供了新的技术框架。

Abstract: We study the list replicability number in the realizable PAC learning framework and prove that every concept class $H$ over a countable domain with Littlestone dimension $d$ and dual VC dimension $v^*$ satisfies \[ \mathrm{LR}(H)\leq 2^{O(d\min\{\log d,v^*\})}. \] This matches the worst-case lower bound of $2^{Ω(d)}$ up to a logarithmic factor in the exponent and exponentially improves the previous best upper bound $\mathrm{LR}(H)\le 2^{2^{O(d)}}$ of Bun, Livni, and Moran [FOCS 2020]. Unlike earlier bounds of $2^{O_ε(d^2)}$ for fixed accuracy $ε>0$ implied by the work of Ghazi, Golowich, Kumar, and Manurangsi [STOC 2021] and Lyu [STOC 2026], our bound is independent of $ε$.
  The $2^{O(d\log d)}$ bound follows from a new inductive framework for bounding list replicability that builds on the recent notion of irreducibility from differentially private learning. The $2^{O(dv^*)}$ bound follows from a simple globally stable learner that takes the majority vote of multiple empirical risk minimizers, with stability established using tools from stable regularity.

</details>


### [388] [Regularised RL improves sim-to-real transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32855)
*Danilo Vucetic,Mohamad H. Danesh,Florence Clerc,Hsiu-Chin Lin,Jonathan Tremblay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于soft mellowmax价值函数的正则化RL算法SMM-AC，验证正则化能提升sim-to-real迁移中的鲁棒性与实机表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则化RL虽有可证明的鲁棒性保证，理论上有利于sim-to-real迁移，但实践中弱正则的PPO仍是主流，正则化对真实迁移的实际收益尚缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 推导新的正则化RL算法SMM-AC（Soft MellowMax Actor-Critic），基于soft mellowmax价值函数，具备强鲁棒性保证；在MuJoCo连续控制基准上与SAC等基线对比，并在真实机器人上评估回报及对观测、环境与动力学扰动的鲁棒性。

Result: 在MuJoCo基准上SMM-AC与SAC等基线性能相当；在真实机器人上，SMM-AC取得更高回报，且对观测、环境和动力学扰动表现出比基线更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 正则化RL通过对sim-to-real差距及真实世界条件变化具有更好的鲁棒性，改善了真实世界策略部署效果。

Abstract: Regularised reinforcement learning (RL) has provable robustness guarantees that should ease the transfer of policies from simulation to robots, known as sim-to-real. Yet, weakly regularised algorithms like Proximal Policy Optimisation (PPO) remain the de facto choice in sim-to-real pipelines, while the practical benefits of regularisation for transfer remain poorly characterised. In this work, we study the sim-to-real performance of commonly used RL algorithms, with the aim of characterising the effects of regularisation on robotic performance. To that end, we derive Soft MellowMax Actor-Critic (SMM-AC), a novel regularised RL algorithm based on the soft mellowmax value function, which carries strong robustness guarantees. On MuJoCo continuous control benchmarks, SMM-AC is competitive with baselines like Soft Actor-Critic (SAC). On real robots, SMM-AC achieves higher return, while exhibiting greater robustness to perturbations in observations, the environment, and dynamics than baselines. Together, these results demonstrate that regularised RL improves real-world policy deployment through better robustness to the sim-to-real gap, and changing real-world conditions.

</details>


### [389] [Sustainability of TSFM Deployment and Pretraining: From Carbon Footprint Analysis to Mitigation via Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32859)
*Cécile Luc,Nathanaël Lemonnier,Ievgen Redko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从碳排放视角评估时序基础模型(TSFM)全生命周期成本,发现Chronos-2在大规模或高频查询场景下数月即可摊薄预训练成本,小规模/低频场景下经典模型始终更省;并提出无梯度剪枝准则,去除单个编码器块(7.9%参数)可稳定降低6-9%的FLOPs、CO2与延迟,且几乎不损失零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSFM零样本预测能力强,但预训练成本高昂,尚不清楚这笔前期投入相对于需周期性重训的监督式独立模型,在完整部署生命周期内是否划算。作者希望回答这一可持续性问题,并探索降低TSFM自身成本的手段。

Method: 建模真实工业场景下不同方案的累计碳排放成本,对比Chronos-2与经典监督模型在全生命周期内的成本分摊;同时提出一种无梯度的编码器块剪枝准则,用于削减TSFM自身开销。

Result: 大规模或高频查询部署中,Chronos-2可在数月内摊薄预训练成本;小规模或低频查询场景中经典模型始终更便宜。剪除单个编码器块(7.9%参数)使FLOPs、CO2排放与延迟一致下降6-9%,在25项任务基准上零样本预测性能基本无损。

Conclusion: TSFM的成本效益高度依赖部署规模与查询频率,需按场景权衡;无梯度剪枝是降低TSFM碳成本与延迟的低代价有效手段。

Abstract: Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) offer strong zero-shot forecasting capabilities, but their high pretraining cost raises a practical sustainability question: does this upfront investment pay off over a full deployment lifecycle, compared to supervised standalone models that must be periodically retrained? We model the cumulative carbon cost of different approaches across a realistic industrial scenario: Chronos-2 amortizes its pretraining cost within months for large or frequently queried deployments, but a classical model remains cheaper indefinitely for small or rarely queried ones. For cases where reducing the TSFM's own cost matters, we show that removing a single encoder block (7.9% of parameters) via a gradient-free pruning criterion yields a consistent 6 to 9% reduction in FLOPs, CO2 emissions, and latency, at essentially no cost to zero-shot forecasting performance across a 25-task benchmark.

</details>


### [390] [Interpretable sparse fine-tuning with TopKLoRA enables faithful circuit discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32866)
*Marek Masiak,Andrzej Szablewski,Nicola Cancedda,Constantin Venhoff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TopKLoRA——一种可解释的稀疏 LoRA 变体，只激活每 token 最大的 k 个 adapter latent，并用它定位并消融 sleeper-agent 后门电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知来源数据微调可能植入后门，且后门难定位、难移除、能逃过安全训练；参数高效微调虽能捕获全部参数变化，但难以解释。

Method: TopKLoRA：在每个 token 上仅让 k 个最大的 adapter latent 生效；不同于 SAE，它仅用下游损失训练，使优化压力促使学习功能上因果的 latent。随后在 gemma-2-2b、Qwen2.5-1.5B、Qwen2.5-7B 上构造投毒指令微调的 model organisms，进行电路搜索与消融；并提出用 TopKLoRA latent 与梯度路由构造局部化电路的 model organism 来验证电路搜索。

Result: 对每个 organism 都识别出既必要又充分实现后门的电路；所得稀疏电路比稠密 LoRA 基线小至多一个数量级；可外科式消融恶意能力而不损失指令遵循性能；构造的局部化电路验证了电路搜索算法结论。

Conclusion: TopKLoRA 作为 by-design 可解释架构，提供忠实分析单元而不牺牲模型性能，是迈向可解释微调的一步。

Abstract: Fine-tuning on datasets of unknown origin can create a backdoor in the trained model when the data includes poisoned examples. Difficult to localise and remove, backdoors can persist through safety training. While parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods capture the full parameter changes introduced during training, they are not easily interpretable. We present the full method of TopKLoRA, extending the preliminary proposal of Masiak et al. (2025), an interpretable-by-design LoRA variant in which only the $k$ largest adapter latents are active at each token. Unlike SAEs, TopKLoRA is trained purely on downstream loss, where optimisation pressure incentivises learning functionally causal latents. We demonstrate this sparse LoRA adapter in a sleeper-agent backdoor case study where we create model organisms by fine-tuning gemma-2-2b, Qwen2.5-1.5B, and Qwen2.5-7B on a poisoned instruction-following dataset. For each organism, we identify and ablate a circuit that is both necessary and sufficient to implement the backdoor. Furthermore, our new fine-tuning method produces sparse circuits that are up to an order of magnitude smaller than a dense LoRA baseline, allowing surgical ablation of the malicious capability without sacrificing instruction-following performance. Finally, we propose a technique to construct model organisms with localised circuits using TopKLoRA latents and gradient-routing, which we use to validate the findings of our circuit search algorithm. We consider these findings as a step toward interpretable-by-design architectures that provide faithful units of analysis without compromising model performance. We share our code: https://github.com/interpretable-finetuning/TopKLoRA.

</details>


### [391] [PoKER: A Probability of Kill Estimation Rate Model for Air-to-Air Missiles Using Machine Learning on Stochastic Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32864)
*Joao P. A. Dantas,Andre N. Costa,Diego Geraldo,Marcos R. O. A. Maximo,Takashi Yoneyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PoKER是一个概率模型，用机器学习结合目标随机行为与导弹脱靶距离来预测BVR空战的杀伤概率，取代传统基于静态距离的WEZ模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统WEZ模型仅用最大/最小/不可逃逸等静态距离划分区域，忽略了目标机动随机性和脱靶距离的不确定性，预测能力有限，难以支撑超视距空战的实际决策。

Method: 采用机器学习方法构建概率杀伤模型，输入包括目标行为动态、导弹脱靶距离分布、战斗部杀伤力、目标与射手朝向及具体交战条件，从而动态评估杀伤概率。

Result: 模型能动态评估目标行为，显著扩展交战分析的预测能力，量化不同条件下的成功概率，并给出交战动态的重要洞见。

Conclusion: PoKER有望成为BVR空战飞行员的重要辅助工具，提升该作战域内的作战决策水平。

Abstract: This work introduces PoKER, a novel probabilistic model engineered to optimize missile launch effectiveness in air-to-air scenarios, specifically within Beyond Visual Range (BVR) air combat. Unlike conventional Weapon Engagement Zone (WEZ) models that delineate zones based on static distances such as maximum, minimum, and no-escape ranges, PoKER applies machine learning to predict kill probabilities more accurately by integrating the stochastic behaviors of targets and missile miss distances. This model dynamically evaluates target behavior, greatly expanding the predictive capabilities of engagement analysis. By factoring in elements such as warhead lethality, target and shooter orientations, and the specific conditions of engagement, PoKER provides important insights into engagement dynamics and quantifies success probabilities. Consequently, it can potentially be an important tool for BVR air combat pilots, improving operational decision-making within this specialized combat domain.

</details>


### [392] [Reinforcement Learning with Multi-Step Transition Predictions: A Bellman Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32887)
*Chenbei Lu,Zaiwei Chen,Tongxin Li,Chenye Wu,Adam Wierman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究块反馈协议下多步转移预测对强化学习的价值：证明最优值满足原状态空间上的贝叶斯Bellman方程，提出BF-BOLA算法并给出匹配的样本复杂度上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步转移预测量能提升RL，但其价值难以量化；直接把预测并入状态会导致状态空间指数增长，动态规划代价高昂。作者希望在有限折扣MDP中定量刻画预测信息带来的收益。

Method: 设定块反馈协议（每K步块开始时观测部分动作的K步概率转移预测，块内依观测状态调整决策），推导原状态空间上的贝叶斯Bellman方程，并用Bellman–Jensen分析刻画最大化与期望顺序交换带来的价值；提出基于模型的BF-BOLA离线学习加在线自适应算法。

Result: 证明最优值可由贝叶斯Bellman方程表示，量化预测时域、误差、动作覆盖度与块内反馈的影响；样本复杂度上下界在对数因子内匹配；预测可将转移学习限制在未被覆盖的行上，且学习近优策略所需预测样本可少于估计贝叶斯值函数。

Conclusion: 预测信息的价值来自递归地改变Bellman展开中局部最大化与期望的顺序；块反馈框架为量化多步预测价值提供了可计算的表示与样本高效的学习方法。

Abstract: Multi-step predictions of future transition realizations can improve reinforcement learning, but their value remains difficult to quantify. Directly including these predictions in the state can lead to exponential state-space growth, making direct dynamic programming computationally expensive. We study discounted finite MDPs under a block-feedback protocol: at the start of each K-period block, the decision maker observes $K$-step probabilistic transition predictions for a subset of actions and adapts decisions to observed states within the block. We show that the optimal value satisfies a Bayesian Bellman equation on the original state space. This representation reveals that predictive information creates value by recursively changing the local order of maximization and expectation in the Bellman expansion. We formalize this effect through a Bellman--Jensen analysis and quantify the effects of prediction horizon, error, action coverage, and within-block feedback. We propose block-feedback Bayesian offline learning with online adaptation (BF--BOLA), a model-based algorithm for learning near-optimal policies, and establish upper and lower sample-complexity bounds that match up to logarithmic factors in the stated regimes. The results show that predictions can reduce transition learning to uncovered rows and that learning a near-optimal policy can require fewer prediction samples than estimating the Bayesian value function.

</details>


### [393] [Recommender regret for improving regularised RL estimation with bandits and MCTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32910)
*Danilo Vucetic,Alexandre Larouche,Mohammad Pedramfar,Audrey Durand,Gauthier Gidel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将基于 bandit 的采样视为"推荐过程"与"改进算子"，提出正则化感知的推荐遗憾来衡量估计质量，并统一为 Recommender MCTS，实验显示其收敛更快、下游表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 bandit 的估计框架只评估采样效率，而不评估估计（policy/value/Q 值）本身的质量，导致无法判断估计好坏，也难以比较 MCTS 与一步前瞻等方法。

Method: 把估计生成形式化为推荐过程，将 bandit 采样作为改进算子；定义正则化感知的推荐遗憾（regulariser-aware recommender regrets）并分析其下界，证明均匀采样等简单算法即可达到下界；最后用 Recommender MCTS 统一 bandit 与搜索式目标改进。

Result: 在合成树、街机游戏和科学发现任务上，推荐器往往带来更快的目标收敛和更优的下游性能；并首次刻画了正则化 MCTS 在科学应用中的潜力。

Conclusion: 以推荐遗憾为核心的评估视角补足了采样效率之外的估计质量度量，Recommender MCTS 为统一与改进正则化 RL 的估计器提供了有效框架，并展示了在科学发现等领域的应用前景。

Abstract: Regularised reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms rely on estimators, like Monte Carlo tree search or one-step look-ahead, to provide policy, value, or Q-value estimates in training and inference. However, existing bandit-based estimation frameworks evaluate sampling efficiency, not the quality of estimates. We address this by formalising estimate generation as a recommendation process, treating bandit-based sampling as an improvement operator. To evaluate the quality of a recommendation (estimate), we define regulariser-aware recommender regrets, analysing their lower bounds, and proving that simple algorithms like uniform sampling can achieve these bounds. Finally, we unify bandit- and search-based target improvement under recommender MCTS. Across synthetic trees, arcade games, and scientific discovery tasks, recommenders often yield faster target convergence and superior downstream performance. Our experiments characterise for the first time the potential of regularised MCTS in scientific applications.

</details>


### [394] [A Tutorial on Physics Constrained Machine Learning for Hybrid Modeling, Optimization, and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32914)
*Rahul Golder,Bimol Nath Roy,MM Faruque Hasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模块化物理约束机器学习框架KKT-HardNet，通过微分投影层保证代数约束严格满足，支持混合建模、过程模拟、优化与模型预测控制的一体化平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCML需将领域知识与物理约束融入ML模型，但现有方法在保证约束满足、处理异构数据及高效端到端训练方面仍有不足，且缺乏统一的建模-仿真-优化-控制平台与基准问题。

Method: 构建模块化KKT-HardNet框架：结合神经网络快速推理与微分投影保证线性和非线性等式/不等式约束；支持自定义网络骨干与投影机制；利用隐函数定理反向传播穿过投影层，避免昂贵自动微分；并引入精确降阶Fischer-Burmeister重构与原始-对偶内点法作为替代投影求解策略；同时提供多类工程基准问题。

Result: 在约束满足度和推理时间上均取得数量级的性能提升。

Conclusion: KKT-HardNet为PCML提供统一、可定制且高效的框架，代码与基准数据集已开源。

Abstract: Physics-constrained machine learning (PCML) integrates domain knowledge, first-principles, and physical constraints into AI/ML models. In this work, we present a step-by-step tutorial of a modular PCML framework, KKT-HardNet, and illustrate how it could be used for hybrid modeling, process simulation, optimization and model predictive control in a single platform. KKT-HardNet combines fast inference capabilities of neural networks with guaranteed satisfaction of algebraic constraints through differential projection. It can handle heterogeneous data and enforce both linear and nonlinear equality and inequality constraints. The modular design supports custom neural-network backbones and user-defined projection mechanisms for PCML. To enable efficient end-to-end training, KKT-HardNet exploits implicit function theorem to backpropagate through the projection layer. This avoids computationally expensive automatic differentiation. This work also introduces an exact reduced-order Fischer-Burmeister reformulation and a primal-dual interior-point method as an alternative projection solve strategy. We also provide a diverse set of benchmarking problems for PCML in engineering applications. KKT-HardNet achieves orders of magnitude better performance in terms of both constraint satisfaction and inference time. The KKT-HardNet code and the benchmark datasets are available at https://github.com/souls-tamu/kkt-hardnet .

</details>


### [395] [ZENO: Nonlinear filtering with Zakai Equation Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32926)
*Aaditya Prasad,Steven W. Flavell,Brian DePasquale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ZENO：以Zakai方程物理信息损失训练神经算子，逼近未归一化滤波分布，并结合EM做潜状态推断与SDE参数学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性滤波中的状态推断与参数学习是科学机器学习的核心计算，其未归一化滤波分布由Zakai方程（SPDE）支配；该高维SPDE通常无法解析求解、数值上也难以处理，需要新方法。

Method: 利用算子学习与物理信息机器学习的进展，构建Zakai Equation Neural Operator（ZENO），以基于Zakai方程的物理信息损失优化神经算子以近似精确未归一化滤波分布；再将ZENO推断的后验分布嵌入EM框架，进行参数学习与状态推断。

Result: 在三个模拟随机动力系统（双井双稳系统、Van der Pol振子、Lorenz系统）上准确推断后验分布并学习对应SDE参数，与已有潜SDE推断/学习方法对比；在固定化秀丽隐杆线虫全脑成像数据中恢复出先前观察到的代表前后运动的极限环。

Conclusion: ZENO通过PDE与算子理论视角为非线性滤波与动力学推断提供了新途径。

Abstract: State inference and parameter learning for nonlinear filters is a core computation for scientific machine learning with varied applications in engineering and neuroscience. The unnormalized filtering distribution is governed by the Zakai equation, a stochastic partial differential equation (SPDE) that combines the forward Kolmogorov operator (known as the Fokker-Planck equation) with observed likelihood updates. Although solving these high-dimensional SPDEs is often impossible analytically and intractable numerically, advances in operator learning and physics-informed machine learning have made it feasible. Harnessing these advances, we introduce the Zakai Equation Neural Operator (ZENO), a model that optimizes a neural operator against a Zakai Equation based physics-informed loss to approximate the exact unnormalized filtering distribution for latent stochastic dynamical systems. We use this ZENO-inferred posterior distribution in an expectation-maximization (EM) framework to perform parameter learning and state inference. We demonstrate ZENO's ability to accurately infer the posterior distribution and learn its corresponding SDE parameters in three simulated stochastic dynamical systems (Double-Well bistable system, Van Der Pol (VDP) oscillator, and Lorenz system) and compare its performance against established methods for latent SDE inference and learning. We show that ZENO recovers previously observed limit cycles representing forward and backward locomotion from a whole-brain imaging dataset of immobilized \textit{C. elegans} nematodes. ZENO is a novel approach to the problem of nonlinear filtering and dynamical inference through the lens of partial differential equations and operator theory.

</details>


### [396] [Where Rounding Happens Matters: Rounding-Location Alignment Stabilizes LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32951)
*Yijing Ke,Tianle Cai,Ruosong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在BF16强化学习训练中，只需对齐推理与训练执行栈中RoPE、SwiGLU、残差RMSNorm三个区域的舍入位置（保留FP32中间值），即可避免训练崩溃，而无需改动推理引擎、矩阵乘/注意力内核或学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL训练LLM常分离推理与训练系统，即便权重和张量一致，实现差异也会使“本应on-policy”的更新变成off-policy并导致训练不稳定。现有方案需对齐整个栈、改全局精度或修改算法，代价大。作者想问：只对齐执行栈中部分环节是否足以稳定BF16 RL？

Method: 在训练侧将RoPE、SwiGLU、残差RMSNorm三处的舍入位置与推理对齐——中间结果保留FP32直至产生BF16输出。在四个Qwen系列模型上验证，且不改推理引擎、矩阵乘与注意力内核、学习目标。

Result: 默认BF16基线出现训练崩溃；三区域全对齐可避免崩溃。仅RoPE路径对齐在实验运行中也足够，尽管仍存在数值差异。

Conclusion: 局部、针对性的数值对齐（尤其RoPE路径）即可稳定BF16 RL训练，无需全栈对齐或改动算法，提供低成本实用方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for large language models often uses separate systems for inference and training. Even with identical weights and BF16 tensors, implementation differences can make an intended on-policy update off-policy and destabilize training. Existing remedies align the entire inference and training stacks, change global precision, or modify the learning algorithm. We ask whether aligning selected parts of the execution stack can stabilize BF16 RL while other implementation differences remain. We align training-side rounding locations with inference in three regions---RoPE, SwiGLU, and residual RMSNorm---by retaining selected intermediates in FP32 until their BF16 outputs. Across four Qwen-family models, aligning all three regions prevents the collapse observed under the default BF16 baseline; RoPE-path alignment alone also suffices in the studied runs, despite remaining numerical discrepancies. The interventions keep the inference engine, matrix-multiplication and attention kernels, and learning objective unchanged.

</details>


### [397] [RobustRLlib: A Unified Library and Benchmark for Robust Reinforcement Learning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32971)
*Xi Chen,Fengqi Hu,Zeyu Shen,Jiaying Xu,Shangding Gu,Pan Xu,Anqi Liu,Laixi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RobustRLlib：一个算法中心的鲁棒强化学习库与基准，统一整合17个在线/离线/安全RL算法，并提供覆盖真实扰动与人形、VLA任务的大规模评测，给出273种扰动配置下的能力图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒RL对真实部署至关重要，但现有算法面临两大障碍：算法可复用性差，以及经验能力边界不清，阻碍其广泛应用。

Method: 提出RobustRLlib库与基准，将17个代表性鲁棒在线、离线与安全RL算法集成到统一接口；构建增强评测平台，覆盖多样真实世界shift及先进的人形机器人与视觉-语言-动作（VLA）任务；进行273种扰动配置的系统性评测。

Result: 给出当前鲁棒RL算法的最新能力图谱，揭示其在何处仍有效、在何处仍存在显著鲁棒性差距。

Conclusion: RobustRLlib作为开源、易用、可复现且可扩展的平台，便于将现有方法应用于新问题并支持新鲁棒RL算法的开发。

Abstract: Robust reinforcement learning (RL) is critical for reliable real-world deployment, where policies may fail under diverse shifts and disruptions. Existing robust RL algorithms offer a rich set of mechanisms and principles, yet two major barriers limit their broader potential: limited algorithm reusability and an unclear empirical capability boundary. We introduce RobustRLlib, an algorithm-centric library and benchmark that addresses both challenges. It integrates 17 representative robust online, offline, and safe RL algorithms under a unified interface for substantially improved reusability, together with an enhanced evaluation platform spanning diverse real-world shifts and advanced humanoid and vision-language-action tasks. Systematic evaluation over 273 perturbation configurations provides an up-to-date capability map of current robust RL algorithms, revealing where they remain effective and where substantial robustness gaps persist. As an open-source, user-friendly platform, RobustRLlib offers a reproducible and extensible codebase for applying existing methods to new problems and developing new robust RL algorithms.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [398] [ENCL: Enabling Efficient Neuromorphic Continual Learning with Local Learning Rule and Sub-Network Freezing Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31927)
*Rachmad Vidya Wicaksana Putra,Shuochen Zhou,Hassan Raza,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: ENCL 提出一种轻量级神经形态持续学习方法，用局部学习规则和子网络冻结替代经验回放与全局反向传播，在保持竞争性精度的同时大幅降低内存、时间与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经形态持续学习（NCL）方法依赖耗内存的数据/经验回放和昂贵的全局学习规则（基于反向传播的替代梯度），导致内存与计算开销大，难以实现高能效的在线持续学习。

Method: ENCL 采用时间与空间局部化的局部学习规则进行高效信用分配，避免全局反向传播；基于任务采用子网络冻结策略，无需网络结构扩张；并对每个任务使用少量训练轮次及调整后的学习率和时间步以提升效率。

Result: 在任务增量学习（TIL）场景下，于 Split MNIST、Split Fashion MNIST、Split CIFAR-10、Split CIFAR-100 上取得与最先进经验回放方法相当的精度，同时内存降低至多 88%、训练时间降低至多 51%、平均功耗降低至多 40%、能耗降低至多 65%。

Conclusion: ENCL 通过局部学习规则与子网络冻结，为神经形态持续学习提供了一种高能效、可扩展且无需回放的解决方案，在精度与资源效率之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) in neural networks is a promising learning approach for updating the knowledge of deployed systems based on new data encountered at run time, that have not been learned during the offline training phase. To enable their power/energy-efficient CL processing, neuromorphic continual learning (NCL) concept with sparse event-based operations have recently been introduced. However, state-of-the-art NCL methods typically employ the memory-hungry data/experience replay, coupled with costly global learning rule (i.e., backpropagation-based surrogate gradient). To address these limitations, we propose ENCL, a novel methodology that enables efficient NCL by employing a lightweight local learning rule and sub-network freezing strategy. ENCL leverages the temporally- and spatially-local learning rule to perform efficient credit assignment for training new tasks, alleviating the expensive cost of global learning rule. ENCL also employs a sub-network freezing strategy based on the given tasks to avoid the needs for network architecture expansion, hence further improving the efficiency and scalability of the NCL training process. To efficiently support this, we train each task with a small number of epochs as well as adjusted learning rate and timestep. Experiment results on task-incremental learning (TIL) scenario show that, our ENCL consistently achieves strong performance across different datasets as compared to state-of-the-art works. Specifically, ENCL achieves competitive accuracy comparable to that of the state-of-the-art data/experience replay, while reducing memory cost by up to 88%, NCL training time by up to 51%, average power consumption by up to 40%, and energy consumption by up to 65% across different benchmarking datasets (i.e., Split MNIST, Split Fashion MNIST, Split CIFAR-10, and Split CIFAR-100).

</details>


### [399] [RobustNCL: Robustifying Neuromorphic Continual Learning under Transient Faults with Lightweight Detect-and-Cure Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31954)
*Rachmad Vidya Wicaksana Putra,Shuochen Zhou,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出RobustNCL框架，通过轻量级"检测-修复"策略提升神经形态持续学习在外存瞬态故障下的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经形态持续学习（NCL）虽能效高，但硬件瞬态故障（如高能粒子撞击DRAM）会破坏其性能，而NCL在瞬态故障下的可靠性尚未被研究，缺乏容错技术。

Method: 提出RobustNCL框架：在从片外DRAM读取权重数据时监测故障，检测到异常签名后修复该权重。提供三种修复变体——置零、最小/最大值、以及基于原始/干净权重分布的最可能值；修复值存放于抗辐射加固存储器中。

Result: 在高故障率（如memory replay的5×10⁻⁴）下，最佳变体精度与无故障模型相差在-0.5%以内，相较无缓解基线显著提升，且在Split MNIST/Fashion-MNIST/CIFAR-10/CIFAR-100上跨多种NCL方法有效，延迟与能耗开销可忽略。

Conclusion: RobustNCL是使神经形态持续学习系统兼具可靠性与高能效的有前景方案。

Abstract: In neural networks, continual learning (CL) is a prominent approach for enabling deployed systems to adapt to dynamic operational environments by effectively learning from new unseen data at runtime. Recently, the neuromorphic continual learning (NCL) concept has emerged as an energy-efficient alternative to CL by leveraging sparse event-based operations. However, NCL performance can be undermined by transient faults in the hardware platforms, which may come from high-energy particle strikes at any time. Currently, NCL reliability under transient faults has not been studied, and hence the corresponding fault-tolerance techniques remain unexplored. To address this, we propose a novel RobustNCL framework to improve NCL reliability in the presence of transient faults in the DRAM-based off-chip memory of hardware platforms through a lightweight detect-and-cure strategy. Specifically, it first monitors the presence of faults during weight data access from off-chip memory, and then cures the accessed weights if anomalous signatures are detected. It provides three curing variants: replacement with zero, min/max value, and most-probable value from the original/clean weight distribution. To support this, the weight curing values are stored in the radiation-hardened memory. Experimental results show that, in the presence of high fault rates (e.g., 5x10^{-4} for memory replay), RobustNCL maintains high accuracy close to the accuracy of fault-free models (i.e., within -0.5% accuracy for the best RobustNCL variant) and significantly improves accuracy from the baseline without fault mitigation across different NCL methods and datasets (i.e., Split MNIST, Split Fashion-MNIST, Split CIFAR-10, and Split CIFAR-100), while incurring negligible latency and energy overheads. These results highlight that our RobustNCL framework is a promising approach for enabling reliable and energy-efficient NCL systems.

</details>


### [400] [A Resource-Efficient and Precision-Scalable FPGA Accelerator for Spiking Neural Networks Using Minifloat Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32052)
*Ali Mehrabi,Philip C. Jose*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出一种基于参数化minifloat算术的精度可扩展、资源高效的SNN硬件加速器架构，在同一RTL框架内支持E8M23/E5M10/E5M6/E4M3格式，在FPGA上验证了降精度可显著降低资源与功耗并保持竞争性精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN适合事件驱动的边缘智能，但传统浮点实现的算术复杂度、存储占用和功耗限制了其在资源受限硬件上的部署，需要一种可按需权衡精度、资源与功耗的硬件方案。

Method: 采用综合时可配置字长与尾数宽度的统一minifloat数据通路，实现递归LIF神经元与LI读出神经元；构建valid驱动的流水线minifloat乘加子系统，并通过时分复用让算术资源在突触累加、神经元状态更新、递归连接和输出处理间共享；支持E8M23、E5M10、E5M6、E4M3四种格式且无需改动神经元架构。

Result: FPGA实现表明，降低minifloat精度可大幅减少算术资源利用率和估计动态功耗，同时降低突触参数的理论存储需求；以递归事件驱动分类网络为验证案例，在显著降精度下仍保持有竞争力的推理精度。

Conclusion: 参数化minifloat计算可作为构建可扩展、低资源、高能效FPGA SNN加速器的有效基础。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) are well suited to event-driven edge intelligence, but the arithmetic complexity, memory footprint, and power consumption associated with conventional floating-point implementations can limit their deployment on resource-constrained hardware. This paper presents a precision-scalable and resource-efficient SNN hardware accelerator architecture based on parameterized minifloat arithmetic. The proposed architecture implements recurrent leaky integrate-and-fire (LIF) neurons and leaky-integrator (LI) readout neurons using a unified minifloat datapath whose word length and mantissa width can be configured at synthesis time. A valid-driven arithmetic subsystem employs pipelined minifloat multiplication and addition, while time-multiplexed processing enables arithmetic resources to be shared across synaptic accumulation, neuron-state updates, recurrent connections, and output processing. This organization reduces replicated arithmetic hardware and provides a scalable design approach for different SNN sizes and precision requirements. The architecture supports E8M23, E5M10, E5M6, and E4M3 formats within the same RTL framework, allowing precision, memory capacity, timing, and power to be traded without modifying the underlying neuron architecture. FPGA implementation results demonstrate that reducing minifloat precision substantially decreases arithmetic-resource utilization and estimated dynamic power, while also reducing the theoretical storage requirement of synaptic parameters. A recurrent event-based classification network is used as a hardware validation case, demonstrating that substantial precision reduction can be achieved while retaining competitive inference accuracy. The results establish parameterized minifloat computation as an effective basis for scalable, low-resource, and energy-efficient FPGA SNN accelerators.

</details>


### [401] [PAFS: Perturbation-Aware Firing Scaffolds for Robust Event-Based SNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32409)
*Ning Liu,Jun Sun,Hao Guo,Sen Shen,Xiangfei Yang,Jie Yang,Lan Wu,Shangke Lyu,Thomas Bräunl*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: PAFS是一种免更新骨干网络的训练后校准方法，利用晚期层LIF发放轨迹的低秩结构与类别残差对齐，来修正SNN在事件丢失、时间戳抖动和速度变化下的预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件驱动SNN的有状态计算使其预测对事件丢失、时间戳抖动与速度变化敏感，这些扰动改变证据累积过程；而仅看最终logits难以诊断错误，晚期层发放轨迹中却保留了可用于纠错的诊断结构。

Method: 先训练Adapter锚点获得类别概率残差；将标准化后的晚期层LIF轨迹与残差关联，通过SVD提取紧凑的残差对齐通道方向，构成固定低秩"脚手架"。推理时把同一骨干前向的发放轨迹投影到该低秩空间，结合锚点统计量预测logit残差修正，骨干全程冻结。

Result: 在3个事件数据集、2种脉冲Transformer架构构成的4组匹配设置中，受损平均与最差条件准确率均优于对应Adapter锚点，最大提升分别为3.06和3.65个百分点；DailyAction-DVS/Spikingformer设置下新增可训练参数约占冻结骨干的0.34%。

Conclusion: 晚期层发放轨迹的低秩残差结构可有效用于SNN鲁棒性校准，且以极小参数开销实现，无需重训骨干，为事件网络提供轻量部署友好的纠错方案。

Abstract: Event-based spiking neural networks (SNNs) encode information through temporal firing dynamics, but the same stateful computation makes their predictions sensitive to event loss, timestamp jitter, and speed variation. These perturbations alter evidence accumulation, yet late-layer firing trajectories can retain error-diagnostic structure that is absent from the final logits alone. We introduce Perturbation-Aware Firing Scaffolds (PAFS), a labeled post-training calibration method that exploits this structure without updating the pretrained backbone. PAFS associates standardized late-layer leaky integrate-and-fire trajectories with the class-probability residuals of an Adapter anchor, then applies singular value decomposition to extract compact residual-aligned channel directions. At inference, firing trajectories from the same backbone pass are projected onto this fixed low-rank scaffold and combined with anchor statistics to predict a residual logit correction. Across four matched dataset-backbone settings spanning three event datasets and two spiking Transformer architectures, PAFS improves corrupted-average and worst-condition accuracy over the corresponding Adapter anchors, with maximum gains of 3.06 and 3.65 percentage points. In the DailyAction-DVS/Spikingformer setting, the added trainable parameters amount to approximately 0.34% of the frozen backbone.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [402] [MICE to Meet You: From Re-ranking Only to End-to-End Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31932)
*Victor Morand,Yuxuan Zong,Mathias Vast,Josiane Mothe,Benjamin Piwowarski*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 MICE-P，首个基于 PLAID 索引 MICE 上下文化文档 token 表示的端到端检索流水线，在训练条件匹配下兼顾晚交互检索吞吐量与 cross-encoder 有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晚交互模型虽可预计算文档表示并高效索引，但 MaxSim 交互机制过于简单，效果不及 cross-encoder；MICE 虽结合 transformer 打分（cross-encoder 级有效性）与文档侧预计算，但缺乏存储这类表示的实用方案，无法用于真实检索。

Method: 用 PLAID 索引 MICE 的上下文化文档 token 表示，构建首个端到端检索流水线 MICE-P；并基于 Ettin 家族在匹配实验条件下训练 MICE、ColBERT 与 cross-encoder 的可比模型进行受控对比。

Result: MICE-P 达到与晚交互检索相当的吞吐量，同时保持 cross-encoder 的有效性，形成一种具有独立效率-效果权衡的新检索架构类别。

Conclusion: MICE-P 解决了 MICE 表示的实用索引与存储问题，验证了其作为新型检索架构的可行性与竞争力。

Abstract: Late-interaction models offer state-of-the-art efficiency-effectiveness trade-offs for neural retrieval by enabling document representations to be pre-computed and efficiently indexed. Yet, their relatively simple interaction mechanisms, typically MaxSim, can limit their effectiveness compared to cross-encoders. MICE, a recently introduced re-ranking architecture, aims to combine cross-encoder-like effectiveness through expressive transformer-based scoring with reduced inference-time computation through document-side pre-computation. However, no practical way to store these document representations was explored. We address this limitation here and introduce MICE-P, the first end-to-end retrieval pipeline with MICE, based on PLAID to index the contextualized document token representations. We conduct a controlled comparison of MICE, ColBERT and cross-encoders by training comparable models using the Ettin Family under matched experimental conditions. Our results show that MICE-P defines a new class of retrieval architecture with an efficiency-effectiveness trade-off of its own: it matches late-interaction retrieval throughput while retaining the effectiveness of cross-encoders.

</details>


### [403] [A Model-Agnostic Correction Framework for Short-Term Building Load Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31959)
*Shiqiao Zhou,Huanbo Lyu,Holger Schöner,Zipeng Wu,Jessica Deuschel,Freya Wentzel,IAG Wilson,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 WeL-RAC——一种与模型无关的"天气-负荷"检索增强修正框架，在推理时基于近期负荷状态与对齐预测时域的气象预报检索相似历史案例，用其观测未来轨迹修正基础预测器，无需重训；在31栋建筑、3数据集、8种骨干、4个时域（1–18h）的96组对比中90组有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期建筑负荷模式随天气与运行条件快速变化，数据驱动模型在罕见或训练中表征不足的运行工况下可靠性下降，即便历史记录中他处存在相似情境也难以被模型利用。

Method: 受检索增强时间序列预测启发，WeL-RAC 在预测时显式检索与当前条件相似的历史案例（依据近期负荷状态 + 与预测时域对齐的天气预报），聚合这些案例的真实未来负荷轨迹，用以修正初始预测；整个过程不重训、不修改基础预测器，具备模型无关性。

Result: 在3个数据集、31个建筑案例、8种预测骨干、1至18小时四个时域上评测，96组数据集级对比中90组优于对应基础预测器；成功与失败案例研究进一步体现可解释性，可追溯检索到的历史证据如何改善或恶化预测。

Conclusion: 显式历史检索是建筑负荷预测中有效且可解释的补充手段；但失败案例表明检索修正并非总是有益，其边界条件与可靠性仍需进一步考察。

Abstract: Short-term building load forecasting remains challenging because load patterns can change rapidly with weather and operating conditions. Data-driven forecasting models may become less reliable when the current operating regime is rare or poorly represented during training, even when similar situations exist elsewhere in the historical record. Recent advances in retrieval-augmented time series forecasting offer a promising way to adapt predictions to changing conditions by directly reusing relevant historical cases at inference time. Motivated by this idea, we propose WeL-RAC, a model-agnostic Weather-Load Retrieval-Augmented Correction framework that explicitly retrieves such evidence at prediction time. WeL-RAC retrieves historical cases that are similar to the current condition based on both recent load states and horizon-aligned weather forecasts. Their observed future load trajectories are then aggregated and used to correct the initial prediction, without retraining or modifying the base forecaster. We evaluate WeL-RAC on 31 building cases across three datasets, eight forecasting backbones, and four horizons from 1 to 18 hours. The framework improves the corresponding base forecasters in 90 of 96 dataset-level comparisons. Beyond accuracy, success and failure case studies further illustrate the framework's interpretability by tracing how retrieved historical evidence improves or degrades forecasts. These findings support explicit historical retrieval as an effective and interpretable complement to the building load forecasting.

</details>


### [404] [From Item Scores to Semantic Tokens: Test-Time Collaborative Adaptation for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32098)
*Xiongfeng Shan,Xing Tang,Hanchong Chen,Fuyuan Lyu,Lingjie Li,Jingyan Jiang,Duguang Liu,Xiuqiang He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TCBSA是一种免训练方法，将判别式推荐器的协同分数转化为共享语义前缀边上的势能，在beam剪枝前注入生成式推荐解码过程，提升目标路径存活率与推荐准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐器用语义ID逐token预测，beam search可能不可逆地剪掉有前景的物品路径，尤其当近期少量交互反映新兴兴趣而早期行为偏向既有偏好时。同时，独立训练的判别式推荐器能在同一历史下给出高分排序，这一互补信号在测试时可得，可用于适配冻结的生成器，但需在剪枝前利用、并作用于共享语义前缀。

Method: 将判别式分数边际转换为共享语义边上的势能，并用置信度门控调节其对累积路径分数的贡献，并在beam选择前生效；理论上给出全局beam竞争下目标前缀存活的充分条件，以及低判别置信度下的分数扰动界。

Result: 全目录实验中，在不同数据集及多种生成式/判别式推荐器组合上均稳定优于强基线；新兴兴趣与解码行为分析显示准确率与目标路径存活率提升，针对性干预实验支持共享前缀修正的贡献；相较单纯加宽原生beam，在准确率与推理延迟间取得更好平衡。

Conclusion: TCBSA通过测试时融合协同信息与语义前缀解码，免训练地缓解beam剪枝问题，兼具效果与效率优势。

Abstract: Generative recommenders using semantic identifiers predict items through successive token decisions, but beam search may irreversibly prune promising item paths. This limitation is particularly consequential when a few recent interactions signal an emerging interest while earlier behavior favors established preferences. Yet an independently trained discriminative recommender may rank the same items highly from the same interaction history. Such complementary scores are available at test time, offering a way to adapt a frozen generator without retraining either model. The challenge is to use these scores before pruning: combining completed recommendation lists cannot alter earlier beam decisions, while scores over complete items cannot directly inform choices among semantic prefixes shared by multiple items. We propose \textbf{T}est-Time \textbf{C}ollaborative \textbf{B}eam \textbf{S}earch \textbf{A}daptation (\textbf{TCBSA}), a training-free method that incorporates collaborative information into semantic decoding. TCBSA transforms discriminative score margins into potentials over shared semantic edges and uses a confidence gate to regulate their contributions to cumulative path scores before beam selection. We establish a sufficient condition for target prefix survival under global beam competition and bound score perturbations under low discriminative confidence. Full-catalog experiments show consistent improvements over strong baselines across datasets and different generative and discriminative recommenders. Analyses of emerging interests and decoding behavior demonstrate gains in recommendation accuracy and target path survival, while targeted interventions support the contribution of shared prefix corrections. TCBSA also offers a favorable balance between recommendation accuracy and inference latency compared with simply widening the native beam.

</details>


### [405] [PersonalQuery-Benchmark:Evaluating Retrieval Robustness to Personalized User Expression Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32164)
*Wenyu Liang,Hongzhi Yin,Tongtong Wu,Fan Liu,Kexin Dai,Weiqing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 PersonalQuery-Benchmark（PQB），通过模拟用户个性化表达与拼写错误构造同一信息需求的查询变体，系统评测检索模型对个性化表达差异的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化信息检索主要关注用户偏好与查询意图的差异，忽略不同用户表达同一检索需求时的个性化表达差异；且真实查询数据缺乏稳定用户身份、长期表达历史和意图对齐的对照，难以系统评估该差异对检索效果的影响。

Method: 基于用户历史文本建模个性化句法表达与个性化书写错误，分别生成句法变体查询与错写变体查询；通过大模型评估与独立人工评估验证基准质量。覆盖亚马逊三个商品领域，共6516条查询实例，并在五类检索模型（词法、学习型稀疏、稠密双编码器、晚期交互、混合架构）及两种基于LLM的排序模型上进行评测。

Result: 在信息需求固定的情况下，个性化表达差异仍会引起可测量的排序变化；不同模型对句法变化与拼写错误的敏感度差异明显；整体检索效果更强的模型不一定对表达变化更鲁棒。

Conclusion: PQB为系统研究个性化表达鲁棒性提供了新的基准与评测框架，并开源了代码与数据集。

Abstract: Existing personalized information retrieval methods mainly model differences in user needs through user preferences and query intent, while paying little attention to the personalized expression differences that arise when different users express the same retrieval need. Existing real query data typically lack stable user identities, long-term expression histories, and intent-matched counterparts, making it difficult to systematically evaluate the impact of personalized expression differences on retrieval outcomes. To this end, we propose PersonalQuery-Benchmark (PQB). PQB models personalized syntactic expression and personalized writing errors from users' historical texts, constructs personalized syntactic expression queries and personalized writing-error queries, and validates benchmark quality through LLM assessment and independent human evaluation. PQB covers three Amazon product domains with 6,516 query instances in total, and is evaluated on five retrieval models representing lexical, learned sparse, dense bi-encoder, late-interaction, and hybrid architectures, together with two LLM-based ranking models. Results show that even when the information need is held fixed, personalized expression differences still induce measurable ranking variation; different retrieval models exhibit clearly different sensitivities to syntactic variation and writing-error variation, while higher overall retrieval effectiveness does not necessarily correspond to stronger robustness against expression variation. PQB provides a new benchmark and evaluation framework for systematically studying personalized expression robustness. The associated code and dataset are available at the following address: https://github.com/cpxjazz123/PersonalQuery-Benchmark.

</details>


### [406] [SkillRep: Characterizing and Understanding the Representation Design Space of Agent Skill Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32336)
*Tianyi Sun,Pengfei Yao,Yanze Wang,Hongda Chen,Yunyun Han,Jun Sun,Chuanrui Hu,Yafeng Deng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SkillRep 首次将五种表示选择（编码器微调、查询/文档扩展、背景投影、词法通道）作为单一检索器模块联合评估，在技能检索上显著超越同规模最强检索器，并提出背景、未言明词汇、稀释三元量来量化 request-skill 差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 需从大技能库检索正确技能，但请求与技能写作目的与用词不同，存在 request-skill 差距。现有检索器各自只用一两种表示选择，其增益能否叠加未知；且无人度量差距本身，不清楚各选择缩小了哪部分差距。

Method: 构建由五个选择组成的表示设计空间（编码器微调、查询侧扩展、文档侧扩展、背景投影、词法通道），首次把它们作为 SkillRep 同一检索器的模块联合评测；使用 0.6B 编码器与 LLM 扩展。另定义三个量度量差距：背景（不区分技能的嵌入分量）、未言明词汇（请求未表达的 gold 技能内容）、稀释（技能编码为单向量时的信息损失）。

Result: SkillRep 在 SRA-Bench 上 MRR@10 较最强同规模检索器提升 7.02 分（官方技能池）、8.79 分（自建 111,247 技能的 UniPool），在 SkillsBench 上表现最佳。各选择的价值相互依赖：微调会反转四个免训练模块的排序，并使其从互补变为重叠。

Conclusion: 三个量化量揭示各模块缩小差距的哪一部分及机制：缩小同一部分的模块相互竞争，缩小不同部分的模块互补。这为技能检索的表示设计提供了可解释的指导。

Abstract: Retrieving the right skill from a large library is essential for LLM agents, yet requests and skills are written for different purposes and in different words. Existing retrievers narrow this request-skill gap with one or two representation choices each, so it is unknown whether their gains hold in combination. No prior work measures the gap itself, so it is also unclear which part of it each choice narrows. We define a representation design space by five choices -- encoder fine-tuning, query-side and document-side expansion, background projection and a lexical channel -- and evaluate them together for the first time, as modules of one retriever, SkillRep. With a 0.6B encoder and LLM expansion, SkillRep improves MRR@10 on SRA-Bench over the strongest same-size retriever by 7.02 points with the official skill pool and 8.79 with UniPool, a 111,247-skill pool we build from public registries, and is best on SkillsBench. The value of each choice depends on the others. Fine-tuning, for instance, reverses the ranking of the four training-free modules and turns them from complementary into overlapping. To understand why, we measure the gap with three quantities: background, the embedding component that does not separate skills; unsaid vocabulary, the gold skill's content a request leaves unsaid; and dilution, the information lost when a skill is encoded into a single vector. They show which part each module narrows and by what mechanism. This reveals that modules narrowing the same part compete for it, while those narrowing different parts complement each other.

</details>


### [407] [Hierarchical GTV Estimation: Bridging the Gap between Container-Level Ranking and Item-Level Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32420)
*Zerong Lan,Fan Zhang,Chuang Chen,Tianchun Huang,Teng Zhang,Xingxing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对直播、O2O等"容器式"电商场景中容器级排序与物品级交易的粒度错配问题，本文提出分层GTV预估框架HGE，通过混合集合感知编码器、代理标签学习和残差校正与集成融合模块，显著提升GTV预估精度并已全量上线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴电商平台（直播、O2O）以"容器"（直播间、门店）为单位进行排序和预估，但实际交易发生在容器内的物品层面。这种粒度错配带来高标签方差、严重标签稀疏和动态物品异构三大挑战，使GTV预估更加困难。

Method: HGE框架包含三部分：(1) 混合集合感知编码器（HSAE），建模物品的动态组成与特征交互；(2) 代理标签学习（PLL），将点击后GTV分解为购买概率、数量与单价以缓解标签方差；(3) 残差校正与集成融合模块（RCEF），结合物品级聚合与容器级推理以应对标签稀疏。

Result: 在大规模工业数据集上，HGE显著优于SOTA基线：离线XAUC提升+0.63%，在线A/B测试中每搜索收入（RPS）提升+1.42%。

Conclusion: HGE有效缓解了容器式电商中粒度错配带来的GTV预估难题，离线与在线指标均取得显著提升，并已全量部署到主流量中，具备实际工业价值。

Abstract: Emerging e-commerce platforms, such as live-streaming and Online-to-Offline (O2O) services, inherently operate under a "Container-based" display paradigm. In this setting, a critical granularity mismatch exists: the platform performs ranking and estimation at the container level (e.g., livestream rooms or stores), whereas the actual transactions occur at the item level within these containers. This structural discrepancy complicates Gross Transaction Value (GTV) estimation, introducing unique challenges including high label variance, severe label sparsity, and dynamic item heterogeneity. To address these challenges, we propose a novel framework named Hierarchical GTV Estimation (HGE). HGE employs a Hybrid Set-Aware Encoder (HSAE) to model the dynamic composition of items and feature interactions. To mitigate label variance, we introduce a Proxy-Label Learning (PLL) strategy that decomposes post-click GTV into purchase probability, quantity, and unit price. Furthermore, a Residual Correction and Ensemble Fusion (RCEF) module is designed to handle label sparsity by effectively combining item-level aggregation with container-level inference. Extensive experiments on large-scale industrial datasets demonstrate that HGE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a +0.63% lift in XAUC offline and a +1.42% increase in Revenue Per Search (RPS) in online A/B tests. HGE framework has been fully deployed into our main traffic.

</details>


### [408] [Agentic Memory Management Is the Next Frontier of Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32464)
*Qingyao Ai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该立场论文提出"智能体记忆管理"(AMM)是信息检索(IR)研究的自然延伸，给出AMM的系统级框架与记忆对象计算分类学（常量/函数/算子），比较其与IR的异同，并提出研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着越来越多AI智能体代替用户消费、保留并作用于信息，信息管理正转移到智能体内部，以"记忆管理"的形式发生；传统面向人类用户的IR定义已显著变化，IR社区需要正视并介入这一新问题。

Method: 立场/展望型论文：(1) 提出AMM的系统级框架，研究智能体记忆中信息的结构、分析、组织、存储、搜索、检索与使用；(2) 提出记忆对象的计算分类学——常量(constants)、函数(functions)、算子(operators)，各自具有不同的检索语义；(3) 将AMM与既有IR研究对比，辨析差异与联系；(4) 梳理研究议程。

Result: 研究议程包括：AMM基准数据集、记忆效用的候选原则、混合参数化与非参数化更新的优化过程等，为IR方法迁移到AMM提供方向。

Conclusion: AMM是IR研究的重要新前沿，IR社区可做出独特贡献；作者希望借此吸引IR内外更多研究者从IR视角思考AMM问题。

Abstract: Information retrieval has long been focusing on connecting people with information they need to complete their tasks. Recently, the meaning of this has changed dramatically as more and more AI agents now consume, retain, and act on information on a user's behalf. Consequently, information management now also takes place \emph{inside} the agent in the form of memory management. In this perspective paper, we argue that \emph{agentic memory management} (AMM), which studies the structure, analysis, organization, storage, searching, retrieval, and \emph{use} of information in an intelligent agent's memory, is a natural extension of IR research and an area in which the IR community can make distinctive contributions. Specifically, we first present a system-level framework for AMM and introduce a computational taxonomy of memory objects as \emph{constants}, \emph{functions}, and \emph{operators}, each with distinct retrieval semantics. Then, we compare AMM with established IR research and identify their difference and connections. Last but not least, we outline a research agenda covering benchmarks, candidate principles of memory utility, optimization procedures that mix parametric and non-parametric updates, and more. We hope this paper could attract more people, both inside and outside the IR communities, to think more about AMM problems and research from IR perspectives.

</details>


### [409] [UAE GovTrust Scope Filtering Gains and Routing Failures in Bilingual Public Service Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32608)
*Aleksandar Mihajlovic*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 UAE GovTrust 诊断集（英文-阿拉伯文公共服务检索），考察"多地点中仅一个控制服务范围"的问题。实验显示：经角色过滤的 RRF 对 72 条可回答查询中 69 条（95.8%）首位命中相关结果，未过滤仅 47 条；但路由在 168 条扰动中仅 2 条正确，说明正确范围元数据可提升排序，而脆弱范围推断会破坏证据链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共服务查询常提及多个地点，但通常仅一个地点决定所请求的服务范围；现有检索系统未区分这一"控制范围"与"提及地点"，导致排序与路由错误。

Method: 构建 UAE GovTrust 受控中英（英-阿）检索诊断集（22 条服务摘要、50 个场景、110 条查询变体）；对比 12 种方法：词法/稠密排序、排序融合、范围过滤、多语言交叉编码器、直接服务查询。

Result: 角色过滤 RRF 在 72 条可回答问题中 69 条（95.8%）首位正确，未过滤融合 47 条（65.3%），过滤稠密与直接查询各 68 条（94.4%）；纯英文证据下排除零分词法候选使过滤融合从 52 提升至 64；但固定路由器在 168 条审计后扰动中仅 2 条选对动作与范围。原始 1,320 条排序与动作可在新环境完全复现。

Conclusion: 正确的范围元数据可改善排序，但脆弱的范围推断会删除回答所需的证据；该研究仅评估检索与路由，不涉及生成答案或法律正确性，阿拉伯语与领域独立验证仍属必要。

Abstract: Public-service queries can mention several locations while only one controls the requested service. We study this distinction in UAE GovTrust, a controlled English-Arabic retrieval diagnostic comprising 22 authored service summaries and 50 scenarios with 110 query variants. Twelve methods separate lexical and dense ranking, rank fusion, scope filtering, a multilingual cross-encoder, and direct service lookup. On 72 matched answerable queries with bilingual evidence, role-filtered reciprocal rank fusion retrieves a relevant first result for 69 queries (95.8%), versus 47 (65.3%) for unfiltered fusion; filtered dense retrieval and direct lookup each achieve 68 (94.4%). With English-only evidence, excluding zero-score lexical candidates raises filtered fusion from 52 to 64 correct first hits. However, the unchanged router selects the correct action and scope for only 2 of 168 post-audit perturbations covering paraphrases, irrelevant facts, negation, and corrections. These perturbations are deliberately authored diagnostics, not held-out evaluation. Fresh-environment execution reproduces all 1,320 original ranking orders and actions. The results show that correct scope metadata can improve ranking while brittle scope inference can remove the evidence needed to answer. The study evaluates retrieval and routing, not generated answers or legal correctness; independent Arabic and domain validation remains necessary.

</details>


### [410] [From archive to resource: bringing legacy data back to life at scale with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32616)
*C. H. Vieira-Vieira,I. Schneider Chernov,A. Serra,M. Correa Marrero,P. L. Bhavani Mohan,G. Duchateau-Nguyen,T. Y. Doktorova,A. Kreuchwig*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 两家药企团队各自用LLM流水线从异构历史临床前研究报告文本中抽取并规范化实验情境元数据（如禁食、麻醉、仪器），附带原文证据与页码章节以便人工核验；400余份文档评估显示整体一致性高，抽取耗时从25分钟降至1.6分钟，并证明禁食状态与临床生化指标的种属/分析物特异性变化相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药企积累了大量传统临床前研究报告，其中禁食、麻醉、实验仪器等研究情境信息极少进入结构化数据，却与临床病理参数值相关，并限制了历史数据的复用与可比性。

Method: 由两个工业团队独立开发基于大语言模型（LLM）的抽取流水线，从异构报告文本中识别并规范化元数据字段，返回逐字证据及页码与章节引用以支持人工核验与迭代优化；在400余份文档上与专家标注的参考值进行对比评估；并以抽取出的禁食状态为例，分析其与临床生化参数的关系。

Result: 在广泛的元数据字段上取得较高一致性；对报告方式一致的条目表现最佳，对受语言表述或报告结构变异影响的描述符表现较弱。抽取时间从人工基线的25分钟缩短至1.6分钟。禁食状态与种属及分析物特异的临床生化参数变化相关，可用于改进历史对照的选择。

Conclusion: 该方法具备可扩展性、可追溯性，并能恢复现有行业标准中缺失的信息；主要局限是依赖原始报告质量以及定义和优化schema所需的初期专家投入。结果表明LLM可将异构、非结构化的历史报告转化为有证据支撑、可复用的结构化元数据，提升实验可比性，从而支持可靠的数据汇聚或有依据的虚拟对照选择。

Abstract: Pharmaceutical companies maintain vast archives of legacy preclinical study reports that contain study-context details, such as fasting, anesthesia, and laboratory instrumentation, that are rarely captured in structured data formats, yet they can correlate with clinical pathology parameters values and limit the reuse of historical data. This work demonstrates practical, scalable solutions to recover such descriptors from heterogeneous report texts using large language model (LLM)-based extraction pipelines developed independently by two industry teams. The pipelines identify and normalize metadata fields and return verbatim evidence with page and section reference, hasting human verification and iterative refinement. Evaluation of over 400 documents against expert-curated reference values showed high agreement across a broad set of metadata fields. Performance was strongest for consistently reported items and lower for descriptors subject to linguistic or report structure variability. Extraction time has shrunk from 25 minutes in the manual benchmark to 1.6 minutes. As a concrete biological demonstration, we show that fasting status extracted from report text was associated with species- and analyte-specific shifts in clinical chemistry parameters, illustrating how recovered metadata can improve selection of historical controls. Key strengths of the approach are scalability, traceability, and the ability to recover information missing from current industry standards. Main limitations are dependence on source-report quality and initial expert effort to define and refine schemas. These results establish how LLMs can convert heterogeneous, unstructured legacy reports into evidence-grounded, reusable and structured metadata, leading to better experimental comparability, and thus enabling reliable data pooling or informed virtual control selection.

</details>


### [411] [Knowledge Distillation across Item and Code Spaces for Parallel Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32943)
*Yixuan Xu,Hailin Liu,Linxiang Peng,Can Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文为并行语义ID生成式推荐提出两种互补的知识蒸馏目标：物品分布匹配（IDM）与码边缘匹配（CMM），将序列教师模型的软分布知识迁移到码空间学生模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义ID推荐器仅用观测下一物品的硬标签训练，只指明目标，却无法提供以同一用户历史为条件的候选物品间的分布信息。序列推荐器可提供这类物品级预测知识，但教师处于物品空间、学生处于码空间，知识迁移困难。

Method: 针对无自回归前缀条件的并行语义ID推荐器，作者提出两种蒸馏目标。IDM：从学生并行码预测重构目录级物品分布，使其与教师物品分布对齐。CMM：利用固定物品到码的映射，把教师物品概率投影到各码位置，得到码边缘分布，并与学生对应预测头匹配。两者均保留学生并行预测结构。

Result: 在基准数据集上，两方法均稳定优于现有最优方法。平均而言，IDM使Recall@10提升15.39%，CMM使NDCG@10提升8.28%。

Conclusion: 在重构物品空间或码边缘空间进行蒸馏，都能显著强化并行生成式推荐的硬标签监督，验证了软分布知识迁移的有效性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation with semantic IDs represents each item as a sequence of discrete codes derived from its semantic representation and predicts these codes to generate recommendations. Existing semantic-ID recommenders are typically trained with hard labels for the observed next item. Although such supervision identifies the target, it provides no explicit distributional information about alternative items conditioned on the same user history. Sequential recommenders can supply this item-level predictive knowledge, but transferring it to semantic-ID generation is challenging because the teacher and student operate in item and code spaces, respectively. We propose two complementary knowledge-distillation objectives for parallel semantic-ID recommenders, which predict all code positions simultaneously without autoregressive prefix conditioning. Item Distribution Matching (IDM) reconstructs a catalogue-level item distribution from the student's parallel code predictions and aligns it with the teacher's item distribution. Code Marginal Matching (CMM) projects the teacher's item probabilities onto each code position using fixed item-to-code assignments, then matches the resulting code marginals to the corresponding student heads. Both objectives transfer the teacher's soft distributional knowledge while preserving the student's parallel prediction structure. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that both methods consistently outperform existing state-of-the-art approaches. On average, IDM improves Recall@10 by 15.39%, while CMM improves NDCG@10 by 8.28%. These results demonstrate that distillation in either reconstructed item space or code-marginal space can substantially strengthen hard-label supervision for parallel generative recommendation.

</details>


### [412] [Separating Thinking from Spelling for Efficient Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32980)
*Danil Gusak,Anna Volodkevich,Ivan Oseledets,Evgeny Frolov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Deco 将生成式推荐的"读历史"与"写标识"解耦：骨干网络用融合 token 压缩历史为一个概念，轻量 KV-cached 解码器在该概念与已生成前缀条件下完成全部 trie 约束 beam search，在保持 SID/因子化不变的前提下把延迟与吞吐显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归生成式推荐把 SID 长度 K 的代价付两遍：历史 T 个物品以 TK 个码 token 进入骨干（读），且每个 beam 步骤都要过骨干（写），B 个候选需 O(BK) 骨干解码 token/用户。第二项开销常占主导，并随骨干规模、SID 深度与候选预算增长，限制了更大模型与更大候选池的可行性。

Method: 将历史读取与标识生成分离：骨干每物品一个融合 token，一次把历史浓缩为紧凑的 next-item concept；随后由轻量、带 KV cache 的浅解码器在该 concept 与已生成前缀条件下执行完整 beam search。SID 编码、trie 约束与自回归分解均保持不变；解码器仅用交叉熵即可逼近全骨干精度，蒸馏可进一步提升。

Result: 在电商、电影、音乐流媒体共 7 个数据集上，Deco 精度与标准生成式推荐及融合输入的完整骨干教师相当或更优；在生产风格服务管线、50 ms p95 延迟预算下吞吐为二者 3.2–8.6×。Deco 生成 1000 个候选快于教师生成 100 个；1.5B 参数 LLM 骨干时吞吐追平 69M 教师且精度更高。

Conclusion: 把 beam search 从重骨干卸载到轻解码器是提升生成式推荐服务效率的有效路径，使更大候选池与更大骨干在延迟约束下变得可行；且不改变 SID 与 trie 语义，具备直接落地与与既有系统兼容的优势。

Abstract: Generative recommenders retrieve items by writing their semantic IDs (SIDs) code by code under trie-constrained beam search, an approach now deployed on large web platforms. Standard autoregressive interfaces pay for the SID length K twice: a history of T items enters the backbone as TK code tokens, and every beam-search step runs through the backbone, so returning B candidates costs O(BK) backbone decode tokens per user. The second cost often dominates, and it grows with the backbone, the SID depth and the candidate budget.
  We introduce Deco (decoding from concepts), which separates reading the history from writing the identifier. The backbone reads each history item as one fused token and condenses the history once into a compact next-item concept; a light KV-cached decoder conditioned on the concept and the generated prefix runs the entire beam search, while the identifiers, the trie and the autoregressive factorization stay unchanged. The decoder approaches full-backbone accuracy with cross-entropy alone, and distillation improves it further. On seven datasets from e-commerce, movies and music streaming, Deco achieves comparable or better accuracy than both a standard generative recommender and the full-backbone teacher with fused inputs, while delivering 3.2--8.6$\times$ their throughput under a 50 ms p95 latency budget in a production-style serving pipeline. Delegating beam search makes larger candidate pools and models practical: Deco generates 1000 candidates faster than its teacher generates 100, and with a 1.5B-parameter LLM backbone, it matches the 69M-parameter teacher's throughput while achieving higher accuracy.

</details>
